Delayed next-state residuals update two external paths: cumulative fixed-bank losses select the bounded action correction, while route-specific residual matrices refine next-state prediction. Neither path uses labels, teacher outputs, rewards, success signals or the true shift value. Joint optimization improves action next-state MSE by 17.10\% while also improving multimodal prediction, semantic accuracy and image-text retrieval. On held-out shear and attenuation streams, the combined external state improves aggregate prediction by 5.48\% and 30.01\%; its fixed-bank action path improves tracking by 24.20\% and 3.94\%, respectively. In a six-arm study comprising 450 new Meta-World trajectories (75 per arm), SpikeWorld raises frozen-policy reward by 7.90 (95\% CI [2.48, 14.06]); the 13.33-point success difference is descriptive (CI [0, 40]). For identical sensory inputs, model parameters and inherited semantic outputs remain bitwise unchanged. A 16-byte RLS estimator obtains the highest non-oracle reward on linear attenuation, showing that the contribution is not superior linear identification, but its integration with a frozen multimodal spiking checkpoint. Reference code is publicly available at https://github.com/Oooorca/SpikeWorld.

</details>


### [35] [Tokenizer Generator Coupling in Medical Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07713)
*Liam Chalcroft*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究质疑潜在医学图像生成器中将分词器视为固定预处理的传统做法。通过ChestMNIST 64x64受控实验，发现分词器、生成器和采样器选择相互影响显著，重建PSNR不可靠作为选择标准。提出了新指标——邻居条件预测增益，能更好区分量化器家族的下游生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在医学图像生成器通常将分词器作为固定预处理，但这种分离可能无效。研究旨在验证这种分离是否合理，探索分词器、生成器和采样器之间的交互关系。

Method: 1. ChestMNIST 64x64受控实验，交叉离散分词器、生成器家族和采样器设置
2. 使用连续潜在参考单元格
3. 在三个随机种子下重新训练词汇量-1024交互块
4. 引入新指标：邻居条件预测增益
5. 在保留验证集上重新调整D3PM和SE-D3PM
6. 使用FID-192作为内部排序指标，与FID-2048和分类器二样本检验验证

Result: 1. 排序结果依赖分词器、生成器和采样器的联合选择
2. 最佳量化器随生成器变化
3. 基于验证的采样器选择改变表观生成器排序
4. 交互作用在重新训练中保留（9个成对量化器比较中有6个超过三个种子标准差）
5. 重建PSNR单独不可靠
6. 新指标（邻居条件预测增益）在区分量化器家族方面达到rank-AUC 1.00
7. 重新调整D3PM和SE-D3PM使FID-192从0.44/0.41提升至0.09/0.10，且NFE更低
8. FID-192与FID-2048（Spearman 0.80）和分类器二样本检验（0.78）排序一致

Conclusion: 通过率失真-可建模性框架解释结果，其中可建模性条件于生成器、采样器和推理预算。所有结论限于低分辨率医学风格图像（64x64）、无条件生成、基于非临床FID指标评估的设置。

Abstract: Latent medical image generators usually treat the tokenizer as fixed preprocessing. We test whether this separation is valid in a controlled ChestMNIST study at 64x64, crossing discrete tokenizers, generator families, and sampler settings under a shared latent grid, with continuous-latent reference cells. In this controlled setting, rankings depend jointly on the tokenizer, generator, and sampler: the best quantizer changes with the generator, and validation-based sampler selection changes the apparent generator ranking. We retrain the vocabulary-1024 interaction block at three seeds and the interaction survives (6 of 9 pairwise quantizer comparisons exceed three seed standard deviations), and we scope the wider single-seed grid accordingly. Reconstruction PSNR alone is not a reliable selection criterion; we instead introduce a generator-free statistic, neighbour-conditional predictive gain, that separates the quantizer families by downstream generation quality (rank-AUC 1.00) where reconstruction PSNR and marginal token entropy do not. On LFQ-1024, retuning D3PM and SE-D3PM (selected on a held-out validation split) moves them from default FID-192 0.44/0.41 to 0.09/0.10 at lower NFE, replicated across seeds; the continuous references were not given an equivalent sampler sweep. We report FID-192 as an internal ranking metric; it ranks consistently with standard FID-2048 (Spearman 0.80) and with a label-free classifier two-sample test (0.78). We interpret these results through a rate-distortion-modelability framing, where modelability is conditional on the generator, sampler, and inference budget. All experiments are at 64x64 on low-resolution medical-style images, unconditional, and evaluated with non-clinical FID-based metrics, and we scope every claim to that setting. Code: https://github.com/liamchalcroft/medtokenizers and https://github.com/liamchalcroft/medlatents.

</details>


### [36] [Vision Meets WiFi: Physics-Grounded Estimation of Volumetric Mechanical Properties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07726)
*Ali Bahri,Hongliang Li,Soufiane Lamghari,Jie Chuai,Zhitang Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ViWi框架通过引入物体中心的材料槽和WiFi RF描述符，解决了纯视觉力学属性估计的歧义性，实现了更准确、连贯的体素级属性估计，在多项基准测试中显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅从视觉估计体素级力学属性存在内在歧义，因为视觉相似的物体可能具有截然不同的材料组成和物理行为；现有方法独立预测各体素属性，忽略了真实物体的分段恒定材料结构，导致噪声或不一致估计，且缺乏解决视觉歧义的明确机制。

Method: 提出ViWi框架，采用物体中心的材料槽机制聚合共享材料身份的体素证据，并融合通过WiFi电磁仿真生成的紧凑RF描述符，将全局组成线索与视觉体素级空间定位相结合。

Result: 在体素级力学属性与质量估计基准上，ViWi在6个体素级指标中的4项上优于先前最先进水平，其纯视觉变体提升了所有质量估计指标，验证了融合RF证据与物体中心材料结构的有效性。

Conclusion: 结合物体中心的材料结构与互补的RF证据，能够实现比仅依靠视觉外观更准确、物理上更连贯的体素属性估计，解决了视觉相似但材料不同的固有歧义问题。

Abstract: Estimating volumetric mechanical properties, including Young's modulus, Poisson's ratio, and density at each voxel, is intrinsically ambiguous from vision alone, as visually similar objects may have substantially different material compositions and physical behavior. Existing approaches predict these properties independently across voxels, overlooking the piecewise-constant material structure of real objects and producing noisy or inconsistent estimates for voxels that share the same material, while lacking an explicit mechanism to resolve visual ambiguity. We introduce ViWi (Vision Meets WiFi), an object-centric framework for volumetric mechanical-property estimation. ViWi represents each object using a compact set of material slots that aggregate evidence from voxels with a shared material identity and produce coherent slot-level property predictions. To complement visual appearance, ViWi incorporates a compact RF descriptor generated through WiFi-band electromagnetic simulation using permittivity and conductivity. The RF descriptor conditions the material slots with global composition cues that may be unavailable from images, while visual features preserve voxel-level spatial localization. Across volumetric mechanical-property and mass-estimation benchmarks, ViWi improves over the prior state of the art on four of six per-voxel metrics, while its vision-only variant improves all mass-estimation metrics. These results demonstrate that combining object-centric material structure with complementary RF evidence enables more accurate and physically coherent volumetric property estimation beyond what is possible from visual appearance alone.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超复数通过引入额外虚部扩展复数概念，增加维度并提供有益代数几何性质，但如何有效利用这些性质于机器学习仍需探索。

Abstract: Hypercomplex numbers extend the concept of complex numbers by introducing additional imaginary components. Besides increasing dimensionality, operations on the imaginary parts provide algebraic and geometrical properties that can be beneficial for solving machine learning problems. In this paper, we show how to create hypercomplex-valued neural network layers where the real part corresponds to the output of a traditional real-valued layer. The additional imaginary parts of these hypercomplex-valued layers produce what we call ``ghost features,'' which contain enhanced information that is not present in the output of the real-valued layer. Moreover, ghost features can be effectively integrated into a trained neural network through a process we refer to as ``spooky transfer learning.'' This approach allows us to harness the richness of ghost features, leading to more efficient neural networks. The source code and Jupyter Notebook are available at https://github.com/mevalle/v-nets/.

</details>


### [38] [BRUCE: Benchmarking Robustness Under Corruption Escalation for Scientific Vision-Language Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07742)
*Saim Rehman,Muhammad Shafique*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出BRUCE框架，用于评估视觉语言模型(VLMs)在图像质量下降时的鲁棒性，通过两种新型指标(RCI和T-RCI)量化多模态推理性能随视觉破坏程度增加的退化速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型(VLMs)在实际应用中经常因低质量或质量变化的输入图像而面临鲁棒性问题。现有评估框架主要关注干净任务的准确率，很少分析推理稳定性在不同鲁棒性维度上的退化情况。

Method: 提出BRUCE(Benchmarking Robustness Under Corruption Escalation)框架，对输入图像施加扰动和失真(如模糊或低对比度)，并采用两种新型指标——鲁棒性破坏指数(RCI)和遍历RCI(T-RCI)——来量化随着视觉破坏严重程度的递增，VLMs的多模态推理性能如何快速恶化。

Result: 在化学和数学推理任务的多個数据集上评估BRUCE，并从四个高层推理域(OCR依赖推理、空间推理、符号推理和语义失败)分析破坏引起的预测失败，每个域包含细粒度的特定破坏子类型，从而实现可解释的失败分析。

Conclusion: 通过BRUCE框架和两种新型指标，可以量化VLMs在视觉破坏严重程度递增时多模态推理性能的退化速度，并从多个推理域提供可解释的失败分析，有助于理解VLMs在真实世界低质量图像输入下的鲁棒性问题。

Abstract: Visual-language models (VLMs) frequently struggle with robustness issues in real-world situations due to low- or varying-quality input images. In this paper, we aim at analyzing VLMs' robustness by applying perturbations and distortions to the input images, such as blur or low contrast. Toward this goal, we propose BRUCE (Benchmarking Robustness Under Corruption Escalation, a multimodal reasoning fragility framework for scientific vision-language reasoning.
  State-of-the-art evaluation frameworks/studies primarily focus on clean-task accuracy and rarely analyze how reasoning stability degrades across robustness dimensions. Besides varying over a wide-range of input perturbations, BRUCE employs two novel metrics -- Robustness Corruption Index (RCI) and Traversal-RCI (T-RCI) -- to quantify how rapidly multimodal reasoning performance deteriorates in VLMs as visual corruption severity increases under progressive perturbation scaling. We evaluate BRUCE across chemistry and mathematical reasoning tasks for multiple datasets, while analyzing corruption-induced prediction failures in terms of four high-level reasoning domains: OCR-dependent reasoning, spatial reasoning, symbolic reasoning, and semantic failures, with each containing fine-grained corruption specific failure subtypes, thereby enabling an interpretable failure analysis.

</details>


### [39] [LoRSA: Toward Generalizable Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning for Biomedical Downstream Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07749)
*Saed Moradi,Benyamin Ghojogh,M. Hadi Sepanj,Yimin Yang,Ashirbani Saha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对参数高效微调中单一低秩更新限制模型同时表示全局共享任务和局部残差方向的问题，论文提出LoRSA框架，联合学习稠密低秩分量和动态结构化稀疏低秩分量，显著提升医学视觉模型在未见领域上的外部泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数高效微调使视觉基础模型能够在有限计算资源下适应生物医学任务，但单一低秩更新将所有任务特定变化约束在一个狭窄的参数子空间内。这种限制可能阻碍模型同时表示全局共享任务结构和实现泛化到未见影像领域所需的局部残差方向。

Method: 提出LoRSA（全局-残差适配框架），联合学习两个低秩分量：(1)稠密低秩分量捕获全局协调的任务适配；(2)动态结构化稀疏低秩分量提供互补的残差校正，其支撑集在训练过程中动态演化。通过表征容量、逼近性质、秩结构和奇异子空间互补性分析该分解的数学特性。

Result: 在四类乳腺密度分类任务上评估（DINOv3-Base模型，源域VinDr-Mammo，未见目标域MammosighTR和RSNA）：内部验证集上保持竞争力；在两个目标数据集上取得最佳外部宏F1分数，在MammosighTR上比最强竞争方法提高2.15个百分点，在RSNA上提高3.09个百分点。权重矩阵分析显示，每个适配分量约92%的能量位于另一个分量的双边奇异子空间之外，表明两个分量学习大体互补的更新方向。

Conclusion: 将适配能力组织为独立的全局和残差路径，能够提升参数高效适配生物医学视觉模型的外部领域泛化性能。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning enables the adaptation of vision foundation models to biomedical tasks under limited computational resources, but a single low-rank update can constrain all task-specific changes to one narrow parameter subspace. This restriction may prevent the model from simultaneously representing globally shared task structure and localized residual directions required for generalization to unseen imaging domains. We introduce LoRSA, a global--residual adaptation framework that jointly learns a dense low-rank component and a dynamically structured-sparse low-rank component. The dense component captures globally coordinated task adaptation, while the structured component provides complementary residual corrections whose support evolves during training. We characterize the representational capacity, approximation properties, rank structure, and singular-subspace complementarity of this decomposition. We evaluate LoRSA for four-class breast-density classification using DINOv3-Base, with VinDr-Mammo as the source domain and MammosighTR and RSNA as unseen external domains. LoRSA remains competitive on the internal validation set and achieves the best external macro-F1 on both target datasets, improving upon the strongest competing method by 2.15 percentage points on MammosighTR and 3.09 percentage points on RSNA. Weight-matrix analysis further shows that approximately $92\%$ of the energy of each adaptation component lies outside the bilateral singular subspace of the other, indicating that the two components learn largely complementary update directions. These results suggest that organizing adaptation capacity into distinct global and residual paths can improve the external-domain generalization of parameter-efficiently adapted biomedical vision models.

</details>

*Cong Chen,Jean-Philippe Monteuuis,Jonathan Petit*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多任务视觉感知系统的高效对抗攻击检测方法，通过检测不同视觉任务（如目标检测和实例分割）输出之间的不一致性来识别对抗扰动，在BDD100k数据集上对PGD攻击实现了99.9%的ROC-AUC检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络在视觉感知系统中的广泛应用引发了对抗攻击脆弱性的担忧，特别是在自动驾驶等关键领域。现有防御方法往往成本效率低，难以在资源受限的应用中部署。

Method: 提出一种利用复杂视觉系统内多任务感知的对抗攻击检测方案，通过以下三个关键步骤实现：1）开发一致性评分指标来测量不同视觉任务之间的输出不一致性；2）设计方法选择最佳模型对以有效检测不一致性；3）在BDD100k验证数据集上评估对PGD攻击的防御效果。

Result: 实验结果显示，在考虑的对抗者模型内，所提出的防御方法实现了99.9%的ROC-AUC检测性能，表明该方法能够高效有效地检测对抗攻击。

Conclusion: 本文提出的多任务感知一致性检测方法为对抗攻击防御提供了高效可行的解决方案，通过利用不同视觉任务输出的不一致性，能够在保持计算效率的同时实现高检测精度，为资源受限应用中的对抗防御提供了新思路。

Abstract: Deep Neural Networks (DNNs) have found successful deployment in numerous vision perception systems. However, their susceptibility to adversarial attacks has prompted concerns regarding their practical applications, specifically in the context of autonomous driving. Existing defenses often suffer from cost inefficiency, rendering their deployment impractical for resource-constrained applications. In this work, we propose an efficient and effective adversarial attack detection scheme leveraging the multi-task perception within a complex vision system. Adversarial perturbations are detected by the inconsistencies between the inference outputs of multiple vision tasks, e.g., object detection and instance segmentation. To this end, we developed a consistency score metric to measure the inconsistency between vision tasks. Next, we designed an approach to select the best model pairs for detecting inconsistencies effectively. Finally, we evaluated our defense against PGD attacks across multiple vision models on the BDD100k validation dataset. The experimental results demonstrated that our defense achieved a ROC-AUC performance of 99.9% detection within the considered attacker model.

</details>


### [41] [Rethinking 3D Segmentation from Individual LiDAR Scans: Incidence-Aware Sampling on the SIP Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07757)
*Seongyong Kim,Jingdao Chen,Yong Kwon Cho*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对建筑工地LiDAR扫描中由于表面覆盖有限、密度变化和平面与非平面元素不平衡导致的3D理解问题，提出了一种"感知入射角的采样策略"。该方法将点云映射到几何归一化流形空间进行体素选择，同时保留原始欧几里得坐标。在SIP基准上使用Point Transformer和PointNeXt的测试表明，该策略显著提升了分辨率平均分割性能，尤其改善了非平面元素和梯子的分割效果，并降低了对采样分辨率的敏感度。研究结论认为，感知采集的采样应被视为独立扫描3D分割的主动组件，而非通用预处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑工地3D场景理解日益重要，但现有方法大多基于精心整理的dataset，无法完全反映真实现场传感条件。实际工作流中，单个LiDAR扫描提供快速局部更新而非完整场景表示，导致：1) 表面覆盖有限；2) 采集驱动的密度变化；3) 主导平面表面与稀疏施工元素之间严重不平衡。此外，大点云必须降采样，采样分辨率和点分配直接影响几何细节与空间上下文之间的平衡。

Method: 研究提出了"感知入射角的采样策略"（incidence-aware sampling strategy），针对单个LiDAR扫描进行优化。该方法的核心是将点映射到几何归一化流形空间（geometry-normalized manifold space）进行基于体素的选择，同时保留原始欧几里得坐标以供下游学习使用。该方法仅需点坐标和法向量，无需修改骨干网络结构。

Result: 在Site in Pieces (SIP)基准上使用Point Transformer和PointNeXt进行实验，结果显示：1) 提升了分辨率平均分割性能；2) 特别改善了非平面元素和梯子的分割效果；3) 降低了对采样分辨率的敏感度。

Conclusion: 感知采集的采样能够提供更加稳定的几何表示，应被视为独立扫描3D分割的主动组件，而非通用预处理步骤。这一发现强调了在单个LiDAR扫描处理中考虑采集特性的必要性。

Abstract: 3D scene understanding is increasingly important in construction, yet most methods are developed on curated datasets that do not fully reflect real site sensing conditions. In many workflows, individual LiDAR scans provide rapid local updates rather than complete scene representations, producing limited surface coverage, acquisition-driven density variation, and severe imbalance between dominant planar surfaces and sparse construction elements. Because large point clouds must be downsampled, sampling resolution and point allocation directly affect the balance between geometric detail and spatial context. This study evaluates these effects under a fixed per-fragment point budget and introduces an incidence-aware sampling strategy for individual LiDAR scans. The method maps points to a geometry-normalized manifold space for voxel-based selection while preserving original Euclidean coordinates for downstream learning. It requires only point coordinates and normals and no backbone modification. Using the Site in Pieces (SIP) benchmark, experiments with Point Transformer and PointNeXt show improved resolution-averaged segmentation performance, especially for non-planar elements and ladders, while reducing sensitivity to sampling resolution. The results show that acquisition-aware sampling can provide a more stable geometric representation and should be treated as an active component of individual-scan 3D segmentation rather than generic preprocessing.

</details>


### [42] [XClipGS: Exact Half-Space Clipping for Medical Volume Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07760)
*Zhongpai Gao,Benjamin Planche,Meng Zheng,Anwesa Choudhuri,Chaoyi Zhou,Terrence Chen,Ziyan Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: XClipGS 提出了一种精确的裁剪方法，通过解析式半空间约束的高斯积分算子实现实时医学影像交互式渲染，在保留隐藏内部结构监督的同时，显著提升了裁剪平面附近解剖结构的渲染保真度和性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高斯溅射代理支持体数据交互式渲染，但裁剪平面会暴露外部视角训练未约束的内部解剖结构，且传统溅射只能整颗保留或丢弃与平面相交的基元，导致裁剪边界伪影。

Method: 在EWA溅射的局部仿射模型下，将半空间约束高斯的射线积分精确分解为2D footprint与条件高斯CDF乘积，构造闭式逐像素裁剪算子，无需学习参数且对基元和平面可微。使用多距离参考视图配合变化的裁剪平面轴和偏移量，通过同一算子监督隐藏内部。提出配对裁剪/未裁剪切面协议，采用差分参考切面误差(CDE)和漏出指标(Leak)取代全局图像度量。

Result: 在8个CT/MRI体上，XClipGS PSNR达33.56 dB（对比ClipGS的32.34 dB），渲染650+ FPS（对比278 FPS）。在体素轴切面视图中，平均带SSIM从0.809提升至0.860，漏出减少约40倍。在任意法向量平面上无需重训即取得四项指标最佳均值；在固定内部上匹配RaRa的面保真度且漏出减少约16倍。

Conclusion: XClipGS 通过解析式可微裁剪算子，在无需重训的情况下实现了任意方向裁剪平面下的高质量医学影像渲染，兼顾了高保真度与实时性能，解决了传统溅射裁剪伪影问题。

Abstract: Gaussian-splatting proxies enable interactive rendering of volumetric medical scans, but a clipping plane exposes anatomy not constrained by external-view training and intersects primitives that conventional splatting can only keep or drop whole. We present XClipGS (eXact Clipping), which treats these as two separate problems: the render-time clip operator and supervision of the hidden interior. Under the local affine model used by EWA splatting, the ray integral of a half-space-restricted Gaussian factorizes exactly into its ordinary 2D footprint and a conditional Gaussian CDF whose argument is affine in pixel coordinates. The resulting closed-form per-pixel operator introduces no learned clipping parameters or auxiliary network and remains differentiable with respect to the primitive and plane. We use multi-distance reference views with varied clipping-plane axes and offsets to supervise the interior through the same operator. We also introduce a paired clipped/unclipped cut-face protocol with difference-referenced cut error (CDE) and culled-side leakage (Leak), because global image metrics dilute errors near the plane. On eight CT and MRI volumes with plane offsets not used for training, XClipGS attains the highest PSNR on every volume (33.56 versus 32.34 dB for ClipGS) while rendering at over 650 FPS, far above real time, versus 278 FPS. On voxel-axis cut-face views, it raises average band SSIM from 0.809 to 0.860 and leaks roughly 40 times less. Without retraining, it also achieves the best average across all four metrics on arbitrary-normal planes; on a fixed interior, it matches RaRa's face fidelity with about 16 times less leakage. Project page: https://gaozhongpai.github.io/XClipGS/

</details>


### [43] [DINO-3DRA: Leveraging 2D Foundation Model Semantics for 3D Cerebral Aneurysm Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07767)
*Jiayang Lu,Fengming Lin,Alejandro F. Frangi,Ali Sarrami-Foroushani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DINO-3DRA双路径框架通过将冻结DINOv3特征注入3D U-Net，实现了高效跨维度语义迁移，在3DRA动脉瘤分割任务中以极少参数达到SOTA性能，并具备跨协议鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D旋转血管造影(3DRA)中动脉瘤分割受限于：极端类别不平衡、动脉瘤与血管形态相似、缺乏大规模3D预训练。2D视觉基础模型虽从17亿图像编码了密集结构先验，但朴素逐片迁移会破坏解剖连续性并导致优化不稳定。

Method: 提出DINO-3DRA双路径框架：将冻结的DINOv3特征通过Room-Lite空间混合与校准残差融合注入3D U-Net骨干网络，实现跨维度语义迁移。

Result: 在多元中心3DRA数据上，DINO-3DRA取得SOTA动脉瘤分割性能（Dice:0.758；HD95:2.75 mm；较nnU-Net提升13%），仅含5.72M可训练参数。消融实验证实增益源于结构化跨维度迁移而非损失设计；桥接基础特征改善了动脉瘤与母血管间的解剖连续性。未微调CADA与SHINY-ICARUS时，DINO-3DRA消除了基线架构中出现的所有灾难性失败案例，展现了跨异构成像协议的鲁棒泛化。

Conclusion: DINO-3DRA通过结构化跨维度特征迁移，有效解决了3DRA中动脉瘤分割面临的类别极度不平衡、与血管形态相似以及缺乏大规模3D预训练的问题，以极少可训练参数实现了SOTA性能与跨协议鲁棒泛化。

Abstract: Accurate aneurysm segmentation in 3D rotational angiography (3DRA) is hindered by extreme class imbalance, morphological similarity to vessels, and absent large-scale 3D pretraining. 2D vision foundation models encode dense structural priors from 1.7 billion images, yet naïve slice-wise transfer fragments anatomical continuity and destabilises optimisation. We propose DINO-3DRA, a dual-path framework achieving effective cross-dimensional semantic transfer by injecting frozen DINOv3 features into a 3D U-Net backbone via Room-Lite spatial mixing and calibrated residual fusion. On multi-centre 3DRA data, DINO-3DRA achieves state-of-the-art aneurysm segmentation (Dice: 0.758; HD95: 2.75 mm; +13% over nnU-Net) with only 5.72M trainable parameters. Ablation studies confirm that gains arise from structured cross-dimensional transfer rather than loss design alone, with bridged foundation features improving anatomical continuity between aneurysms and parent vessels. Without fine-tuning on CADA and SHINY-ICARUS, DINO-3DRA eliminates all catastrophic failure cases observed in baseline architectures, demonstrating robust generalisation across heterogeneous imaging protocols.

</details>


### [44] [SeqLoc: Beyond the Single Frame for Cross-View Geo-Localization in Feature-Sparse Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07835)
*Junwei Zheng,Yun Huang,Ruize Dai,Ruiping Liu,Yufan Chen,Kunyu Peng,Kailun Yang,Jiaming Zhang,Guangming Wang,Olaf Wysocki,Rainer Stiefelhagen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对跨视角地理定位在乡村等特征稀疏场景失效的问题，提出了SeqLoc序列聚合机制及配套基准测试集CV-FSS，显著提升了定位的精度和召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨视角地理定位在结构丰富的城市环境中表现良好，但在乡村道路等特征稀疏场景中性能急剧下降，需要解决这一失效模式以提升鲁棒性。

Method: 引入CV-FSS基准测试集，包含五个乡村地区的序列全景图及对OSM地图；提出SeqLoc在线测试时间序列聚合机制，包含三个关键组件：熵调节不确定性(ETU)、地图引导重定位(MGR)和峰值锚定平滑(PAS)。

Result: 在CV-FSS和CV-RHO数据集上的实验表明，SeqLoc大幅优于单帧定位方法，位置和方向召回率均提高了50%以上。

Conclusion: SeqLoc有效解决了特征稀疏场景下的定位难题，相关基准测试集和源代码将开源，为后续研究提供支持。

Abstract: Cross-View Geo-Localization (CVGL) with OpenStreetMap (OSM) performs well in structure-rich urban environments but collapses in feature-sparse scenes such as rural roads. To study this failure mode, in this work, we introduce CV-FSS, a benchmark that pairs sequential panoramas from five rural regions with aligned OSM maps, on which single-frame methods degrade drastically. We then propose SeqLoc, an online test-time sequence aggregation mechanism that recursively maintains a log-belief volume with three key components: (1) Entropy-Tempered Uncertainty (ETU) tempers each incoming pose likelihood volume by its normalized entropy; (2) Map-Guided Relocalization (MGR) mixes a map-shaped recovery distribution into the belief so that a suppressed true pose can recover; (3) Peak-Anchored Smoothing (PAS) derives the final pose at sub-grid precision. Extensive experiments on CV-FSS and CV-RHO demonstrate that SeqLoc outperforms single-frame localization by a large margin, improving both position and orientation recall by over 50%. The benchmark and source code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [45] [IRPol-Fuse: Energy-structure coordination for infrared polarization fusion under low visibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07848)
*Zhuangfan Huang,Chusheng Fang,Xiaosong Li,Yang Liua,Xiaoqi Cheng,Haishu Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对低能见度条件下现有红外-偏振图像融合方法过度强调红外响应、抑制偏振纹理的问题，提出能量-结构协同的IRPol-Fuse框架，通过三个关键模块实现红外与偏振信息的自适应融合，并构建专用评估数据集LI-PI。实验表明该方法在热目标保持、结构细节恢复和视觉自然度方面均表现优异，有效提升了低能见度场景下的感知鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低能见度条件下，感知系统需要融合图像同时保留红外热显著性和偏振衍生的结构细节。然而，现有红外-偏振图像融合方法常过度强调主导的红外响应，导致暗区中弱但信息丰富的偏振纹理被抑制，影响复杂场景下的感知性能。

Method: 提出IRPol-Fuse框架，采用能量-结构协同策略，包含三个模块：偏振注意力融合模块用于自适应分配红外与偏振信息；红外高亮注入模块基于高亮引导保留红外特征；偏振纹理注入模块恢复偏振纹理和精细细节。同时构建LI-PI数据集用于低能见度及视觉遮挡场景的评估。

Result: 在LI-PI和公开LDDRS数据集上的实验表明，IRPol-Fuse在热目标保持、结构细节恢复和视觉自然度方面均取得良好性能。区域感知评估和下游目标检测进一步验证了能量-结构协调策略能有效保留红外目标显著性和偏振衍生结构信息。

Conclusion: IRPol-Fuse通过能量-结构协同机制，有效解决了低能见度条件下红外-偏振图像融合中的信息失衡问题，实现了热显著性与结构细节的均衡保留，提升了复杂环境下的感知鲁棒性，为低能见度条件下的视觉感知提供了可靠方案。

Abstract: Robust perception under low-visibility conditions requires fused imagery that jointly preserves infrared thermal saliency and polarization-derived structural details. However, existing infrared-polarization image fusion (IPIF) methods often overemphasize dominant infrared responses, causing weak yet informative polarization textures in dark regions to be suppressed. To address this issue, we propose IRPol-Fuse, an energy-structure coordinated IPIF framework for challenging low-visibility scenarios. The proposed framework contains three key modules: Polarization Attention Fusion for adaptive infrared-polarization allocation, Infrared Highlight Injector for highlight-guided infrared preservation, and Polarization Texture Injector for polarization texture restoration and fine-detail recovery. We further construct LI-PI, a dedicated infrared-polarization evaluation dataset for low-visibility and visually concealed scenes. Experiments on LI-PI and the public LDDRS dataset demonstrate that IRPol-Fuse achieves favorable performance in thermal target preservation, structural detail recovery, and visual naturalness. Region-aware evaluation and downstream object detection further verify that the proposed energy-structure coordination strategy effectively preserves both infrared target saliency and polarization-derived structural information. Code is available at https://github.com/1hzf/IRPolar-Fuse .

</details>


### [46] [Distilling CT Foundation Models into Editable Concept Bottlenecks for Lung Nodule Malignancy Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07857)
*Fakrul Islam Tushar,Stephen Adamo,Geoffrey D. Rubin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究提出概念瓶颈模型，将两个冻结的CT基础模型表示映射到八个放射科医生定义的肺结节属性，并结合结节大小预测恶性，实现了可解释的恶性肿瘤预测，其性能与仅使用结节大小相当，但概念保真度受基础模型表示影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型提供了可迁移的CT表示，但直接基于这些嵌入的预测难以解释。因此，本研究旨在开发可解释的概念瓶颈模型，以透明地预测肺结节恶性。

Method: 构建了概念瓶颈模型，使用两个冻结的CT基础模型表示（CT-FM全CT自监督编码器和FMCIB结节聚焦对比编码器），通过岭回归概念头将表示映射到八个放射科医生定义的肺结节属性，并基于估计的概念和结节大小预测恶性。概念头在2610个LIDC-IDRI结节上训练，恶性模型在LUNA25上训练，并在内部测试集和外部DLCS队列上评估。概念保真度用五折交叉验证R²评估，恶性判别用AUROC及95%置信区间评估。

Result: 概念保真度中等，但FMCIB在细微、毛刺、纹理和分叶属性上优于CT-FM。内部AUROC为0.86（95%CI 0.80-0.92），外部AUROC为0.72-0.73，与仅结节大小的0.73相当，优于嵌入探针的0.60-0.67。预测可分解为特征级贡献，并通过受控概念干预修改。

Conclusion: 概念瓶颈模型提供了透明的恶性预测，判别能力与仅结节大小相当；概念保真度的差异表明概念恢复依赖于底层基础模型表示。

Abstract: Foundation models provide transferable CT representations, but predictions based directly on these embeddings are difficult to interpret. We developed concept bottleneck models that map two frozen CT foundation-model representations to eight radiologist-defined pulmonary-nodule attributes and predict malignancy from the estimated concepts and nodule size. The models included CT-FM, a whole-CT self-supervised encoder using a 96^3-voxel nodule-centered patch, and FMCIB, a nodule-focused contrastive encoder using a 50-mm crop. Eight ridge-regression concept heads were trained on 2,610 LIDC-IDRI nodules. Malignancy models were trained on LUNA25 and evaluated on a held-out internal test set and the external DLCS cohort. Concept fidelity was assessed using five-fold cross-validated R^2, and malignancy discrimination was assessed using AUROC with 95% confidence intervals estimated by patient-grouped bootstrap resampling. Concept fidelity was modest but higher for FMCIB than CT-FM for subtlety (R2, 0.24 vs. 0.11), spiculation (0.17 vs. 0.08), texture (0.17 vs. 0.07), and lobulation (0.15 vs. 0.05). Internally, the CT-FM and FMCIB concept+size models achieved AUROCs of 0.86 (95% CI, 0.80-0.92) and 0.86 (0.79-0.92), respectively. Externally, AUROCs were 0.72 (0.68-0.75) and 0.73 (0.70-0.76), compared with 0.73 for nodule size alone and 0.60 and 0.67 for the corresponding embedding only probes. Additive predictions could be decomposed into feature-level contributions and modified through controlled concept interventions. Concept bottlenecks provided transparent malignancy predictions with discrimination similar to nodule size alone, while differences in concept fidelity suggest that concept recovery depends on the underlying foundation-model representation.

</details>


### [47] [How Much Does It Cost to Answer My Question? Benchmarking Cloud VLM-based VQA Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07861)
*Henri Vanhuynegem,Weitao Xu,Yiran Shen,Guohao Lan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VQABench 是首个系统评估面向云VLM移动端视觉问答系统的客户端图像预处理技术的基准测试。研究发现预处理的好处高度依赖于模型、API、token计费规则及任务特性，选择不当反而会适得其反。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代VLM过于庞大，难以在移动端运行，导致必须将推理卸载到云端。由于专有API限制了控制能力，客户端预处理成为主要的优化手段，然而其在商业云VLM上的成本-质量权衡从未被系统研究。

Method: 作者构建了VQABench，在3个视觉问答数据集和4个来自3家提供商的商业VLM上评估了12种预处理技术，共调用API 95,168次，将预处理作为受控变量。

Result: 预处理并非普遍有益；其有效性取决于目标模型、API范式、提供商的token计费规则及任务表述。不当选择可能增加成本/延迟并降低准确性。

Conclusion: VQABench阐明了预处理的适用场景、失效情形及原因，为视觉问答系统的研究和部署提供了可操作的见解。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are becoming a practical backend for mobile visual question answering (VQA) systems, enabling smartphones and smart glasses to answer users' questions about the physical world. Since modern VLMs remain difficult to run on mobile and edge devices, VQA systems increasingly offload inference to cloud-based VLMs. This gives mobile devices access to stronger computation, but it also makes visual input preparation a key system variable: how the image is prepared before offloading affects not only answer quality but also payload size, token cost, and system latency. Proprietary APIs expose little control over model internals or serving behavior, leaving client-side preprocessing as the main practical optimization space for downstream developers. Many such techniques have been proposed for visual offloading, yet their cost-quality impact on commercial cloud VLMs has never been studied. To fill this gap, we present VQABench, the first systematic benchmark that treats client-side input preprocessing as a controlled variable for cloud-VLM-based VQA. We evaluate 12 preprocessing techniques across three VQA datasets and four commercial VLMs from three providers, totaling 95,168 API calls. Our results show that preprocessing is not universally beneficial: its effectiveness depends on the target model, API paradigm, provider token-accounting rule, and task formulation. A poorly selected preprocessing strategy can increase deployment cost or latency while degrading answer accuracy. Overall, our benchmark clarifies when preprocessing helps, when it fails, and why, providing insights to guide future research and real-world deployment of VQA systems.

</details>


### [48] [LHSDet: High-Resolution AI-Generated Image Detection via Visual Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07863)
*Qian Yao,Jun-Jie Huang,Yongjun Wang,Luming Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LHSDet，一种基于多模态特征融合的高分辨率AI生成图像检测方法，有效克服现有方法降采样导致的细节丢失问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着扩散模型和自回归模型的发展，AI生成图像的逼真度和分辨率已接近真实图像。然而，现有检测方法通常对图像进行降采样，导致丢失高分辨率图像中的低频纹理细节，限制了检测性能；此外，未知生成模型的不断出现使得大规模预训练数据集难以获取。

Method: 将AI生成图像检测任务建模为视觉问答问题，利用微调的视觉-语言框架，结合视觉和文本模态的互补信息。重新设计视觉编码器以更好捕获AI生成图像的低级和高级伪影。引入语义级文本分支实现多模态特征融合。采用三分支架构：低级视觉分支聚合非重叠图块提取局部纹理线索；基于SigLIP2的高级视觉分支提取全局感知特征；基于BLIP-2的语义文本分支生成图像描述。

Result: 大量实验表明，LHSDet在不同生成模型（包括扩散和自回归模型）上均实现了高检测精度和鲁棒性能。

Conclusion: LHSDet通过多模态特征融合和专门设计的视觉编码器，有效解决了高分辨率AI生成图像检测的挑战，为应对不断涌现的新生成模型提供了有力工具。

Abstract: Driven by advances in diffusion models and autoregressive models, the fidelity and resolution of AI-generated images now rival those of real images. However, existing AI-generated image detection methods often downsample the images, inevitably overlooking critical low-level texture details in high-resolution AI-generated images, therefore limiting their detection performance. In addition, the ceaseless emergence of unknown generative models makes large-scale pre-training datasets inaccessible. To address these challenges, we propose a novel high-resolution AI-generated image detector, termed LHSDet. Specifically, we formulate the AI-generated image detection task as a Visual Question Answering problem, leveraging a fine-tuned vision-language framework to fully exploit the complementary information between visual and textual modalities. Recognizing that the default visual encoder of existing vision-language models is not tailored for AI-generated image detection, we redesign a visual encoder to better capture both the low-level and high-level artifacts inherent in AI-generated images. Furthermore, we incorporate a semantic-level textual branch to enable multi-modal feature fusion and detection. Consequently, LHSDet employs a triple-branch architecture to extract complementary multi-modal features: a low-level visual branch that aggregates non-overlapping patches for local texture cues, a high-level visual branch based on SigLIP2 for global perception feature extraction, and a semantic-level textual branch that generates captions using BLIP-2. Extensive experimental results demonstrate that LHSDet achieves high detection accuracy and robust performance across diverse generative models, including both diffusion and autoregressive models.

</details>


### [49] [UniScale: Arbitrary-Scale Industrial Anomaly Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07864)
*Shilei Zeng,Linxin Guan,Xurui Li,Yaohan Tang,Yu Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对工业异常检测中小规模异常生成的难题，提出UniScale框架，通过误差抑制多尺度训练和生成后融合去噪技术，实现任意尺度异常的高质量生成，显著提升了生成质量和下游检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业异常检测面临真实异常样本匮乏的挑战。现有生成模型在处理小规模异常时存在困难，因为扩散模型中的极端下采样导致小异常信息在潜在空间中丢失。

Method: 提出UniScale统一训练与推理框架。训练阶段引入误差抑制多尺度训练（EMT）策略，使模型学习异常的位置感知纹理，同时抑制上采样插值误差。推理阶段提出生成后融合去噪技术，将异常生成与背景集成解耦，防止小异常被淹没。

Result: 实验显示该方法在异常生成质量和下游检测性能上均优于最先进方法。VisA上相对IS(a)提升45.86%（1.81→2.64），MVTec AD 2上提升37.70%（1.22→1.68）；下游检测性能：VisA上像素级IoU提升4.22%，MVTec AD 2上AUROC提升6.55%。

Conclusion: UniScale框架有效解决了小尺度异常生成的信息丢失问题，实现了跨尺度的高保真工业异常生成，在生成质量和检测性能方面取得显著突破，为工业异常检测提供了更可靠的训练数据生成方案。

Abstract: Industrial anomaly inspection faces a major challenge due to the lack of real-world anomaly samples. While generative models are used to create anomaly data, existing methods still struggle when handling small-scale anomalies.This failure occurs because extreme downsampling in diffusion models causes the information of small anomalies to be lost in the latent space.To address this, we introduce UniScale, a unified training and inference framework for high-fidelity industrial anomaly generation across arbitrary scales.During training, we introduce an Error-Suppressed Multi-Scale Training (EMT) strategy, which enables the model to learn the rich location-aware textures of anomalies, while suppressing upsampling-induced interpolation errors in texture acquisition, ensuring the model is capable of learning small-scale anomalies, while remaining effective for regular scale anomalies.For inference, we propose Generation-then-Fusion Denoising. It decouples anomaly generation from background integration, preventing small anomalies from being overwhelmed.Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method outperforms state-of-the-art competitors in both anomaly generation quality and downstream detection performance.It achieves a relative IS(a) improvement of 45.86\% (from 1.81 to 2.64) on VisA and 37.70\% (from 1.22 to 1.68) on MVTec AD 2, while also improving the downstream pixel-level IoU by 4.22\% on VisA and AUROC by 6.55\% on MVTec AD 2. Code is available at https://github.com/HUST-SLOW/UniScale.

</details>


### [50] [Vision-Language Grounding as Bidirectional Concept Correspondence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07886)
*Jieyu Zhang,Ziqi Gao,Luke Zettlemoyer,Ranjay Krishna*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种双向概念对应（bidirectional concept correspondence）的视觉-语言接地（Vision-language grounding）新范式。不同于传统单向定位方法，该方法旨在同时识别文本中的视觉指代片段及其对应的图像区域。作者提出了基于预训练视觉-语言模型的ConCor-1模型，利用可学习的桥接令牌（bridge tokens）预测文本掩码、图像掩码和对应存在分数。在长标题数据集和零样本LVIS数据集上的实验表明，ConCor-1显著优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言接地方法通常将其简化为单向定位问题，即给定预定的文本短语或类别名称来识别图像区域。这种设定假设相关的语言单元已知，忽略了接地交流中一个更基础挑战：确定文本中哪些部分具有视觉指代性以及它们如何对应于图像中的实体。

Method: 作者将接地任务公式化为图像-文本对上的“双向概念对应”。为此，他们提出了ConCor-1模型，该模型建立在预训练视觉-语言模型之上。该模型使用可学习的“桥接令牌”来表示候选的图像-文本对应关系，并为每个令牌预测一个文本掩码、一个图像掩码以及一个对应存在分数。为了训练和评估该任务，作者将多种接地和分割数据集转换为统一的对应格式。

Result: 实验结果显示，ConCor-1模型始终优于基线方法。在长标题数据集上，其对应F1分数提高了48%；在零样本LVIS数据集上（该数据集的大类别列表作为文本输入），F1分数提高了29%。

Conclusion: 本文提出的双向概念对应公式统一了常见的接地任务，包括短语接地、指代表达接地和开放词汇检测，将文本分割、图像分割和跨模态对齐视为单一的对应预测问题。

Abstract: Vision-language grounding connects language to visual content, yet most existing formulations reduce grounding to a unidirectional localization problem: given a prespecified text phrase or category name, identify the corresponding image region. This setup assumes that the relevant linguistic unit is already known, overlooking a more basic challenge in grounded communication: determining which parts of the text are visually referential and how they correspond to entities in the image. We formulate grounding as $\textit{bidirectional concept correspondence}$ over an image-text pair. Given an image and its paired text, the goal is to recover all correspondences between visually referential text spans and instance-level image segments, without assuming that the relevant text spans are provided. This formulation unifies common grounding tasks, including phrase grounding, referring expression grounding, and open-vocabulary detection, by treating text segmentation, image segmentation, and cross-modal alignment as a single correspondence prediction problem. To address this task, we introduce $\textbf{ConCor-1}$, a grounding model built on top of a pretrained vision-language model. It uses learnable $\textit{bridge tokens}$ to represent candidate image-text correspondences and predicts, for each token, a text mask, an image mask, and a correspondence presence score. To train and evaluate this task, we convert diverse grounding and segmentation datasets into a unified correspondence format. Experiments show that $\textbf{ConCor-1}$ consistently outperforms baselines, improving correspondence F1 by 48% on the long-caption dataset and by 29% on zero-shot LVIS, where the large category list serves as the text input.

</details>


### [51] [DeCo: Zero-Shot Industrial Anomaly Generation through Decoupling and Recoupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07904)
*Shilei Zeng,Xurui Li,Yaohan Tang,Yu Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DeCo方法，通过解耦异常结构与其源产品并显式地重新耦合到目标产品的正常纹理上，实现零样本工业异常生成，达到新的最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业异常检测因真实异常数据稀缺而受到严重阻碍。零样本工业异常生成通过无需真实异常图像即可在特定产品上生成异常来解决这一问题。然而，现有方法存在两个关键局限性：异常信息获取不准确和异常-产品融合不可控。

Method: DeCo方法在异常信息获取阶段使用双路由流(DR-Flow)将纹理不变异常结构绑定到异常令牌，同时并行约束产品不变流(PI-Flow)防止异常令牌绑定源产品。在异常-产品融合阶段，提出混合注入将获取的异常结构与目标产品重新耦合，并使用产品兼容性校正(PCC)补偿获取的异常结构与产品之间的不兼容性。

Result: 大量实验表明DeCo建立了新的最先进性能。在生成的数据上训练下游检测模型，在MVTec AD上像素AP提升5.1%，在VisA上提升8.2%。

Conclusion: DeCo通过解耦异常结构与其源产品并显式地将其与目标产品的正常纹理重新耦合，成功克服了现有方法的局限性，显著提升了工业异常检测性能。

Abstract: Industrial anomaly inspection is severely hindered by the scarcity of real anomalous data.Zero-shot industrial anomaly generation addresses this by generating anomalies on specific products without requiring any of their real anomalous images. However, existing methods suffer from two critical limitations, i.e., inaccurate anomaly information acquisition and uncontrolled anomaly-product fusion.To overcome these challenges, we propose DeCo, which decouples the anomaly structure from its source product, and explicitly recouples it with the normal textures of the target product. During anomaly information acquisition,Dual-Routing Flow (DR-Flow) binds the texture-invariant anomaly structure to an abnormal token, while a parallel constraint, Product-Invariant Flow (PI-Flow), prevents the abnormal token from binding the source product. During anomaly-product fusion, we propose a hybrid injection to recouple the acquired anomaly structure with the target product, and Product Compatibility Correction (PCC) to compensate for the incompatibility between the acquired anomaly structure and the product. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DeCo establishes a new state-of-the-art. Training downstream detection models on our generated data yields massive pixel AP improvements of 5.1\% on MVTec AD and 8.2\% on VisA. Code is available at https://github.com/HUST-SLOW/DeCo.

</details>


### [52] [SegDem: Segmentation helps Demosaicing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07916)
*Ping Chen,Xiangming Wang,Yongyong Chen,Jiezhang Cao,Kai Zhang,Jingyong Su,Jie Liu,Haijin Zeng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SegDem通过跨任务解码器表示迁移，将实例分割的结构化表征迁移至demosaicing任务，利用视觉理解与重建共享的场景结构一致性，在不同架构和CFA布局下显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有demosaicing方法多依赖像素级重建、局部纹理、跨通道相关性和低级图像统计，忽略了重建与视觉理解共享的物理世界结构。本文认为两者应保持一致的结构和物理信息，从而提出跨任务表示迁移。

Method: SegDem框架首先通过实例感知结构预训练学习区域和边界感知的表征，然后将解码器迁移至RAW条件重建任务。分割和demosaicing条件特征锚定到共享的冻结DINOv2表示空间以保持结构一致性。支持卷积、Transformer和状态空间骨干网络，统一适用于Single-和Quad-Bayer demosaicing。

Result: 在合成、外部和挑战性数据集上的大量实验表明，SegDem在不同架构和CFA布局下均能取得一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过利用视觉理解与重建任务间的结构一致性，SegDem证明了跨任务表示迁移在demosaicing中的有效性，实现了多架构、多CFA布局下的性能突破。

Abstract: Image demosaicing reconstructs a full-color image from incomplete color measurements produced by a sensor covered with a color filter array (CFA). Most existing methods formulate demosaicing as pixel-level reconstruction and mainly rely on local textures, cross-channel correlations, and low-level image statistics. Our core insight is that reconstruction and visual understanding can be viewed as complementary views of shared scene structure: both are grounded in the same underlying physical world, and therefore the structural and physical information inferred from an image should remain consistent across the two tasks. We instantiate this idea with instance segmentation and propose \emph{SegDem}, a cross-task decoder representation transfer framework for demosaicing. SegDem first learns region- and boundary-aware representations through instance-aware structural pretraining and then transfers the decoder to RAW-conditioned reconstruction. Segmentation- and demosaicing-conditioned features are further anchored to a shared frozen DINOv2 representation space to preserve structural organization across tasks. We instantiate SegDem with convolutional, Transformer-based, and state-space backbones for unified Single- and Quad-Bayer demosaicing. Extensive experiments on synthetic, external, and challenging datasets demonstrate consistent improvements across different architectures and CFA layouts.

</details>


### [53] [Forged Peer Judgments Mislead Multimodal LLM Judge Panels: Source-Blind Anchoring and Panel-Consensus Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07920)
*Yang Shu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文揭示了多模态大语言模型(VLM)评审面板中的"源盲锚定"文本攻击面：通过引用伪造的视觉判断可将正确裁决翻转为错误的比率提高1.5-2.7倍。提出的"面板共识验证"防御机制可拦截84.9%的伪造攻击，净伤害降低97.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态LLM评审面板可通过交叉引用同行进行判断，但被引用的同行判断本身可能是不可信的。这一信任假设构成了潜在的安全漏洞，需要识别其攻击面并开发防御机制。

Method: 通过实验揭示"源盲锚定"攻击：引用独立视觉判断在自我和同行框架下均产生19-26个百分点的锚定差距。采用匹配内容、仅标签对照实验验证标签本身的影响。对比故意生成的简洁错误引用与自然错误同行陈述的破坏力。提出"面板共识验证"防御：将引用与独立收集的盲选投票进行交叉核查。

Result: (1)引用独立视觉判断产生显著锚定差距(19-26pp)；(2)仅标签对照实验显示标签本身不影响破坏率(变化仅-0.17pp)；(3)故意生成的错误引用推翻正确裁决的频率是自然错误陈述的1.5-2.7倍(两个数据集、七个VLM评审均确认)；(4)面板共识验证拦截84.9%伪造攻击，净伤害降低97.5%，并保留对真实同行信息的正向点估计。

Conclusion: 本文识别出多模态协作评估中低成本的攻击面，并提供了具体的防御方案。该防御机制在有效阻断伪造攻击的同时，不显著影响真实同行信息的利用价值，为更安全的多模态协作评估提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Multimodal LLM judge panels can cross-reference peers, but a quoted peer judgment may itself be untrusted. We expose source-blind anchoring as a text-level attack surface in vision-language model (VLM) panels. Quoting independent visual judgments creates large anchoring gaps (19--26 percentage points) under both self and peer framing. A matched-content, label-only control changes the broken rate by only $-0.17$pp (95\% CI $[-0.68,0.35]$), showing that the self/peer label itself does not explain the effect. Under our tested construction, deliberately generated, concise wrong quotes overturn originally-correct verdicts 1.5--2.7$\times$ more often than naturally occurring wrong peer statements, with bootstrap 95\% CIs excluding parity across two datasets and seven VLM judges. Because the two statement populations differ in selection and form, this ratio measures differential damage under the tested attack rather than a provenance-only causal effect. We then introduce panel-consensus verification, which cross-checks a quote against independently collected blind votes. It blocks 84.9\% of fabricated attacks, cuts their net harm by 97.5\%, and preserves the positive but statistically inconclusive point estimate for genuine peer information under leave-one-out re-verification. These results identify a low-cost attack surface and a concrete defense for safer multimodal collaborative evaluation.

</details>


### [54] [SCoPE: Training-Free Audio-Visual Event Perception via Sparse Cross-Modal Prior Exchange](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07923)
*Jaemo Jeong,Junho Yoon,Hyunju Kim,Dongman Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SCoPE，一种无需训练的框架，通过让所有查询标签竞争共享证据并利用跨模态引导，解决音频-视觉事件感知中的虚假共激活问题，在多个数据集上显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练无免费的AVEP方法在处理新事件词汇时，相关标签共享证据导致错误标签可能得分不低于正确标签，产生虚假共激活(FCA)问题。标量阈值无法同时排除错误标签并保留正确标签。

Method: SCoPE框架让所有查询标签竞争共享证据，每个模态引导另一模态的事件选择，并推导出二标签拟合下竞争消除FCA的精确条件。

Result: 在LLP上使用相同的冻结CLIP+CLAP骨干，SCoPE使Type@seg提升7.45点，Event@seg提升5.04点，相比AV^2A报告值。同一固定配置可直接迁移至OV-AVEBench和VGGSound-AVEL100k。

Conclusion: SCoPE有效缓解了FCA问题，在多个数据集上实现了显著的性能提升，且无需重新训练即可迁移。

Abstract: Audio-visual event perception (AVEP) determines which events occur in a video, when they occur, and whether they are audible, visible, or both. Training-free methods query new event vocabularies by matching frozen audio and visual features with text-encoded event names. However, related labels share evidence. An incorrect label can then score at least as high as a correct one. We call this a false co-activation (FCA). No scalar cutoff can reject the incorrect label while keeping every correct one. Class-specific thresholds may prevent that label from becoming a final prediction, but the FCA remains in the underlying score vector. We introduce SCoPE, a training-free framework in which all queried labels compete for shared evidence and each modality guides event selection in the other. We derive an exact condition for when this competition removes an FCA in a two-label fit. With identical frozen CLIP+CLAP backbones on LLP, SCoPE improves Type@seg by 7.45 points and Event@seg by 5.04 points compared with the reported AV$^2$A values. The same fixed configuration transfers unchanged to OV-AVEBench and VGGSound-AVEL100k.

</details>


### [55] [SportsGrounder: Proposal-Aided Interleaved Grounding for Dense Sports Video Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07932)
*Yizhi Li,Jiawei Jiang,Guanhong Wang,Yingcai Wu,Gaoang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SportsGrounder，一个利用开放词汇视觉专家进行密集体育视频推理的框架，通过域引导对象建议、交替定位融合(IGF)机制和行动感知监督(AAS)模块，结合混合偏好优化(MPO)，在体育问答任务上取得最新状态精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 体育视频分析需要细粒度理解大量小尺度、高互动性、视觉同质实体（如球员、球），当前多模态模型缺乏细粒度视觉细节，过度依赖文本先验，难以区分相似动作和球员。

Method: 提出SportsGrounder框架，包含域引导对象建议提取、IGF机制逐帧融合显式边界框坐标与隐式视觉语义，保持时间对齐并避免序列长度爆炸；AAS模块直接正则化隐藏状态，学习准确运动表示；使用MPO优化以区分欺骗性干扰。

Result: 在基于SoccerNet和FineSports构建的密集体育VQA数据集上实验表明，SportsGrounder显著提升细粒度推理能力，达到最新状态精度。

Conclusion: SportsGrounder有效解决体育视频密集推理中的细粒度视觉理解问题，为体育分析和广播增强提供了更准确的解决方案。

Abstract: Sports video analysis is crucial for athletic analytics and broadcasting enhancement. Dense sports video reasoning, however, demands a fine-grained understanding of numerous small-scale, highly interactive, and visually homogeneous entities (e.g., players sharing identical uniforms, the ball) across long temporal contexts. Current Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) inherently struggle with such dense visual complexities. Due to the lack of fine-grained visual details, these models often over-rely on textual priors to guess answers, especially when distinguishing visually similar actions and players. To address this, we propose \textbf{SportsGrounder}, a framework that leverages an open-vocabulary visual expert to aid interleaved grounding specifically for dense sports video reasoning. To achieve precise spatial localization, we extract domain-guided object proposals and introduce an Interleaved Grounding Fusion (IGF) mechanism. The IGF frame-by-frame integrates explicit bounding box coordinates and implicit visual semantics with global grid features. This design preserves strict temporal alignment and prevents sequence length explosion. Furthermore, we design an Action-Aware Supervision (AAS) module that directly regularizes the model's hidden states, forcing the network to learn accurate motion representations rather than relying on language bias. Optimized with Mixed Preference Optimization (MPO) to better distinguish deceptive distractors, our extensive experiments on newly curated dense sports VQA datasets (derived from SoccerNet and FineSports) demonstrate that SportsGrounder significantly improves fine-grained reasoning and achieves state-of-the-art accuracy.

</details>


### [56] [FlexSplat: Flexible Feed-Forward 3D Gaussian Splatting without Point Cloud Correspondence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07937)
*Amir Sabbaghziarani,Hanting Ye,Maria Gorlatova,Yi Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlexSplat是一个前馈新视角合成框架，能从无标定、物体中心的多视角图像集合中重建紧凑的3D高斯表示。该方法通过联合训练几何变换器与高斯解码器，在无需相机位姿和真实深度先验的情况下，达到与有标定SOTA方法相当的重建质量，且高斯数量不随图像分辨率或视角数量增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于查询的方法通过Transformer查询细化3D高斯表示，但依赖已知的相机位姿假设，限制了实际应用。FlexSplat旨在移除这一标定依赖，实现从完全无标定图像集合中进行新视角合成。

Method: 采用几何变换器与高斯解码器联合训练架构：1)几何变换器预测每幅图像的相机参数和深度图；2)深度引导的高斯参数化利用预测深度约束3D高斯分布；3)多视图可变形交叉注意力聚合所有输入视图证据，生成单一、视图一致的基元集合；4)不确定性加权的深度一致性目标使几何前端适应重建任务；5)表示使用与输入分辨率解耦的紧凑高斯预算，基元数量不随图像网格或视角数量增长。

Result: 在ShapeNet-SRN和Google Scanned Objects数据集上评估显示：FlexSplat在无需相机位姿和真实深度条件下，匹配或接近有标定SOTA重建器性能；在GSO上实现最佳感知质量（LPIPS），与有标定方法的PSNR差距仅0.7 dB，证明联合训练几何前端足以实现免标定操作。

Conclusion: FlexSplat验证了联合训练几何前端可有效将免标定能力引入基于查询的高斯重建框架，在显著简化输入要求的同时保持与有标定方法相当的视觉质量，为无标定场景下的3D重建提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: We present FlexSplat, a feed-forward framework for novel view synthesis (NVS) from uncalibrated, object-centric multi-view image collections. A recent line of query-based methods reconstructs a compact set of 3D Gaussians by treating them as transformer queries that are refined with multi-view deformable attention; these methods, however, assume that camera poses are given. FlexSplat removes this assumption: a geometry transformer is trained jointly with the Gaussian decoder to predict per-image camera parameters and depth, which in turn ground a depth-guided Gaussian parameterization and a multi-view deformable cross-attention that aggregates evidence across all input views into a single, view-consistent set of primitives. An uncertainty-weighted depth-consistency objective lets the jointly trained geometry adapt to the reconstruction task, while the cross-view consensus formed during decoding absorbs the residual error of the estimated cameras and depth. The representation uses a compact Gaussian budget that is decoupled from the input resolution - unlike pixel-aligned methods, the primitive count does not grow with the image grid - and is not dictated by the number of views. On ShapeNet-SRN and Google Scanned Objects (GSO), FlexSplat matches or approaches posed state-of-the-art reconstructors while requiring neither camera poses nor ground-truth depth, and matches the best perceptual (LPIPS) quality among the compared methods on GSO. Our results indicate that a jointly trained geometry front-end is sufficient to bring calibration-free operation to query-based Gaussian reconstruction while staying within 0.7 dB PSNR of posed methods and matching their perceptual quality.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [57] [Unified Hallucination Fuzzing for Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07525)
*Pengfei Zhou,Jiajun Song,Zhiwei Tang,Yixing Ma,Xiaopeng Peng,Donghui Si,Yuhang Xu,Huiqi Song,Yiyuan Miao,Yichen Qian,Weihua Chen,Wangbo Zhao,Bohan Zhuang,Jiasheng Tang,Yang You*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的幻觉问题，提出了一套整合全面基准与自进化压力测试的系统性评估框架。框架包含精细化的UniHall数据集（覆盖对象、指令、知识三个维度）和自适应性多模态模糊测试（SAMF）方法。实验表明，主流MLLMs在模糊测试下性能显著下降，揭示推理能力与事实依据之间的脱节，并发现强化学习对齐会加剧指令遵循任务中的谄媚性幻觉问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 幻觉问题仍是多模态大语言模型面临的持续挑战，严重限制了其在高风险应用中的可靠性。现有基于静态基准的评估方法存在分类覆盖面窄和性能快速饱和的问题，无法反映模型在不断变化的真实世界场景中的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出一套系统性评估框架，整合了全面基准与自进化压力测试。具体包括：1）UniHall数据集——基于统一分类法（涵盖对象、指令、知识维度）构建的细粒度数据集；2）自适应性多模态模糊测试（SAMF）——采用进化突变策略探索模型幻觉边界，并引入由多模态预言机集合驱动的指标套件以确保动态输入的可靠评估。

Result: 广泛实验表明，与传统设置相比，最先进的MLLMs在模糊测试下表现出显著的性能下降，暴露了推理能力与事实依据之间的脱节。研究发现了一种帮助性-幻觉权衡关系：强化学习对齐无意中加剧了指令遵循任务中的谄媚性（sycophancy）问题。

Conclusion: 该工作强调了超越静态基准的评估方法的必要性，以评估MLLMs在动态、真实世界场景中的性能。框架、代码和基准已公开可用，为社区提供了更全面的幻觉评估工具。

Abstract: Hallucination remains a persistent challenge for Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), severely limiting their reliability in high-stakes applications. Existing evaluations, predominantly based on static benchmarks, suffer from narrow taxonomical coverage and rapid performance saturation, failing to reflect model robustness in evolving real-world scenarios. To bridge this gap, we present a systematic evaluation framework integrating a comprehensive benchmark with self-evolving stress testing. First, we introduce UniHall, a fine-grained dataset grounded in a unified taxonomy spanning Object, Instruction, and Knowledge dimensions. Second, to address benchmark saturation, we propose Self-Adaptive Multimodal Fuzzing (SAMF), a self-adaptive framework that employs evolutionary mutation strategies to explore the boundaries of model hallucinations. Crucially, to ensure reliable assessment of dynamic inputs, SAMF incorporates a structured metric suite driven by an ensemble of multi-modal oracles. Our extensive experiments reveal that state-of-the-art MLLMs exhibit significant performance degradation under fuzzing compared to conventional settings, exposing a dissociation between reasoning capabilities and factual grounding. Furthermore, we identify a helpfulness-hallucination trade-off, where reinforcement learning alignment inadvertently exacerbates sycophancy in instruction-following tasks. The framework, code and benchmark are available at https://github.com/LanceZPF/EvalHall.

</details>


### [58] [DocAtlas: Long-Document Understanding as Mutable-State Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07527)
*Hongchen Wei,Yuanzhe Wang,Bei Liu,Yifan Yang,Qi Dai,Kai Qiu,Yunsheng Li,Dongdong Chen,Chong Luo,Zhenzhong Chen,Baining Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DocAtlas将长文档理解视为可变状态的信息寻求过程，通过可变文档工具包（包含搜索、阅读、笔记、审查工具）实现自我改进检索、选择性证据访问和主动工作记忆。该系统在MMLongBench-Doc上达到71.4%，超过人类专家基准65.8%，并显著提升紧凑VLM代理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长文档理解需要模型在大量页面、布局、表格、图表中找到并结合证据。现有检索增强系统通常在生成前从静态索引选择证据，而智能体系统虽添加多轮工具使用，但依赖提示词设定的冻结专有模型。传统方法难以灵活处理动态信息需求。

Method: DocAtlas实例化为可变文档工具包：外部环境决定每一步向模型展示什么文档信息。该工具包提供搜索、阅读、笔记和审查工具，维护分层树状结构和笔记存储，并随代理记录证据而更新。系统结合自我改进检索、选择性证据访问和主动工作记忆，在固定上下文预算下运行，支持大VLM推理时使用和紧凑VLM代理的端到端强化学习。

Result: GPT-5.4配合DocAtlas在MMLongBench-Doc上达到71.4%，超过人类专家参考值65.8%。使用DocAtlas环境进行端到端强化学习训练的Qwen3.5-4B VLM达到63.7%，相比直接输入基线54.4%有显著提升，证明可变文档工具包设计能大幅改善紧凑文档代理性能。

Conclusion: 可变文档工具包设计有效解决长文档理解挑战，通过动态信息管理和主动工作记忆，不仅支持大型VLM实现超越人类的性能，还能为紧凑VLM代理带来显著提升，验证了该方法的有效性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Long-document understanding requires models to find and combine evidence across many pages, layouts, tables, figures, and charts. Existing retrieval-augmented systems usually select evidence from a static index before generation, while recent agentic systems add multi-turn tool use but often rely on frozen proprietary backbones whose behavior is set by prompts. We present DocAtlas, a system that treats long-document understanding as a mutable-state information-seeking process. We instantiate DocAtlas as a mutable document harness: an external environment that determines what document information is searched, read, stored, reviewed, and shown to the model at each step. Given a document and question, the harness exposes search, reading, note-taking, and review tools, maintains a hierarchical tree and note store, and updates both as the agent records evidence. DocAtlas combines self-improving retrieval, selective evidence access, and active working memory under a fixed context budget. The same harness supports inference-time use with large VLMs and end-to-end reinforcement learning for compact VLM agents. With GPT-5.4, DocAtlas reaches 71.4\% on MMLongBench-Doc, exceeding the human-expert reference of 65.8\%. A Qwen3.5-4B VLM trained with end-to-end RL in the DocAtlas environment reaches 63.7\%, compared with a 54.4\% direct-input baseline, showing that mutable document-harness design can improve compact document agents by a large margin.

</details>


### [59] [WuYuEval: A Multi-Level Benchmark for Large Language Models in Solid Waste Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07529)
*Yi Zhang,Hongyang Wang,Zheng Hao Leong,Zihao Wu,Kaijun Lin,Zhixing Pan,Qixun Huangfu,Wei Ren,Wenyan Wu,Fangyun Wang,Wenting Yu,Hengyu Lin,Muling Yang,Zongguo Wen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: WuYuEval 是一个针对固废管理领域的大语言模型多级评估基准，包含基础知识和专家决策模块，评估结果显示模型在复杂约束和开放性问题上的能力仍有限，思维链推理仅在受控条件下有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准侧重通用知识而非固废管理专业决策；LLM在固废管理中的能力难以评估；需要覆盖工程、环境和政策约束的专业评估工具。

Method: 构建WuYuEval基准，包含4590道封闭式选择题（6类任务、8个领域）和247道场景式开放式专家题；采用锚定校准的LLM-as-a-Judge评分与Elo配对比较；审计质量；评估33个LLM。

Result: 领先模型在基础模块达94.64%准确率，但平均准确率从简单题84.14%降至难题42.50%；在计算、实验设计、城市规划和开放式专家任务表现较低；推理导向的Thinking模式对多数配对模型有提升，但依赖基线能力且不总是正效应；可见推理仅在与单位、假设和工程约束锚定时有效，否则易偏离答案边界。

Conclusion: WuYuEval为固废管理领域大模型评估提供资源和实证基础，有助于开发具备专业推理链和显式约束控制能力的固废管理基础模型。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as technical assistants, but their competence in solid waste management (SWM) remains difficult to assess because existing benchmarks emphasize general knowledge rather than professional decisions under engineering, environmental, and policy constraints. We introduce WuYuEval, a multi-level benchmark for evaluating LLMs in SWM across foundational knowledge, domain reasoning, and expert decision-making. After quality auditing, WuYuEval contains a Foundation Module with 4,590 closed-ended multiple-choice questions across six task types and eight domain categories, together with an Expert Module with 247 scenario-based open-ended questions involving multi-objective optimization, constraint trade-offs, and system design. For expert tasks, we combine anchor-calibrated LLM-as-a-Judge scoring with Elo-based pairwise comparison. Across 33 LLMs, performance varied widely. The leading model reached 94.64\% accuracy on the Foundation Module, but average accuracy still fell from 84.14\% on easy questions to 42.50\% on hard questions, with lower performance concentrated in calculation, experimental design, urban planning, and open-ended expert tasks. Reasoning-oriented Thinking modes improve most matched model pairs after auditing, but the gains depend on baseline capability and are not uniformly positive. These results suggest that visible deliberation helps only when it remains anchored to units, assumptions, and engineering constraints; otherwise, it may drift from decisive answer boundaries. WuYuEval therefore provides both an evaluation resource and an empirical basis for developing SWM-oriented foundation models with professional reasoning chains and explicit constraint control.

</details>


### [60] [Search-G1: Grounded Search Agents via Representation-Based Intrinsic Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07531)
*Cheng Ruoxi,Ma Haoxuan,Zhang Hongyi,Zhang Junming,Duan Ranjie,Xia Qiaolin,Wang Hao,Lu Yu,Shi Haibo,Ma Xingjun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Search-G1，一种基于表示的内在奖励框架，通过两个干预校准的读取机制测量智能体答案的操作落地程度，在无需昂贵注释或LLM推理的情况下改善了落地-搜索成本权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 搜索增强语言智能体应仅在必要时检索外部信息并基于检索证据回答问题。现有外部奖励存在局限：结果奖励无法区分有依据的检索与冗余搜索，而更丰富的信号需要昂贵的注释或训练时的推理。内部奖励主要反映模型置信度而非证据落地。需要一种既能有效指导智能体高效搜索行为，又不依赖高成本外部信号的内在奖励机制。

Method: Search-G1框架通过两个干预校准的读取机制工作：(1)提示状态读取预测闭卷充分性，其补集定义策略相对检索必要性；(2)答案承诺读取从证据删除时的答案阶段敏感性估计证据依赖。该框架在检索估计必要且答案具有证据敏感性时，为正确的搜索轨迹提供额外奖励；在闭卷知识充分时支持直接回答；并惩罚重复搜索。由于强化学习会改变策略表示，Search-G1定期在最新检查点的轨迹上重新拟合两个读取机制，使奖励与策略共同进化。奖励评分无需过程注释或策略优化期间的LLM作为裁判推理。

Result: 在多个基于搜索的问答基准和两个模型规模上的实验表明，Search-G1改善了落地-搜索成本权衡，在具有竞争力的任务准确率下产生更短的回答侧轨迹。

Conclusion: Search-G1提供了一个成本有效的内在奖励框架，使语言智能体能够学习高效且有依据的搜索行为，无需在策略优化过程中依赖过程注释或LLM作为裁判推理，实现了智能体搜索效率与答案质量的有效平衡。

Abstract: Search-augmented language agents should retrieve external information only when necessary and ground their answers in retrieved evidence. Existing external rewards provide either sparse outcome supervision or richer feedback from process annotations and LLM judges. Outcome rewards scale readily but cannot distinguish grounded retrieval from redundant search, whereas richer signals require costly annotation or inference during training. Internal rewards based on policy-side signals such as entropy, likelihood, or information gain are graded and inexpensive to evaluate, yet mainly reflect model confidence rather than evidence grounding. We propose Search-G1, a representation-based intrinsic reward framework that measures the operational grounding of an agent's answers through two intervention-calibrated readouts. A prompt-state readout predicts closed-book sufficiency, whose complement defines policy-relative retrieval necessity; an answer-commit readout estimates evidence reliance from answer-stage sensitivity to evidence deletion. Together, they provide additional credit to correct searched trajectories when retrieval is estimated necessary and the answer is evidence-sensitive, favor correct direct answers when closed-book knowledge suffices, and penalize repeated search. After calibration, reward scoring requires neither process annotations nor LLM-as-judge inference during policy optimization. Because reinforcement learning changes policy representations, Search-G1 periodically refits both readouts on trajectories from the latest checkpoint, allowing the reward to co-evolve with the policy. Experiments across multiple search-based question-answering benchmarks and two model scales show that Search-G1 improves the grounding--search-cost trade-off, producing shorter response-side trajectories at competitive task accuracy. Code is available at https://github.com/Rosy0912/Search-G1.

</details>


### [61] [Scaling Inherently Interpretable Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07594)
*Guide Labs Team,Andreas Madsen,Aya Abdelsalam Ismail,Giang Nguyen,Isaac Plant,Muawiz Chaudhary,Nathaniel Monson,Saqib Azim,Zhichen Guo,Julius Adebayo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究突破传统认知，提出将可解释性作为训练流程的核心约束而非事后补救措施。通过Steerling-8B扩散语言模型验证：可解释性与模型能力呈正相关，在更大规模下模型表示更解耦且与人类概念高度对齐；该模型以2-16倍更少计算量实现与先进模型相当的竞争力，并支持无需重训的闭环干预（概念归因→训练数据检索→概念引导修正）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前主流方法将可解释性视为模型能力的代价，通常作为训练后的附加步骤，其可靠性难以保证。本研究挑战这一前提，主张可解释性应成为训练流程的基础性约束条件，与语言建模目标同步优化。

Method: 构建Steerling-8B扩散语言模型（含因果注意力掩码），在训练中直接优化可解释性：① 为生成token组建立输入token/人类概念/训练数据的三重归因机制；② 实现闭环干预系统：通过概念归因诊断输出→检索相似训练样本→利用概念引导无重训修正行为。在跨越三个数量级的计算规模上，对自回归及扩散语言模型进行验证。

Result: ① 可解释性随模型能力同步扩展而非退化；② 大规模下模型表示呈现更强解耦性并与人类认知概念精准对齐；③ Steerling-8B在2-16倍更低计算成本下，性能优于多数开源基准模型。

Conclusion: 本研究证明可解释性可作为训练过程的设计要素并随规模提升，开创了兼顾能力与可解释性的新扩展范式。该发现颠覆'可解释性必然牺牲能力'的传统认知，为构建透明且高性能的语言模型提供新路径。

Abstract: Interpretability is often treated as a tax on capability: language models are trained as opaque systems, then explained after the fact, with methods whose reliability is difficult to establish. In this work, we challenge this premise. Rather than reverse-engineering a model, we make interpretability a constraint of the training pipeline, optimized alongside the language modeling objective. Across three orders of magnitude of compute, on both autoregressive and diffusion language models, interpretability scales with capability rather than against it. Surprisingly, model representations become more disentangled and aligned with human-understandable concepts with scale.
  We instantiate the training-time recipe with Steerling-8B, a diffusion language model with a causal attention mask. For any group of generated tokens, Steerling-8B attributes the output to relevant input tokens, human-understandable concepts, and training data. This enables closed-loop intervention: diagnose an output through its concept or feature attribution, retrieve similar training data, and correct the behavior through concept steering without retraining. Steerling-8B remains competitive with open peer models trained on substantially 2-16x more compute, suggesting a different scaling paradigm: interpretability can be designed into training, and it improves with scale.

</details>


### [62] [Embedding Initialization for Unseen Low-resource Languages in Multilingual NMT: A Case Study on Limbum-English Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07629)
*Samiratu Ntohsi,Neza David Tuyishimire,Anesu Kafesu,Marvin Ogore,Samuel Oluwajunwonlo Babalola,Oche Ankeli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于多个类型学相关语言嵌入平均初始化的方法，用于零样本低资源班图语（Limbum）到英语的神经机器翻译，消除了启发式代理语言选择的需要，性能与最佳单语言代理相当，且显著优于从头训练的模型；但所有系统均未能保留声调变音符号，这是一个待解决的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NLLB-200等多语言神经机器翻译模型覆盖了200种语言，但仍有数千种语言未被支持，包括喀麦隆的大多数Grassfields班图语。在针对未见语言进行微调时，实践者必须选择一个代理语言token，但目前缺乏有原则的选择方法。因此需要一种新的初始化策略来解决这个问题。

Method: 实现了一种嵌入初始化策略，即将一种语言的token设置为模型中多个类型学相关语言嵌入的平均值。使用从新约文本和平行词典中获得的8,837个句子对，评估了Limbum到英语的翻译。比较了NLLB-200零样本、从头训练的Transformer、使用斯瓦希里语代理token微调的NLLB-200以及采用所提出平均嵌入初始化的NLLB-200。

Result: 多语言初始化达到了与最佳单语言代理相当的性能（chrF2++ = 46.7 vs 47.3）。两种NLLB-200变体均比从头训练基线提高了超过32个chrF2++点。然而，所有系统都未能保留声调变音符号。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，在极低资源的班图语翻译中，多语言迁移是主导因素，该方法消除了对启发式代理选择的需要。但是，声调变音符号的保留仍然是一个公开的挑战。数据集和代码已公开以促进进一步研究。

Abstract: Multilingual neural machine translation models such as NLLB-200 cover 200 languages but leave thousands unsupported, including most Grassfields Bantu languages of Cameroon. When fine-tuning these models for an unseen language, practitioners must choose a proxy language token, yet no principled method exists for this selection. We implemented an embedding initialization strategy where a language token is the average of embeddings from multiple typologically related languages already in the mod el. We evaluate this approach on Limbum-to-English translation using a parallel corpus of 8,837 sentence pairs from New Testament text and a bilingual dictionary. We compare models: NLLB-200 zero-shot (chrF2++ = 12.5), a Transformer trained from scratch (chrF2++ = 14.5), NLLB-200 fine-tuned with a Swahili proxy token (chrF2++ = 47.3), and NLLB-200 with our averaged embedding initialization (chrF2++ = 46.7). We find that the multi-language initialization achieves performance comparable to the best single-language proxy. Both NLLB-200 variants improve over the from-scratch baseline by over 32 chrF2++ points. These results show that multilingual transfer is the dominant factor in extremely low-resource Bantu translation while eliminating the need for heuristic proxy selection. However, all systems fail to preserve tonal diacritics, highlighting an open challenge. We make our dataset and code available to support further research.

</details>


### [63] [SurveyReview: A Reviewer-Aligned Benchmark for Survey Evaluators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07641)
*Yuheng Zhang,Yuanchun Wang,Fanjin Zhang,Ruyu Zhao,Juanzi Li,Jie Tang,Jing Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出SurveyReview基准数据集和评估框架，用于评估LLM作为综述论文评审者的能力；开发SurveyAlign基线模型，显著提升与人类评审者的一致性，相比GPT-5.2的提示式判断方法，在四个维度上的MSE从2.28降至1.38，MAE从1.15降至0.69。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型的快速发展，综述写作已实现自动化，但可靠评估成为瓶颈。现有方法主要依赖现成的LLM-as-a-judge方法，缺乏与人类评审者的系统性对齐，且缺乏量化与人类评审者对齐程度的系统性框架。

Method: 构建SurveyReview基准，收集并标注675篇综述论文及1,630份评审报告，将自由格式评论转化为四维度评分（可读性、批判性、全面性、结构性）及支持性理由；制定标准化训练/测试划分和评估协议；开发SurveyAlign基线评估器，使用LoRA微调Qwen3-32B，并融合外部知识增强知识密集型维度。

Result: 在测试集上，SurveyAlign在所有四个维度上都显著提升了与人类评审者的一致性：平均MSE从2.28降至1.38，MAE从1.15降至0.69，优于基于GPT-5.2提示的评审方法。

Conclusion: 贡献包括：(1)建立首个多维度、评审者对齐的数据集及可复现的评估框架；(2)开发强基线评估器，显著提升与人类评审者的对齐程度，为未来研究提供竞争性参考。代码和数据已公开发布。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of large language models has transformed survey writing from a months-long manual effort into an automated process. As generation scales, reliable evaluation becomes the bottleneck, and LLMs are increasingly used as survey evaluators. However, existing approaches largely rely on off-the-shelf LLM-as-a-judge methods without systematic alignment to human reviewers, and there remains a lack of systematic frameworks for quantifying alignment with human reviewers. To address this gap, we propose SurveyReview, a reviewer-aligned, multi-dimensional benchmark and dataset for survey evaluation. We collect and annotate 675 survey papers with 1,630 review reports. We structure authentic peer-review reports by converting free-form comments into four-dimensional scores (Readability, Criticalness, Comprehensiveness, Structure) paired with supporting rationales. We further release standardized train/test splits and an evaluation protocol to measure alignment between automatic evaluators and human reviewers. To validate the benchmark, we develop SurveyAlign, a strong baseline evaluator by fine-tuning Qwen3-32B with LoRA on our annotated data, augmented with external knowledge for knowledge-intensive dimensions. On the test set, SurveyAlign substantially improves reviewer alignment over prompt-based judging with GPT-5.2, reducing average MSE from 2.28 to 1.38 and MAE from 1.15 to 0.69 across all four dimensions. Our contributions are twofold: (1) we establish the first multi-dimensional, reviewer-aligned dataset with a reproducible evaluation framework for survey reviewing; (2) we develop a strong baseline evaluator that substantially improves alignment with human reviewers, providing a competitive reference for future research. Our code and data are available at https://surveyreview.github.io

</details>


### [64] [Evaluating Dedicated Monolingual and Joint Multilingual Causal Models for Dravidian Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07727)
*Venkata Naga Sai Vishnu Rohit Pulipaka*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文对比了达罗毗荼语系（泰米尔语、泰卢固语、卡纳达语、马拉亚拉姆语）的四种单语GPT-2模型与一种多语GPT-2模型的性能。实验表明，单语模型在情感分类和命名实体识别任务上优于多语mGPT模型，且单语分词器在所有测试语言上都比共享的多语分词器更高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 达罗毗荼语系语言在训练多语语言模型的数据中只占很小比例，因此这些模型实际保留的逐语言能力尚不清楚。研究者需要评估单语模型是否比多语模型更适合这些低资源语言。

Method: 研究者从零训练了五个GPT-2架构模型：四个单语模型（分别对应泰米尔语、泰卢固语、卡纳达语和马拉亚拉姆语，各自使用32K词汇的subword分词器）和一个多语模型（四种语言共享64K词汇的subword分词器）。所有模型均在清洗后的CC-100、Wikipedia和Samanantar数据上训练。评估指标包括困惑度、每字节比特数、分词器效率以及微调结果，并与mGPT基线进行比较。

Result: 单语模型在情感分类和命名实体识别任务上优于多语mGPT模型；单语分词器在所有测试语言上都比共享的多语分词器更高效。

Conclusion: 对于达罗毗荼语系等低资源语言，专用单语模型比多语模型性能更优，建议在低资源场景下优先考虑单语模型而非多语共享模型。

Abstract: Dravidian languages, mainly Tamil, Telugu, Kannada, and Malayalam make up only a small part of the data used to train multilingual language models, so it's not clear how much per-language ability these models actually keep. I have trained five GPT-2 architecture models from scratch to compare four monolingual models (one each for Tamil, Telugu, Kannada, and Malayalam, each with its own 32K-vocabulary subword tokenizer) against one multilingual model sharing a 64K-vocabulary subword tokenizer across all four languages. All the 5 models are trained on cleaned CC-100, Wikipedia, and Samanantar data. I have tested the models on perplexity, bits-per-byte, tokenizer efficiency, and fine-tuning results which are compared against mGPT. The monolingual models outperform mGPT on sentiment classification and named entity recognition, and their tokenizers proved more efficient than the shared multilingual model across all the languages tested.

</details>


### [65] [The No-Meaning Falsity: The Structural Impossibility of the Arbitrary Sign in Classical Arabic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07737)
*Elnaserledinellah Mahmoud Abdelwahab*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过建立古典阿拉伯语非拼接形态学的数学模型，证明其词项唯一由词根-图式对生成且语义在派生层面即确定，推翻了后现代语义不确定性及索绪尔任意性假设，并证明阿拉伯语符号动机性显著高于印欧语言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验后现代语言学中"语义完全不确定"的主张及其基础——索绪尔任意性符号公理，是否与古典阿拉伯语的结构特征相容，旨在挑战该理论对自然语言的普适性解释。

Method: 构建阿拉伯语非拼接形态学形式数学模型；提出形态对应定理与语义定位定理；利用条件柯尔莫哥洛夫复杂度将相对任意性形式化为"无规则属性"；进行可判定性分析。

Result: 1) 证明每个词项唯一由词根-图式对生成；2) 证明词义在表层实现前已确定；3) 证得一般相对任意性不可判定；4) 证得阿拉伯语动机性符号（W级和M级）的任意性可判定且严格小于1；5) 确立阿拉伯语比印欧语言具有严格的系统复杂度不对称性。

Conclusion: 古典阿拉伯语的形态结构否定了语义完全不确定性和符号绝对任意性，其动机性符号系统呈现高度规则性，揭示了与印欧语系的本质差异，为索绪尔理论的非普适性提供了形式化证据。

Abstract: This paper investigates whether the postmodern claim of unrestricted semantic indeterminacy, and its foundational Saussurean axiom of the arbitrary sign, are compatible with the structural architecture of Classical Arabic. We develop a formal mathematical model of Arabic non concatenative morphology in which lexical meaning is determined by the interaction between an invariant root and a morphosyntactic pattern. Within this framework, we establish a Morphological Correspondence Theorem, demonstrating that every lexical item is uniquely generated by a root pattern pair, and a Semantic Localization Theorem, proving that lexical meaning is determined at the derivational level prior to surface realization. To address Saussurean weaker notion of relative arbitrariness, we formalize it via conditional Kolmogorov complexity, defining arbitrariness algorithmically as the no rule property. We prove that general relative arbitrariness is formally undecidable, while Arabic relative arbitrariness is decidable and provably less than 1 for its motivated signifiers (Levels W and M), establishing a strict system complexity asymmetry over Indo-European languages.

</details>


### [66] [Jako Tako or Fluent? Presenting PoVisLE: A Polish Vision-Language Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07763)
*Anna Kołos,Grzegorz Statkiewicz,Karolina Seweryn,Katarzyna Kowol,Karolina Piosek,Wojciech Kusa*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PoVisLE 是一个针对波兰语的单一文化视觉语言基准，包含1,117张图像和2,366个人工标注的VQA对，旨在评估超越表面识别的文化基础多模态理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型主要基于以英语为中心的数据训练，导致其在处理特定文化背景的视觉理解时存在局限，无法准确解读地区特异性含义、符号内容及上下文相关视觉线索。现有的文化能力基准多为模板驱动，侧重于表面识别，无法评估文化情境下更深层次的语言和语用理解。

Method: 作者构建了 PoVisLE 基准，包含1,117张图像和2,366个人工标注的视觉问答对，采用基于情境的评估范式，即在视觉语境中解释语言。

Result: 该数据集提供了一个受控且具挑战性的资源，用于评估超越表面识别的文化基础视觉语言理解。

Conclusion: PoVisLE 填补了评估视觉语言模型更深层次文化能力的空白，为文化情境下的多模态理解提供了有价值的基准。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have achieved strong performance on tasks such as image captioning, visual question answering, and image-to-text generation. However, they are predominantly trained on English-centric data, which limits their ability to handle culturally grounded visual understanding and leads to failures in interpreting region-specific meanings, symbolic content, and context-dependent visual cues. Existing benchmarks for cultural competence are often template-driven and focused on surface-level recognition, making them insufficient for evaluating deeper linguistic and pragmatic understanding in culturally situated settings. We introduce PoVisLE, a monocultural vision-language benchmark for Polish designed to evaluate culturally grounded multimodal understanding under a grounded evaluation paradigm, where language is interpreted in interaction with visual context. The dataset contains 1,117 images and 2,366 manually annotated VQA pairs. Overall, our dataset provides a controlled and challenging resource for assessing culturally grounded vision-language understanding beyond surface-level recognition.

</details>


### [67] [On the use of foundation models in cognitive science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07812)
*Raj Sanjay Shah,Alex Warstadt,Michael Frank,Sashank Varma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: A host of recent studies have evaluated the cognitive and developmental alignment of Foundation Models (FMs). These investigations include evaluations of their correspondence to adult performance across a range of cognitive domains, as well as whether aspects of model training track children's cognitive development. However, using FMs as candidate cognitive models poses significant methodological and conceptual challenges. A key question underlies this effort: under what conditions does behavioral alignment justify treating FMs as explanatory models of cognition? In this paper, we articulate a four-stage inferential framework for evaluating FMs as cognitive and developmental models: adapting human experimental tasks to model-compatible formats, specifying linking hypotheses that map model outputs to human measures, evaluating behavioral correspondence, and comparing across candidate models or manipulations. We clarify the role of linking hypotheses in mapping model outputs to human behavioral measures, identify challenges that constrain alignment claims, and propose principles for theory-driven and comparative evaluation. Throughout, we argue that behavioral fit alone is insufficient. Alignment becomes scientifically meaningful only when embedded within explicit theoretical commitments, theory-diagnostic tasks, and systematic contrastive evaluation across candidate models.

</details>


### [68] ["Many Are My Names": The Anatomy of the Assistant and Its Personas via Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07852)
*Adelaide Danilov,Aria Nourbakhsh,Oleksandr Marchenko Breneur,Salima Lamsiyah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过稀疏自编码器特征分析，揭示了语言模型中不同说话者（助手、角色扮演、故事角色）的内部表示机制。核心发现：角色扮演保留助手核心特征但在不同层逐步分化；故事角色缺乏该核心特征；三种模式可通过"沉浸式模拟模式"区分，但助手在默认设置下偶尔也会进入该模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型如何内部表示不同说话者身份（助手、角色扮演角色、故事角色）的研究仍不充分。理解这些表示机制对可控生成和模型行为分析具有重要意义。

Method: 使用用户情感文本及其模型响应数据集，将三种生成设置（助手、角色扮演、故事）分解为在轮次边界和代词标记位置提取的稀疏自编码器特征。通过不同深度的筛选管道选择特征，并通过控制效果和激活分布对每个保留特征进行表征。

Result: 主要发现：助手和角色扮演不是独立的替代方案，角色扮演保留助手关联的核心特征，同时在网络层间从操作性机制向行为性和风格性特征逐步分化。生成的故事角色缺乏助手关联的核心特征。故事和角色扮演都可以通过沉浸式模拟模式与助手区分，但助手有时即使默认设置下也会进入或缓慢漂移至该模式。

Conclusion: 语言模型中不同说话者的内部表示并非完全独立。角色扮演与助手共享核心表示但在不同深度分化，而故事角色采用完全不同的表示策略。"沉浸式模拟模式"作为区分概念具有解释力，但助手与角色扮演的边界并非绝对清晰。

Abstract: How a language model internally represents who is speaking, the Assistant, an assigned roleplay persona, or a narrated story character, remains underexplored. We study speaker representations using a dataset of user-expressed emotional text and corresponding model responses. We decompose three generation settings (Assistant, Roleplay, and Story) into sparse autoencoder features extracted at turn-boundary and pronoun-token positions and selected through a filtering pipeline for different depths. We characterize each surviving feature through its steering effects and activation distribution. Our main finding is that the Assistant and roleplay personas are not independent alternatives: personas retain the Assistant-associated feature core while progressively differentiating from it across layers, starting from operational machinery towards behavioral and stylistic features. Meanwhile, generated story characters lack the Assistant-associated core. Both Story and Roleplay can be distinguished from the Assistant with Immersive Simulation Mode.
