Its Run Panorama separates immutable events, deterministic relations, inferred diagnoses, and controlled outcomes with four evidence grades; optional trace import and OTLP/HTTP export support existing observability deployments.
  Across six frozen repository profiles, three coding agents, and seven clean or fault-injected conditions, all 126 executions preserve source worktrees and each correlates to exactly one source session. Yet adapters expose three distinct semantics: no Skill runs; complete runs but no failure-like events; or failure-like events in every operational-failure and clean session. In a seven-template diagnostic study, semantic aliases and Panorama localize the same six non-clean boundaries but differ in exact/status behavior; both Raw views emit a failure status on all 18 clean cases, while Panorama emits none. A known-rule graph conforms to 126/126 frozen contracts, whereas a second model completes only 228/378 calls. These observations motivate executable adapter qualification and show that event presence is not boundary fidelity, composite exact scores mask distinct errors, and model explanations must not overwrite deterministic facts.

</details>


### [103] [Instability of LLM Pre-Pretraining: It Doesn't Always Help. An Investigation on Multiple Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08800)
*Sofiia Riazhskykh,Nam Luu,Ondřej Bojar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文验证了在人工语言上预训练LLMs（“预预训练”）是否能提升token效率，发现增益依赖于实验设置和随机种子，但在大多数语言中，使用Llama分词器对小模型进行128-Dyck预训练可稳定提升效率，建议多次训练以避免采用不稳定方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证先前报告的预预训练可提升33% token效率的结果，并探究其跨语言泛化性，同时分析语言学特征与效率增益的关系，以推动更可靠的预训练方法。

Method: 在四个语系的多语言上，使用两种分词器和不同模型规模，评估预预训练（特别是128-Dyck语言）的token效率，并量化语言学属性（句长、形态丰富度、依存句法树特征）与效率的关系，进行多次训练以检查稳定性。

Result: 增益强烈依赖实验设置和随机种子；但在多数语言中，使用Llama分词器对小模型进行128-Dyck预训练可稳定提升token效率；语言学属性与效率增益存在关联。

Conclusion: 预预训练的token效率提升并非普适，需谨慎选择实验设置；建议进行多次训练以验证方法稳定性，避免社区采用不稳定的预训练技术。

Abstract: Pretraining LLMs on artificial languages ("pre-pretraining") is a technique that could reportedly increase token efficiency by 33%, i.e., save up to 33% of training tokens needed to reach a certain performance. We validate this prior result for English on a larger set of natural languages across four language families, using two different tokenizers and varying model sizes. We also relate the observed gains (or losses) in token efficiency to quantified linguistic properties of the languages, such as sentence length, morphological richness, and features of dependency syntactic trees (tree depth, number of children, number of crossing dependencies). Our empirical results indicate that the reported gains depend heavily on the experiment setup and the choice of random seed, although we can confirm the trend of stable gains with 128-Dyck pretraining of small models with the Llama tokenizer for most of the examined languages. On a general note, we argue that multiple training runs should be carried out at least for a subset of experiments to avoid the community adopting unstable approaches.

</details>


### [104] [IDRAAK: From Multi-Agent NLP to Few-Shot Prompting for Semantic Drift Detection in Technical Requirements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08801)
*Shiva Ahir*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出IDRAAK框架，通过语言无关的语义需求表示(SRR)检测技术需求翻译中的语义漂移。实验表明，简单的LLM few-shot提示(单次调用+6个示例)在300个需求/890个扰动上达到MCC=0.888和F1=0.983，性能优于结构化方法，证明增加智能体复杂性不一定能提升语义漂移检测效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 技术需求跨语言翻译会引入语义漂移，改变数值约束、极性、模态等关键规范含义，因此需要开发有效的语义漂移检测方法。

Method: 提出IDRAAK框架，使用语言无关的语义需求表示(SRR)，评估六种检测工作流，包括确定性比较、多智能体验证和少样本提示等方法。

Result: 在890个合成扰动(300个需求，10个工程领域)上，单次LLM调用+6个few-shot示例达到MCC=0.888和F1=0.983，优于结构化方法。在PAWS-X和XNLI数据集上进一步验证，确定性SRR比较对技术需求表现良好(F1=0.898)，但对通用文本表现差(F1=0.012)，结构化证据能改善对抗性改写性能，事后Platt缩放提升置信度校准。

Conclusion: 增加智能体复杂性不一定能提升语义漂移检测性能，简单的少样本提示可提供强大且高效的替代方案。不同方法在不同领域(技术vs通用)表现出互补的优劣势，结构化方法对技术需求更有效，而LLM方法在通用文本上更适应。

Abstract: Translating technical requirements across languages can introduce semantic drift, altering numerical constraints, polarities, modalities, or other specification-critical meaning. IDRAAK is presented as an interpretable framework for detecting such drift using a language-independent Semantic Requirement Representation (SRR), with six detection workflows evaluated, ranging from deterministic comparison to multi-agent verification and few-shot prompting. On 890 synthetic perturbations across 300 requirements from 10 engineering domains, a single LLM call with six few-shot examples achieves MCC=0.888 and F1=0.983, outperforming the evaluated structured and multi-stage alternatives. Further evaluation on PAWS-X (805 pairs, 5 languages) and XNLI (700 pairs, 7 languages) exposes complementary strengths and limitations of structured and LLM-based approaches. Deterministic SRR comparison performs strongly on technical requirements (F1=0.898) but poorly on general-domain text (F1=0.012), while structured evidence improves performance on adversarial paraphrases. Post-hoc Platt scaling further improves confidence calibration. The results demonstrate that increased agentic complexity does not necessarily improve semantic-drift detection and that simple few-shot prompting can provide a strong and efficient alternative.

</details>


### [105] [Tevatron-Elastic: A Unified Abstraction for Training Elastic Retrievers and Rerankers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08809)
*Yu Wang,Shengyao Zhuang,Xueguang Ma,Zongyu Wu,Jimmy Lin,Vivek Srikumar,Zhichao Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出统一框架将三种模型压缩方法（减少层数、减少上层tokens、缩短embedding）整合为单一抽象，训练一个checkpoint即可支持所有尺寸，在检索系统中实现弹性部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产检索系统面临单一模型规模难以满足多样化需求的问题：不同场景对速度、索引大小要求不同，且最优权衡随工作负载变化。现有三种模型缩小方法各自独立研究，难以组合或适配新模型。

Method: 设计统一抽象框架，通过单个对象命名模型可运行的任意尺寸，用简短计划列出训练尺寸。训练产生单一checkpoint服务所有尺寸，部署时用户自由选择。该抽象覆盖检索器和重排序器，通过Hugging Face transformers接口实现，新backbone仅需配置更改。使Matryoshka嵌入、早退出、2D Matryoshka等方法成为特例，并支持Matryoshka LTC联合训练多种token压缩比率。

Result: 在三个backbone和两个任务上训练20个checkpoint，质量曲线平滑，单一checkpoint成本略高于单尺寸模型，对照实验确认了加速效果。

Conclusion: 统一框架成功整合多种模型压缩策略，实现弹性检索系统构建，并开源框架和所有checkpoint作为资源。

Abstract: A single model scale challenges the flexibility of a production retrieval system: some settings need it faster, others need a smaller index, and the right trade-off changes with the workload. In the context of information retrieval (IR), a transformer-based model can be made smaller in three ways---using fewer layers, passing fewer tokens through the upper layers, or producing a shorter embedding---and each way saves a different compute resource. These options have been studied one at a time, each as its own method with its own code and training setup, which makes them hard to combine or adapt to a new model. We present~\ours to bring all three under one simple abstraction: a single object names any size the model can run at, and a short schedule lists the sizes to train. Training then produces one checkpoint that serves all of those sizes, and at deployment the user picks any of them. The same abstraction covers both retrievers and rerankers and both encoder and decoder models, as it works through interfaces that Hugging Face transformers already expose; a new backbone is a configuration change, not new modeling code. Prior methods---Matryoshka embeddings, early exit, 2D~Matryoshka (e.g., Starbucks), and layerwise token compression---become special cases of our unified abstraction. The same interface also enables Matryoshka~LTC (MLTC), which jointly trains several token-compression ratios in one retriever checkpoint. To validate our framework, we train 20 checkpoints across three backbones and two tasks: the quality curves are smooth, one checkpoint costs little over a model trained for a single size, and a controlled study confirms the wallclock speedups. We release the framework and all checkpoints as a resource for building elastic retrieval systems.

</details>


### [106] [Deployable Per-Instance Multi-Layer Activation Steering for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08829)
*Muhammad Faishal Adly Nelwan,Alfan Farizki Wicaksono*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 当前激活引导方法固定全局注入层，但最佳层应为实例级决策。本文提出可部署的逐实例多层选择方案，在无需推理时标签的情况下，通过提示嵌入驱动的层排序器、方向分类器及自适应门控机制，实现接近Oracle效果的性能提升，同时避免过强全局选择导致的流畅性崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有激活引导技术对冻结语言模型的行为编辑依赖固定的全局层注入策略，但作者认为最优注入层应随输入实例动态变化。由于当前方法无法在部署阶段获取真实答案进行层评估，亟需一种无需标签、可部署且能恢复逐实例优化优势的解决方案。

Method: 设计包含三个核心组件的部署方案：(1) 基于提示嵌入的逐实例层排序器替代需真实答案的贪心规则；(2) 推断引导方向分类器；(3) 自适应门控机制，通过比较短步长引导结果与推断方向，动态确定最少必要引导层数。该方法以可训练的预测器复现离线贪心规则的目标。

Result: 在两个8B参数开权模型及六种二元人格特质测试中：(1) 部署方案恢复大部分Oracle性能提升（强模型主体效果/难模型显著多数）；(2) 所有特质-模型对平均未低于未引导基线；(3) 有效规避高引导层数下的全局选择引发的流畅性崩溃，并通过'方向优于幅度'机制解释行为翻转与输出崩溃现象。

Conclusion: 逐实例多层选择显著优于固定全局层策略，所提部署方案在保证性能的同时实现实际可用性，其机制分析揭示了引导效果的关键边界条件，为激活引导技术的工程化提供新范式。

Abstract: Activation steering edits the behaviour of a frozen language model by adding a learned vector to its residual stream, and current practice fixes the injection layers globally per task. We argue that the best layers are an instance-level decision, and we make per-instance, multi-layer selection both well understood and deployable. On two open-weight 8B models and six binary persona traits, a per-instance oracle over layer subsets shows that the best layers vary from one input to the next: on most trait-model pairs, no fixed global layer set recovers the per-instance benefit. A greedy rule that ranks layers by single-layer marginal effect recovers nearly all of the oracle's benefit, but both must score candidate layers against the gold answer, so neither can run at deployment; the rule instead becomes the target a prompt-only predictor is trained to reproduce. Our deployable recipe needs no label at inference: a per-instance layer ranker read off the prompt embedding, a classifier that infers the steering direction, and an adaptive gate that scores short steered passes against that inferred direction and steers no more layers than necessary. The recipe recovers most of the oracle's lift (the bulk on the stronger model, a clear majority on the harder one), never drives any trait-model pair below its unsteered alignment baseline on average, and largely avoids the fluency collapse that strong global selection incurs at higher layer counts. A mechanistic account, "direction over magnitude", explains the behavioural flip under a mis-directed global set, the output collapse from steering too many layers, and the ceiling of unsteerable inputs.

</details>


### [107] [Explicit Boundary Markers for Subword Vocabularies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08847)
*Sander Land,Clara Meister*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出用显式词边界标记替代传统空格约定，解决子词分词器中因空格和大小写导致的词汇重复问题，虽然未显著提升压缩率，但能改善语言建模性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有子词分词器在处理空格书写系统时，会将同一单词以带前导空格和不带前导空格的形式重复表示，导致嵌入向量分裂并独立训练。大小写变体进一步将单词分割为多达六种形式。这种重复导致数据分散在不同行中训练，影响模型性能。

Method: 引入显式词边界标记替代标准空格约定：用边界标记分隔单词，词间空格用标记对表示；通过两个移位码处理首字母大写和全大写情况，使单词的一种内部表示可在不同设置中复用。

Result: 采用新约定后，词汇重复问题得到缓解，但分词压缩率未提升（六种语言平均每标记字符数与基线相差1%以内）；下游语言建模性能在所有测试标记方案中均优于基线（比特/字节更低）。

Conclusion: 词汇重复会带来压缩率指标无法捕捉的性能成本，因此需要显式边界标记来统一单词表示，从而提升模型效果。

Abstract: Subword tokenizers represent many common words twice in space-using writing systems, once with a leading space and once without. The two entries have separate embeddings in models, so occurrences of one word are divided across rows that are trained independently, and the two forms need not even segment the string the same way: " together" may be a single entry while the same word without a preceding space is tokenized as "to|gether". Capitalization divides a word further, into as many as six forms. We introduce an alternative to standard whitespace conventions using an explicit word boundary marker, which prevents such duplication. Words are delimited by the boundary markers, and spaces between words are represented as pairs of such markers. Two shift codes do the same for title case and upper case, allowing one internal representation of a word to be re-used across different settings. Switching to this convention mitigates the duplicate-entry issue, but does not improve tokenization compression: for both vocabulary-learning algorithms, the best marker scheme stays within one percent of the baseline in characters per token, averaged across six languages. It does result in better language modeling performance. Every marker scheme tested downstream reaches lower bits per byte than the baseline, suggesting that duplication carries a cost that compression does not capture.

</details>


### [108] [Conversation as Measurement in Clinical Encounters: Observable Phase Structure, Partially Observable Patient State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08868)
*Lily Chen,Ted Mau,Michael Gensheimer,Brian Anthony Nuyen,Nancy Jiang,James Zou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探讨对话转录本的"可观测性"——即目标信息是否能仅从对话记录中恢复。通过在439个真实临床对话（134小时）中使用患者报告结局测量(PROM)作为外部锚点，研究发现存在"可观测性不对称"：对话阶段结构是可观测的且对描述临床会话组织有用，而患者状态则仅部分可观测，即使在旨在引出患者症状的临床设置中也是如此。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多现代AI系统分析对话痕迹以推断人类互动和状态，隐含地假设此类信息可从对话中恢复。然而，"可观测性"——目标是否能仅从对话转录本中恢复——难以评估，因为转录本可能只提供了许多目标的部分视图，且大规模分析需要基于模型的标注，使得对话信号的真实限制与标注错误难以区分。研究需要找到一个有外部验证锚点且遵循结构化模式的领域来测试这一假设。

Method: 研究聚焦于临床遭遇场景，其中患者报告结局测量(PROMs)为患者状态提供外部锚点，且访问遵循广泛的结构化模式。数据集包括439个真实临床对话转录本（134小时），其中245个ENT转录本与273份PROM调查配对。患者状态通过声音、咳嗽和吞咽的PROM评分进行操作化；阶段结构通过对话阶段分割进行操作化。为在大规模上使分析可信，使用符合PHI的GPT-5部署进行转录本标注，并进行40小时的人工验证，以降低表观可观测性限制仅反映标注错误的风险。

Result: 核心发现是"可观测性不对称"：阶段结构是可观测的，对描述临床遭遇组织有用；而患者状态仅部分可观测，即使在旨在引出患者症状和体验的设置中也是如此。这表明对话转录本对某些目标（如对话流程）的恢复效果良好，但对患者主观状态等深层目标的恢复能力有限。

Conclusion: 研究警示不应仅凭转录本推断人类状态。尽管对话转录本对于恢复对话阶段结构等表面组织信息是有效的，但它们对恢复患者的深层主观状态（如症状严重程度）能力有限。这一发现对依赖对话转录本进行状态推断的AI系统设计具有重要启示：需要结合多模态数据源或外部验证机制，而不能仅依赖文本对话记录。

Abstract: Many modern AI systems analyze conversational traces to infer aspects of human interaction and state, implicitly assuming that such information is recoverable from conversation. We study observability: whether a target is recoverable from conversational transcripts alone. Observability is difficult to assess because transcripts may provide only a partial view of many targets, and large-scale analysis requires model-based annotation, making true limits of the conversational signal hard to distinguish from annotator error. We therefore study clinical encounters, where patient-reported outcome measures (PROMs) provide an external anchor for patient state, and visits follow broadly structured patterns. We study observability of patient state and conversational phase structure using 439 real-world clinical encounter transcripts spanning 134 hours, including 245 ENT transcripts paired with 273 PROM surveys. We operationalize patient state using PROM scores for voice, cough, and swallowing; phase structure using conversational phase segmentation. To make these analyses credible at scale, we use a PHI-compliant GPT-5 deployment for transcript annotation and conduct 40 hours of manual validation, reducing the risk that apparent limits of observability simply reflect annotator error. Our core finding is an observability asymmetry: phase structure is observable and useful for characterizing clinical encounter organization, while patient state is only partially observable, even in a setting designed to elicit patient symptoms and experiences, cautioning against transcript-only inference of human state.

</details>


### [109] [Position Bias in Ordinal Classification: A Systematic Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08869)
*Yu Wang,Jeffrey Zhou,Menglin Liu,Ge Shi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究系统分析了大型语言模型在序数分类任务中的位置偏差问题，发现标签顺序、示例顺序和示例位置均会影响模型预测。通过对10个前沿LLM在5个数据集上的实验，结果表明位置偏差是普遍存在的，现有校正方法效果有限，基于比较的列表式推断提供了最佳平衡。研究强调序数分类系统的鲁棒性取决于完整系统配置而非单一模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型越来越多地用于序数分类任务，但研究发现语义等价的提示组织方式改变会导致预测结果变化。这种位置偏差现象缺乏系统性的深入分析，特别是对于位置偏差的普遍性、影响因素和有效解决方案等方面理解不足。研究者旨在通过系统性实验刻画标签顺序、示例顺序和示例位置这三个位置偏差来源，为构建更稳健的序数分类系统提供指导。

Method: 研究采用三步实验方法：(1)对10个前沿LLM在通用序数分类任务上应用三个探测实验，检验标签顺序、示例顺序和示例位置的位置偏差；(2)在5个数据集上变化8个提示级、任务级和模型级因素，分析准确率与稳定性的关系；(3)比较逐点、成对和列表式推断，以及替代聚合和去偏方法。实验设计系统全面，涵盖了模型、任务、提示等多个维度的影响因素。

Result: 实验发现：(1)所有10个模型均对三种位置偏差来源敏感，证明问题具有普遍性；(2)准确率与稳定性往往不一致，仅较低规模基数能同时提升两者；(3)测试的校正方法无法提供可靠解决方案，基于比较的列表式表述提供最佳平衡但跨模型和偏差来源的迁移效果不均匀。位置鲁棒性取决于完整系统配置而非单一模型。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了LLM在序数分类中的位置偏差是一个系统性问题，需要同时考虑模型选择、提示设计和推断策略。建议序数分类系统应联合选择预测性能和稳定性，而非仅关注单一指标。这些发现对构建实际应用的序数分类系统具有重要指导意义，强调了系统级配置优化而非单一模型优化的重要性。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used for ordinal classification, yet semantically equivalent changes to prompt organization can alter their predictions. We conduct systematic experiments to characterize positional bias from label order, demonstration order, and demonstration placement. First, we apply the three probes to ten frontier LLMs on a common ordinal-classification task; every model is sensitive to all three positional sources, showing that the problem is pervasive. Second, we vary eight prompt-, task-, and model-level factors across five datasets; accuracy and stability are often misaligned, and only lower scale cardinality consistently improves both. Third, we compare pointwise, pairwise, and listwise inference, alternative aggregation and debiasing methods, and joint configurations; the tested corrections do not provide a reliable remedy, while a comparison-based listwise formulation offers the best balance but transfers unevenly across models and bias sources. These findings show that positional robustness depends on the full system configuration rather than the model alone. Ordinal-classification systems should therefore be selected jointly for predictive performance and stability.

</details>


### [110] [Tied Trit-Planes: Constraining PTQTP to a Uniform Nine-Level Quantizer, with a Persistent Folded Format for Disk-Streamed Mixture-of-Experts Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08910)
*Matteo Grella*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本论文提出了一种新颖的LLM权重量化方法PTQTP，通过将权重分解为三进制平面并约束为平衡三进制恒等式，实现统一的9级量化与4位代码平面表示。在DeepSeek-V4-Flash-0731模型上应用，结果显示其匹配官方API性能，在MMLU上得分86，解码速度快6.7%，文件体积小9%，相关代码已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 首次将平衡三进制恒等式作为约束引入PTQTP求解器，旨在创建一种新颖的、高效的统一量化方案，以解决LLM权重表示与传输效率问题。

Method: PTQTP技术将LLM权重矩阵分解为两个三进制平面，通过固定比例（3:1）的标量约束将其合并为一个统一的9级量化器，随后无损折叠为4位代码平面作为持久化表示，并集成CPU-SIMD内核与SSD专家流式传输，在64GB笔记本电脑上从MXFP4专家权重单次量化实现。

Result: 在DeepSeek-V4-Flash-0731的5/5基准测试和12/14延续步骤中匹配官方API，MMLU子集得分86，解码速度快6.7%，文件体积小9%，未检测到保真度差异；但代理指标（权重重构误差、困惑度）与参考保真度存在分离现象。

Conclusion: 该研究实现了一种基于平衡三进制恒等式约束的高效LLM权重量化方案，在DeepSeek-V4-Flash-0731模型上验证了其在保持保真度的同时，显著提升解码速度（快6.7%）并减小文件体积（小9%），相关代码与格式已开源。

Abstract: PTQTP decomposes LLM weight matrices into two ternary (trit) planes with two free per-group scales. Tying the scales to a fixed ratio of three collapses the decomposition into a single uniform nine-level quantizer, a known balanced-ternary identity. To our knowledge, at the time of writing, this work is the first to impose that identity as a constraint inside PTQTP's solver. The two trit planes then fold losslessly into one 4-bit code plane that we make the persistent serving representation: disk bytes, expert-cache bytes, and kernel input are the same 4.0625-bits/weight blocks, consumed in one integer dot pass. For this conjunction (ratio-3 nine-level code, CPU-SIMD kernels, SSD expert streaming, identical persistent bytes) we likewise found no precedent. We apply this to the routed experts of DeepSeek-V4-Flash-0731, a 284B-A13B mixture-of-experts model, quantizing in one shot from the released MXFP4 expert weights and streaming experts from SSD on a 64 GB laptop. Against a 4.5-bit Q4_K baseline, measured one process per fixture with an expert-lossless anchor arm as reference control, the tied model matches the official serving API on 5/5 fixtures at step 0 (Q4_K: 4/5) and 12/14 captured continuation steps (11/14), scores 86 vs. 84 on a 100-item MMLU subset, decodes 6.7% faster in decode phase, and ships 9% smaller files: no detected fidelity difference at these small evaluation sizes, and every fixture-level difference between the arms traces to a single measured near-tie cell. The tied fit nevertheless shows higher weight-reconstruction error and worse perplexity, a measured dissociation between proxy metrics and reference fidelity. A cumulative trunk-ternarization ladder and bitwise-pinned aarch64/x86-64 kernels complete the report. All code, formats, and evaluation artifacts are open source in the fucina inference stack.

</details>


### [111] [Investigating Multimodal Informativity under Different Partner Visibility Conditions in Video-Mediated Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08915)
*Esam Ghaleb,Hugh Mee Wong,Kristina Kobrock*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了手势与语言在多人对话中的多模态信息传递，发现手势本身具有预测指代对象的能力，多模态融合在手写文本不确定时最有益，并与人类交互数据对比验证了视觉可见性对话语产生的语用效应以及重复交互中的言语和多模态适应效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 情境化语言使用是多模态且具身性的，手势可携带语音信号中缺失或不充分的信息，然而现有对话模型通常仅依赖文字转录。研究动机在于探究在不同伙伴可见性条件下，手势及其与语言组合在多人对话中携带多少指代信息。

Method: 构建基于视频中介指代通信游戏的模型，识别预期指代对象，使用语音转录、手势骨骼表示或两者模态组合。此外，训练学习型表征与指代图像的对齐以提升融合模型性能，并与人类交互数据进行比较分析。

Result: 手势本身即可预测预期指代对象；多模态融合在转录模型不确定时最为有益；训练阶段表征对齐进一步提升了融合模型性能。与人类交互数据比较发现： interlocutor可见性对手势产生和 informativeness 有语用效应；言语和多模态（非手势）性能在重复交互轮次中呈现适应效应。

Conclusion: 本研究对多人对话中多模态信息的技术建模和通过训练模型表征分析人类交互数据做出了贡献，揭示了手势作为独立信息源的预测能力，以及模态融合在不确定情境下的优势。

Abstract: Situated language use is multimodal and embodied. For example, gestures can carry information that is absent or underspecified in the speech signal, yet dialogue models typically rely on transcripts alone. We study how much referential information gestures and their combination with speech carry in multimodal dialogue under different partner visibility conditions. % We build models that identify the intended referent in a video-mediated referential communication game based on either the speech transcript, the skeletal representation of gesture, or both modalities. Our results show that gesture alone is predictive of the intended referent and that multimodal fusion is most beneficial when the transcript-based model is uncertain. Training-only alignment of learned representations with the referent image further improves the fusion model performance. % In a comparison with human interaction data, we further see pragmatic effects of interlocutor visibility on gesture production and informativeness as well as an entrainment effect in speech and multimodal, but not gesture, performance across rounds of repeated interaction. We thus make contributions to the technical modelling of multimodal information in human dialogue and the analysis of human interaction data via trained model representations.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [112] [Generalizing deep reinforcement learning across cable-driven parallel robot configurations with actuator-level policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07546)
*Abir Bouaouda,Mohamed Boutayeb,François Charpillet,Dominique Martinez,Rémi Pannequin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于深度强化学习的电缆驱动并联机器人（CDPR）控制新方法，通过训练执行器级策略（ALP），实现了对任意配置机器人的控制，无需正向运动学，且能成功从仿真迁移到真实机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电缆驱动并联机器人（CDPR）具有多样配置和复杂的控制挑战，传统深度强化学习方法训练时间长且难以泛化到不同配置或执行器数量，因此需要一种配置无关、高效且通用的控制策略。

Method: 本文提出了一种新的深度强化学习方法，训练执行器级策略（ALP），控制每个电机以达到目标电缆长度，而非控制整个机器人到达期望末端位置。该方法依赖逆运动学，避免正向运动学难题，训练在仿真中进行，并迁移到真实机器人。

Result: 实验结果表明，执行器级策略在鲁棒性和精度上均优于传统强化学习方法。使用在4电机平面CDPR仿真中训练的策略，成功控制了一个8电机CDPR的3D运动，证明该方法可适用于任意CDPR配置，与执行器数量或位置无关。

Conclusion: 本文首次将深度强化学习应用于电缆驱动并联机器人的执行器级控制，提出了一种配置无关的通用策略，具有高效、鲁棒、精确的特点，能够适应不同执行器数量和放置位置的机器人。

Abstract: Cable-driven parallel robots (CDPRs) present diverse configurations and complex control challenges, which can be addressed by deep reinforcement learning (DRL) by learning their nonlinear dynamics. However, DRL methods often require extensive training time, and the resulting policies do not generalize well to different robot configurations or varying numbers of actuators. In this article, we introduce a novel DRL approach for controlling CDPRs that does not depend on the specific robot configuration. Our method trains an actuator-level policy that controls each motor to achieve its target cable length, in contrast to conventional DRL approaches that learn to control the entire robot to reach a desired end-effector position. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to apply DRL to control CDPRs using an actuator-level policy. This approach offers two main advantages: (i) a single shared policy can be applied to any CDPR configuration, regardless of actuator count, and (ii) reliance on inverse kinematics, avoiding the more challenging forward kinematics problem. Training is performed in simulation, and the learned policy is successfully transferred to a real CDPR. Experimental results show that the actuator-level policy (ALP) surpasses traditional reinforcement learning methods in both robustness and precision. We further control a real 8-motor CDPR with 3D motion using a policy trained on a simulated 4-motor planar CDPR operating in 2D. This illustrates that the proposed method is applicable to any CDPR configuration, independent of actuator number or placement.

</details>


### [113] [SC$^{2}$-WM: A Self-Correcting World Model with Closed-Loop Feedback for Vision-and-Language Navigation in Continuous Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07548)
*Xuan Yao,Yuze Zhu,Junyu Gao,Zongmeng Wang,Changsheng Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SC²-WM框架解决VLN-CE中开环执行的状态漂移问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言导航（VLN-CE）需要智能体在部分可观测环境下进行细粒度导航决策，但现有方法普遍采用开环执行模式，缺乏检测和纠正推理过程中内部状态漂移的机制，导致导航鲁棒性不足。

Method: 设计SC²-WM自校正世界模型框架，通过世界模型预见性生成内部反馈实现闭环决策；在行动执行前进行状态级计划细化；引入条件性世界感知适应机制，在测试时根据反馈信号选择性更新世界模型以应对模型容量不足场景。

Result: 在标准VLN-CE基准测试中，该方法显著提升了导航任务的鲁棒性与泛化能力，实验验证了自校正机制的有效性。

Conclusion: SC²-WM通过引入内部反馈闭环和自适应更新机制，有效解决了连续环境中视觉语言导航的开环执行缺陷，为部分可观测场景下的智能导航提供了新范式。

Abstract: Vision-and-Language Navigation in Continuous Environments (VLN-CE) requires agents to make fine-grained navigation decisions under partial observability. However, most existing methods rely on open-loop execution, lacking mechanisms to detect and correct internal state drift during inference. We propose SC$^{2}$-WM, a self-correcting world model framework that introduces internal feedback for closed-loop decision making in VLN-CE. Our method derives feedback from world-model foresight to perform state-level plan refinement before action execution. To handle challenging scenarios, we further introduce conditional world-aware adaptation, which enables model-level correction by selectively updating the world model at test time when feedback indicates model capacity insufficiency. Experiments on standard VLN-CE benchmarks demonstrate improved navigation robustness and generalization. Our code is available at https://github.com/sunrise-ikun/SC2_WM.

</details>


### [114] [Self Supervised Learning from Automatically Generated Demonstrations for Visual Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07553)
*Andres Rivas,Anselmo R. Cukla,Rodrigo S. Guerra,Bruna V. Guterres,Ricardo B. Grando*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自监督视觉操作方法，机器人自动生成目标位姿周围的示教数据，从腕部RGB图像直接学习相对位姿校正，无需显式相机-机器人外参标定。采用ROS2和Isaac Sim构建管线，分别生成平面精修和粗略3D接近的数据集，使用卷积网络从单帧RGB回归相对平移和旋转。执行时采用从粗到细的控制策略：先用包含高度变化的模型接近物体，再用平面数据精修对齐。仿真中精修阶段将平面离散度从9.69mm降至5.38mm；真实实验中在UR5e机器人上对三个物体进行端到端抓取尝试，无旋转情况下两个物体成功率分别为66.6%和63.6%，旋转条件下保持部分鲁棒性。结果表明自动生成示教数据能以有限设置支持实用视觉操作，但仍面临深度预测和物体依赖泛化挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作通常需要对象特定的编程、人工数据标注或校准的感知流水线，限制了实际场景中的快速部署。虽然从示教中学习提供了更直接的替代方案，但收集示教仍需要人工遥操作或运动觉教学。本文旨在解决这些限制，提出一种能够自动收集数据并学习的自监督方法。

Method: 本文提出一种自监督视觉操作方法，核心创新在于：(1)机器人围绕目标位姿自动生成示教数据，从腕部安装的RGB图像直接学习相对位姿校正；(2)使用ROS2和Isaac Sim构建数据采集管线，无需显式相机到机器人的外参标定，自动收集带标签的图像-位姿对；(3)为平面精修和粗略3D接近分别生成独立数据集；(4)训练卷积网络从单帧RGB观测回归相对平移和旋转；(5)执行时采用从粗到细的控制策略，先用含高度变化的模型接近物体，再用平面数据精修最终对齐。

Result: 实验在仿真和真实环境中验证。仿真结果：精修阶段将最终平面离散度从9.69mm降至5.38mm。真实实验：在配备夹爪和单目相机的UR5e协作机器人上进行端到端抓取尝试，对三个物理物体进行测试。无旋转条件下两个物体成功率分别为66.6%和63.6%，在旋转条件下仍保持部分鲁棒性。实验验证了自动生成示教数据的可行性，同时揭示了深度预测和物体依赖泛化的剩余挑战。

Conclusion: 本研究证明了通过自动生成示教数据，机器人可以在有限的设置努力下实现实用的视觉操作。自监督方法减少了对外部标定和人工标注的依赖，提升了部署效率。然而，深度预测准确性和物体依赖泛化仍是关键挑战，未来工作需在这些方向进一步改进。该方法为快速部署的机器人视觉操作提供了新方向，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation often requires object specific programming, manual data annotation, or calibrated perception pipelines, which limits rapid deployment in practical settings. Learning from demonstration offers a more direct alternative, but collecting demonstrations can still demand human teleoperation or kinesthetic teaching. This paper presents a self supervised visual manipulation method in which a robot automatically generates demonstrations around a target pose and learns relative pose corrections directly from wrist mounted RGB images. The proposed pipeline uses ROS~2 and Isaac Sim to collect labeled image-pose pairs without requiring explicit camera to robot extrinsic calibration. Separate datasets are generated for planar refinement and coarse three dimensional approach, and a convolutional network is trained to regress relative translation and rotation from single frame RGB observations. During execution, a coarse to fine controller first approaches the object using models trained with height variation and then refines the final alignment using planar data. The method is evaluated both in simulation and on a real UR5e collaborative robot equipped with a gripper and a monocular camera. In simulation, the refinement stage reduces the final planar dispersion from 9.69 mm to 5.38 mm. In real world experiments, the system performs end to end grasp attempts on three physical objects and reaches success rates of 66.6% and 63.6% for two objects without object rotation, while still maintaining partial robustness under rotated conditions. These results show that automatically generated demonstrations can support practical visual manipulation with limited setup effort, while also exposing remaining challenges in depth prediction and object dependent generalization.

</details>


### [115] [You Don't Need To Stay in The Loop: An Agentic Robotics Loop for Robot-Policy Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07555)
*Hang Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AgenticRobotics 是一个用于机器人策略改进的控制平面，通过证据驱动升级、崩溃恢复与篡改检测，实现低错误升级率的操作可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码智能体已实现软件闭环，但机器人工具（策略、训练管道、数据收集）频繁失败，需将其架构移植到机器人策略改进，并强调工具质量测量与过期机制。

Method: 提出 AgenticRobotics，一个后端无关的控制平面，其中 LLM 控制器驱动一次性工作器执行持久的训练-评估-改进事务；核心组件包括不可变目标、控制器持有的测量、基于提交键的崩溃恢复、证据分级技能库以及具有标准化记录调用接口的工具注册表。

Result: 实验表明，该系统的闸门机制显著降低错误升级率（每运行 0.001 次硬化 vs 0.005-0.021 次已部署），支持任意时刻有效决策与可选停止，抵抗杀死注入无丢失或重复效应，并通过签名验证器检测六种工件篡改类别。

Conclusion: 该系统实现操作自治，使操作者可离开，因为升级由证据控制、状态可恢复、能力质量源自记录；并非选择比人类更优的检查点（在测量谱系上并未如此），而是专注于可靠操作。

Abstract: Coding agents such as Claude Code and Codex close the software loop: a main agent manages the loop, subagents analyze and execute, tools do the work. We port this architecture to robot-policy improvement, where one difference dominates the design: robotic tools---trained policies, training pipelines, data collection---fail routinely, so a tool's quality must be measured, recorded at every call, and expired when the artifact behind it changes. AgenticRobotics is a backend-independent control plane in which an LLM controller drives disposable workers through durable train--evaluate--improve transactions: an immutable objective, controller-owned measurement, commit-keyed crash recovery, an evidence-graded skill library, and a tool registry with a standardized, recorded call surface. The title is an operational claim, not a selection claim: the operator can leave because promotion is evidence-gated, state is recoverable, and capability quality is derived from records---not because the loop picks better checkpoints than a human; on the one lineage we measured, it does not. The gates measurably buy false-promotion control (0.001 per run hardened versus 0.005--0.021 shipped), anytime-valid decisions under optional stopping, zero lost or duplicate effects under kill injection, and six of six artifact-tampering classes caught by a signed verifier.

</details>


### [116] [AeroDPO: Unleashing Lightweight UAV Navigation with High-Fidelity Perception and Automated Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07557)
*Peng Xu,Chengcheng Wang,Shaohua Wan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对无人机视觉-语言导航（UAV-VLN）问题，挑战了依赖大规模语言模型的传统范式。研究发现感知质量比语言推理能力更为关键，轻量级2B模型配合高保真视觉输入即可匹配7B基线的成功率。针对纯行为克隆方法在分布外场景中的碰撞问题，提出AeroDPO自动直接偏好优化流水线，利用物理模拟状态回滚、因果推理错误提取和碰撞规避干预，在未知场景中取得49.16%成功率的新SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UAV-VLN需要复杂3D环境中的快速响应控制，现有端到端方法依赖数十亿参数的大模型，导致部署延迟过高；同时纯行为克隆方法缺乏显式负反馈，在分布外场景存在严重的碰撞问题，且依赖不可扩展的人工标注。

Method: 提出AeroDPO自动直接偏好优化流水线：基于确定性物理模拟状态回滚机制，检测碰撞后自动回退环境提取因果推理错误作为拒绝动作；应用解耦的特权干预合成碰撞规避偏好操作；利用离线视觉语言检查器过滤视觉歧义。

Result: 轻量级2B模型配合高保真视觉输入完全匹配7B基线的整体成功率；应用AeroDPO后，在未知场景中成功率达到49.16%，同时大幅抑制碰撞率，建立自主空中智能体的新SOTA。

Conclusion: 感知质量根本性优于语言推理能力是UAV-VLN的关键洞察。最小化策略配合自动化数据飞轮能够有效克服行为克隆的鲁棒性缺陷，实现高效的无人机自主导航。

Abstract: Vision-Language Navigation for Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAV-VLN) requires rapid and reactive control in complex 3D environments. Recent minimalist end-to-end paradigms show great promise but typically rely on massive language models containing billions of parameters, incurring prohibitive latency for real-world edge deployment. In this paper, we challenge this parameter-heavy reliance. Comprehensive cross-scale evaluations reveal the critical insight that perception quality fundamentally outweighs language reasoning capacity. We demonstrate that a lightweight 2B model equipped with high-fidelity visual inputs completely matches the overall success rates of massive 7B baselines. However, this minimalist policy exposes a fundamental robustness flaw inherent to pure Behavior Cloning (BC). Lacking explicit negative feedback, the agent fails to internalize robust spatial constraints and exhibits alarming collision rates in out-of-distribution (OOD) scenarios. To overcome this vulnerability without relying on unscalable human annotations, we propose AeroDPO, a zero-cost automated Direct Preference Optimization pipeline driven by deterministic physical simulation state rollback. Upon detecting collisions, the system autonomously rewinds the environment to extract causal reasoning errors as rejected actions, applies decoupled privileged interventions to synthesize collision-avoidance preferred maneuvers, and leverages an offline vision language inspector to filter visual ambiguities. By equipping our 2B model with this automated data flywheel, AeroDPO boosts success rates to 49.16% on unmapped scenarios while drastically suppressing collision rates, establishing a new SOTA for autonomous aerial agents.

</details>


### [117] [Learning Physical Interaction: A Survey of Tactile- and Force-aware Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07558)
*Shilin Shan,Chuhao Zhou,Ruize Wang,Xinyan Chen,Xiangyu Chen,Xinyu Zhou,Boyu Ma,Iris Yuxuan Hu,Jingliang Li,Celeste Yuxuan Hu,Geng Li,Guohao Chen,Tianrui Zhu,Zhe Li,Yanjie Ze,Haoran Geng,Zhiyang Dou,Jianxin Bi,Yuejiang Liu,Jianshu Zhou,Jiachen Li,Paul Liang,Tatsuya Harada,Robert Katzschmann,Harold Soh,Na Li,Edward Johns,Danica Kragic,Jan Peters,Wojciech Matusik,Masayoshi Tomizuka,Jitendra Malik,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本综述提出了TF-ART框架，统一梳理了基于力和触觉感知的机器人学习多模态与多阶段方法，填补了现有综述在这一领域的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理基础机器人智能需要机器人感知、推理并调节与物理世界的交互，尤其在接触敏感操作中，除了视觉感知和运动生成外，力的调节和自适应控制同样关键。尽管近期机器人学习方法在整合力、触觉、视觉、语言和本体感觉等多模态感知方面取得进展，且许多系统采用多阶段架构，但现有综述尚未从统一视角明确回顾力与触觉感知的机器人学习，未能同时涵盖多模态感知与多阶段系统设计。

Method: 提出TF-ART（力/触觉感知机器人学习分类框架）——一种统一的层级结构，将各方法映射其中。该框架从方法论视角刻画近期工作如何组织观测模态、编码与融合异质感官输入、跨多阶段生成与细化动作，以及将学习策略与机器人末端反应式控制连接。在此基础上，进一步考察物理交互的任务设置与基础设施需求。

Result: 构建了融合算法与实践视角的综合框架，系统梳理了力与触觉感知机器人学习的多模态感知机制、多阶段架构设计及实际应用要求，为领域研究提供了统一分类体系。

Conclusion: 通过提出TF-ART分类框架，弥补了现有综述在力与触觉感知机器人学习方面的不足，为从多模态感知和多阶段系统设计统一视角理解该领域提供了系统性工具，并整合了算法层面与实用层面的考量。

Abstract: Physically grounded robot intelligence requires robots to perceive, reason about, and regulate their interactions with the physical world. This capability is particularly critical in contact-sensitive manipulation, where successful task execution depends not only on visual perception and motion generation, but also on force regulation and adaptive control. In this context, recent robot learning methods have made substantial progress by integrating force, tactile, vision, language, and proprioceptive sensing into learned manipulation policies. In parallel, many systems adopt multi-phase architectures that combine high-level policies, action-refinement modules, and low-level controllers to bridge semantic task understanding with reactive physical execution. Despite these advances, existing surveys have not explicitly reviewed force- and tactile-aware robot learning from a unified perspective that jointly captures multimodal sensing and multi-phase system design. This survey addresses this gap by proposing TF-ART, a Tactile/Force-Aware Robot learning Taxonomy for multimodal and multi-phase frameworks, which maps individual methods into a unified hierarchical structure. The framework characterizes how recent works organize observation modalities, encode and fuse heterogeneous sensory inputs, generate and refine actions across multiple phases, and connect learned policies to reactive robot-end control. Building on this methodological view, we further examine the task settings and infrastructure requirements of physical interaction, thereby integrating both algorithmic and practical perspectives on force- and tactile-aware robot learning.

</details>


### [118] [Projection-Retraction MPPI: Exact Constraint-Manifold Control for Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07573)
*Seulchan Lee,Leesai Park,Minhyeong Kang,Sanghyun Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PR-MPPI 通过在采样动态中直接约束处理（投影+回缩）实现硬约束控制，在双臂系统仿真与真实机器人上验证了闭合链约束与避障的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MPPI依赖成本惩罚处理约束，导致强任务成本下约束失效（如机械臂关节限位穿透或障碍物碰撞），尤其难以维持闭式运动链等严格等式约束。

Method: 提出投影-回缩MPPI（PR-MPPI）：1) 投影步骤将采样速度约束至满足等式子空间和不等式半空间的交集；2) 回缩步骤将命令精确拉回约束流形至数值容差，独立于任务权重。在14-DoF双臂系统上实现。

Result: 仿真中通过关节限位压力测试和随机避障，闭链约束误差达数值容差；真实硬件实验显示Unitree H1-2人形机器人可实时避开移动障碍物。

Conclusion: PR-MPPI首次在模型预测控制中实现约束的严格数值级满足，且不受任务成本强度影响，为复杂操控任务提供可靠解。代码及视频见https://rcilab.github.io/prmppi。

Abstract: Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) control is widely used in manipulation for its gradient-free, parallel handling of non-convex costs. Manipulation tasks, however, often impose constraints that hold throughout the motion: a closed kinematic chain that two grasping arms keep exactly, or joint limits and obstacle clearances that are never crossed. MPPI handles such constraints only through the cost, as soft penalties that hold approximately and fail under a strong task cost. To address this, we propose Projection-Retraction MPPI (PR-MPPI), which enforces the constraints inside the sampled dynamics. At every rollout step, the sampled velocity is projected to satisfy both constraint types: the equality restricts it to a subspace, and each inequality to a half-space within that subspace, so inequality handling never breaks the equality. This projection, however, satisfies the constraints only to first order, and a finite step leaves a small drift off the equality. Therefore, we retract the returned command back onto the constraint to numerical tolerance and independent of task weighting. We validate PR-MPPI on 14-DoF dual-arm systems. In simulation, the returned commands satisfy the closed-chain equality to numerical tolerance through a joint-limit stress test and randomized obstacle avoidance. On real hardware, the arms of a Unitree H1-2 humanoid reactively avoid a moving obstacle. Code and experiment videos are available at https://rcilab.github.io/prmppi.

</details>


### [119] [Reconfigurable Structural Robotic Assembly: Interlocking 3D Aggregations with Self-Aligning Compound Nested Lattice Modules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07576)
*Alexander Htet Kyaw,Miana Smith,Paul Richard,Neil Gershenfeld*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自对齐复合嵌套晶格模块，将几何智能编码于建筑化材料系统中，解决机器人抓取、自对齐、可逆连接、结构性能和三维聚合等需求，实现可重构的循环建筑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人建造系统通常将材料系统与机器人作为独立设计问题处理，智能主要集中在硬件、传感、运动规划和控制方面。本文旨在通过将几何智能编码于建筑化材料系统中，同时解决机器人抓取、自对齐、可逆连接、结构性能和三维聚合等多方面需求。

Method: 设计了一种自对齐复合嵌套晶格模块，由连接的立方八面体-八面体单元组成。立方八面体特征提供机器人抓取和对齐的明确表面，八面体特征集成可拆卸的快拆连接器和相应受体。采用嵌套排列实现沿x、y、z轴的可互锁聚合。使用机械臂和移动装配器进行家具和建筑尺度结构的组装演示。

Result: 压缩测试显示复合模块刚度为4,556 N/mm，最大载荷为3,445 N，压缩模量为17.5 MPa。组装配置包括座椅、跨越结构、表面和垂直框架。模块可拆解并在不同配置间重复使用。

Conclusion: 该研究通过将几何智能编码于建筑化材料系统中，实现了机器人友好、自对齐、可逆连接的模块化建筑系统，支持可重构和循环建筑，为机器人建造提供了新的设计理念。

Abstract: Robotic construction systems often treat the material system and the robot as separate design problems, locating intelligence primarily in hardware, sensing, motion planning, and control. This project instead investigates how geometric intelligence can be encoded within architected material systems to simultaneously address requirements for robotic grasping, self-alignment, reversible connection, structural performance, and three-dimensional aggregation. We introduce a self-aligning compound nested lattice module composed of conjoined cuboctahedral-octahedral units. The cuboctahedral features of the modules provide defined surfaces for robotic grasping and alignment, while the octahedral features incorporate screw-releasable snap-fit connectors and corresponding receptors. Additionally, we present a nested arrangement that enables interlocking aggregation along the x, y, and z axes. We demonstrate the system through furniture and architectural scale structures assembled using both a robotic arm and mobile assembler. The resulting configurations include seating, spanning structures, surfaces, and vertical frames. Compression testing of the compound module produced a stiffness of 4,556 N/mm, a maximum load of 3,445 N, and a compressive modulus of 17.5 MPa. The modules can also be disassembled and reused across different configurations, supporting reconfigurable and circular construction.

</details>


### [120] [LIRA: Local Cross-Layer Information Routing for Vision-Language-Action Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07596)
*Zhewei Zhang,Puyue Wang,Guanren Qiao,Yijie Weng,Jiawei Hu,Guo Li,Lujia Wang,Junyan Wang,Tao Gu,Hongliang Lu,Guiliang Liu,Hong Jia,Xinhu Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出LIRA机制，通过深度感知的信息路由将视觉-语言模型(VLM)的中间特征灵活地传递到动作解码器，在不改变骨干架构的前提下显著提升了机器人操作任务的性能和零样本迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型中VLM特征到动作解码器的路由接口研究不足，要么仅暴露表示层次结构的狭窄部分，要么将每个解码器块与单个VLM层刚性匹配，限制了对跨深度互补任务证据的访问。

Method: LIRA将VLM到动作的条件化表述为深度感知的信息路由。该机制在任务令牌特征和从中间VLM状态推导的LIRA查询特征上操作，为每个并行融合块分配以相应VLM层为中心的、深度对齐的局部窗口。并行融合块聚合相邻的LIRA查询特征，并与任务令牌特征和本体感觉输入集成后进行动作预测。该路由接口保持骨干架构、动作解码器和监督训练方案不变。

Result: 在LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus、CALVIN ABC→D和真实世界操作任务中，LIRA在相同的0.5B参数配置下，主要聚合指标均优于VLA-Adapter基线。在零样本迁移到LIBERO-Plus时，LIRA将平均成功率从59.1%提升至78.0%，提升18.9个百分点。

Conclusion: LIRA提供了一种更灵活有效的接口，用于将VLM特征路由到动作解码器，在不修改骨干架构和训练流程的情况下，显著改善了受控分布变化下的鲁棒性，为VLA模型的特征利用开辟了新的研究方向。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models transform representations from pretrained vision-language models (VLMs) into robot actions, yet the interface that routes intermediate VLM features into action decoders remains underexplored. Existing designs either expose only a narrow part of the representation hierarchy or rigidly match each decoder block to one VLM layer, restricting access to complementary task evidence across depths. We introduce LIRA, a local cross-layer action-conditioning mechanism that formulates VLM-to-action conditioning as depth-aware information routing. LIRA operates on task-token features and LIRA Query features derived from intermediate VLM states, then assigns each Parallel Fusion Block a depth-aligned local window centered on its corresponding VLM layer. Parallel Fusion Blocks aggregate neighboring LIRA Query features and integrate them with task-token features and proprioceptive inputs before action prediction. This routing interface leaves the backbone architecture, action decoder, and supervised training recipe unchanged. Across LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus, CALVIN ABC$\rightarrow$D, and real-world manipulation, LIRA improves the principal aggregate metrics over the VLA-Adapter baseline under the same 0.5B-parameter configuration. In zero-shot transfer to LIBERO-Plus, LIRA increases average success from 59.1% to 78.0%, an 18.9-point gain indicating improved robustness under controlled distribution shifts.

</details>


### [121] [AdaDexGrasp: Adaptive Dexterous Grasping via 3D Visuo-Tactile Representation Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07600)
*Xirui Liang,Jiaqi Liang,Jingkai Xu,Yuran Wang,Ruochong Li,Yuanpei Chen,Masayoshi Tomizuka,Wei Zhan,Ruihai Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种统一的视触觉融合抓取框架，通过将触觉信号与手指身份关联，实现接触感知抓取生成和触觉引导调整，显著提升机器人抓取的成功率和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类通过无缝整合视觉感知和触觉反馈实现稳定自适应抓取，而现有机器人抓取方法主要依赖视觉输入，缺乏接触后触觉引导适应机制，限制了系统的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Method: 提出统一的视触觉融合抓取框架，核心是高效的视触觉表示，将物体几何与触觉反馈紧密融合，通过将触觉信号与手指身份关联，支持规划阶段的接触感知抓取位姿生成和接触后的触觉引导精化。

Result: 在仿真和真实环境中的综合实验表明，该方法显著提高了抓取成功率，并在多样物体上展现出良好的泛化性能。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了机器人抓取中触觉引导适应的挑战，通过视触觉融合实现了更鲁棒、泛化能力更强的抓取系统。

Abstract: Humans achieve stable and adaptive grasps by seamlessly integrating visual perception and tactile feedback, a capability that remains challenging to replicate in robotic systems. Existing robotic grasping approaches predominantly rely on visual inputs and lack mechanisms for tactile-guided adaptation after contact, limiting robustness and generalization. To address this challenge, we propose a unified visuo-tactile-fusion grasping framework that integrates grasp generation, feasibility prediction, and adaptive refinement. At its core, our method introduces an efficient visuo-tactile representation that tightly fuses object geometry with tactile feedback by associating tactile signals with finger identities. This unified representation supports contact-aware grasp pose generation during planning and tactile-guided refinement after contact, enabling the system to reason about fine-grained finger-object interactions and adjust grasps dynamically. Comprehensive experiments in both simulation and real-world environments demonstrate that our approach significantly enhances grasp success rates and generalization across diverse objects.

</details>


### [122] [Enhanced Real-Time 6-DOF Extended Reality Catheter Tracking for Evaluating Potential Improvement in Efficiency, Precision, and Depth Perception for Cardiac Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07606)
*Mohsen Annabestani,Sandhya Sriram,Andrew Kuzemczak,S. Chiu Wong,Alexandros Sigaras,Bobak Mosadegh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究开发了一种基于扩展现实(XR)的3D可视化平台，用于心脏导管介入操作，相比传统2D可视化方式，显著提升了操作效率(快5倍)、路径精确性(路径缩短5倍)和训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管3D超声技术有所进步，但大多数经皮心脏介入操作仍依赖2D可视化，限制了深度感知和空间理解能力。研究人员旨在解决这一局限性，提高心脏介入操作的效率和精确性。

Method: 开发了一种XR平台，集成了定制机器视觉算法(实现5自由度导管追踪)和3D打印机电机械编码器(测量导管滚动)，实现患者特异性3D心脏模型中的实时6自由度(6-DOF)导管追踪与可视化。研究设计：20名医学专业学生在沉浸式3D可视化和传统2D导管室风格视图两种条件下，导航心脏内超声(ICE)导管至6个解剖目标。

Result: 3D可视化条件下的表现显著优于2D条件：(1)任务完成时间：54.6秒 vs 267.5秒(快5倍以上)；(2)导管行程：1,939毫米 vs 7,854毫米(减少约5倍)；(3)目标精确度提高，表现变异性降低；(4)运动学分析显示3D模式下深度轴导航更平滑，2D模式依赖重复校正运动；(5)用户评估：3D可视化在准确性、速度、可用性和临床价值方面获得更高评价。

Conclusion: 基于XR的3D可视化系统能够显著提升介入手术训练的效率和精确性，改善运动控制，相比传统2D可视化方式具有明显优势，为心脏介入操作培训提供了更有价值的解决方案。

Abstract: Despite advances in 3D ultrasound, most percutaneous cardiac interventions still rely on 2D visualization, limiting depth perception and spatial understanding. To address this challenge, we developed an Extended Reality (XR)-based platform that enables real-time six-degree-of-freedom (6-DOF) catheter tracking and visualization within a patient-specific 3D heart model. The system combines a custom machine-vision algorithm for 5-DOF catheter tracking with a 3D-printed electromechanical encoder that measures catheter roll, providing complete 6-DOF motion reconstruction. In a proof-of-concept study, 20 novice medical students navigated an intracardiac echocardiography (ICE) catheter to six anatomical targets using either immersive 3D visualization or a conventional 2D cathlab-style view. Participants in the 3D condition completed the task in 54.6 seconds and traveled 1,939 mm on average, compared with 267.5 seconds and 7,854 mm in the 2D condition. Therefore, the XR-based 3D system was more than 5x faster and required ~5x less catheter travel. The 3D mode also improved targeting precision and reduced performance variability. Participants consistently rated immersive visualization higher for accuracy, speed, usability, and clinical value. Kinematic analysis showed smoother depth-axis navigation in 3D, whereas 2D users relied on repeated corrective movements. These findings demonstrate that XR-based visualization can substantially improve procedural training efficiency, precision, and motor control.

</details>


### [123] [GWM-VLA: Geometry-Aware Latent World Modeling for Vision-Language-Action Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07619)
*Yanping Zhao,Hang Yu,Yiwei Wang,Chen Ye,Siyu Tian,Di Zhang,Qingjun Wang,Qian Chen,Junqiao Zhao,Chen Ye,Guang Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GWM-VLA通过几何感知的多视图状态编码、全局上下文条件化目标视图预测和共享潜在动作表示，提升视觉-语言-动作模型在视觉和环境变化下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在视觉和环境变化时性能下降，而现有潜在世界建模方法通常独立编码相机视图，未显式建模几何关系，导致鲁棒性不足。

Method: GWM-VLA提出几何感知的多视图状态编码，使用VGGT-Ω联合聚合多视图观测构建几何感知的多视图状态；潜在世界模型预测选定目标视图（如腕部视图）的下一帧patch tokens，保留多视图几何信息；共享潜在动作表示同时条件化潜在世界模型和流匹配动作头，结合潜在预测监督和真实机器人动作监督。

Result: 在仿真和真实环境中实验表明，GWM-VLA在鲁棒性和有效性方面优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过几何感知的潜在世界建模，GWM-VLA增强了VLA模型的鲁棒性，为视觉-语言-动作学习提供了更稳定的解决方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models achieve strong robotic manipulation performance but often degrade under visual and environmental shifts. Latent world modeling offers a promising approach to improving robustness, yet existing methods commonly encode camera views independently and predict holistic scene dynamics without explicitly modeling their geometric relationships. We propose GWM-VLA, a geometry-aware latent world modeling framework for VLA learning. GWM-VLA combines geometry-aware multi-view state encoding, global context-conditioned target-view prediction, and shared latent-action representations grounded by robot-action supervision. Specifically, VGGT-$Ω$ jointly aggregates multi-view observations at each timestep to construct geometry-aware multi-view states. The latent world model predicts the next-step patch tokens of a selected target view using patch and register tokens obtained after multi-view aggregation, thereby retaining multi-view geometric information without predicting the complete multi-view state. We use the wrist view as the target in our experiments, placing greater emphasis on end-effector motion and local gripper-object interactions. Finally, the shared latent-action representations condition both the latent world model and the flow-matching action head, allowing latent-prediction supervision and ground-truth robot-action supervision to jointly shape the same latent-action representations. Experiments across both simulation and real-world environments demonstrate the effectiveness and robustness of GWM-VLA.

</details>


### [124] [A Reconfigurable Tracked Robot for Enhanced Obstacle Traversal Through Movable Articulation Point and Internal Mass Relocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07624)
*Yuki Uda,Yasutaka Nakashima,Motoji Yamamoto,Ayato Kanada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出TRASER，一种具有可重构脊柱的履带机器人，通过关节点和质心可调节设计，结合铰链弹簧机制，显著提升了越障能力，实验结果显示其越障性能达到机器人体长的74%、66%和59%，为已知履带机器人中最高水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 履带机器人广泛应用于非结构化环境，但其越障能力受限于前端可达性与运动稳定性之间的权衡。

Method: 本文提出TRASER，一种可重构履带机器人，能够重新定位关节点和内部质量。采用铰链弹簧机制，将柔性局部化于弯曲区域，同时保持其余部分高刚度，从而提升前端可达性和质心转移能力。建立了几何和静态模型，分析关节点和质心位置对越障性能的影响。

Result: 实验表明，TRASER可实现阶梯、悬空平台和沟壑越障，分别达到机器人体长的74%、66%和59%。据作者所知，这些结果代表了履带移动机器人中已知最高的越障能力。

Conclusion: TRASER通过可重构设计和铰链弹簧机制，突破了传统履带机器人的越障限制，在非结构化环境中展现出卓越的地形适应能力。

Abstract: Tracked robots are widely used in unstructured environments; however, their obstacle traversal capability is fundamentally limited by a tradeoff between front-end reachability and locomotion stability. This study presents TRASER (Tracked Robot with Articulated Spine for Extended Reach), a reconfigurable tracked robot capable of relocating both its articulation point and internal mass. TRASER employs a tape-spring mechanism that localizes compliance to the bending region while maintaining high stiffness in the remaining body, thereby improving both front-end reachability and center-of-mass (CoM) shifting capability. Geometric and static models are developed to analyze the effects of articulation point and CoM position on step and ditch traversal performances. Experiments demonstrate step traversal, suspended-platform traversal, and ditch traversal of 74\%, 66\%, and 59\% of the robot body length, respectively. To the best of our knowledge, these results represent the highest reported obstacle traversal capabilities among tracked mobile robots.

</details>


### [125] [Hölder Signed Distance: A Differentiable, Signed, Parallelizable Metric for Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07707)
*Felipe Bartelt,Ali Umut Kaypak,Anthony Tzes,Farshad Khorrami,Luciano C. A. Pimenta,Vinicius M. Gonçalves*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Hölder算子的凸多面体可微符号距离函数，实现了闭式计算和GPU并行化，在机器人控制和运动规划中具备实时优化优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器人运动规划与控制中，可微距离函数对实时优化至关重要。然而现有欧氏符号距离函数(SDF)存在不可微点，且替代方法常牺牲可微性、符号信息或计算效率。

Method: 提出Hölder最小值和Hölder最大值算子作为可微的最小/最大算子替代，将其应用于经典SDF公式中，得到闭式可微的Hölder符号距离。

Result: 通过运行时间对比验证了计算性能优势，并在机器人机械臂实验中展示了其在控制应用中的适用性。

Conclusion: 该方法实现了闭式可微符号距离计算，避免了迭代收敛问题，支持GPU并行化，为机器人实时控制提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Computing distances between sets is essential in robotic motion planning and control, where differentiable gradients enable real-time optimization. The Euclidean Signed Distance Function (SDF), however, is not differentiable everywhere, and existing alternatives often sacrifice differentiability, sign information, or computational efficiency. In this letter, we introduce a novel differentiable signed distance between convex polyhedra. To this end, we first propose differentiable versions of the minimum and maximum operators, termed the Hölder minimum and Hölder maximum. We then replace the original min-max operators in the classical SDF formulation, yielding the Hölder signed distance. Unlike prior differentiable distance formulations that rely on iterative algorithms, our approach is computed in closed form, eliminating convergence issues while remaining naturally amenable to GPU parallelization. We validate the practical advantages and computational performance of the proposed distance through runtime comparisons with existing approaches. We also present a robotic manipulator experiment, demonstrating its suitability for applications in control.

</details>


### [126] [Complete, Scalable, and Robust Prioritized Planning for Multi-Robot Ordered Storage and Retrieval at Maximum Capacity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07734)
*William Zhang,Tzvika Geft,Jingjin Yu,Kostas Bekris*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新型多机器人在线优先路径规划算法，用于解决高密度拼图式仓储系统中在最大容量下的有序存取问题，实现了随机器人数量近线性优化的执行时间，并保证了无死锁运行与系统鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化仓库面临存储密度与检索吞吐量之间的根本权衡。拼图式存储（PBS）通过消除通道提高存储容量，但在高密度空间中协调多个机器人计算上具有挑战性，存在死锁风险，需要高效可靠的解决方案。

Method: 针对单边界可访问的矩形网格，提出最大容量下有序存取问题的多机器人建模方法。利用免重排布置的特性，设计在线、优先级的多智能体路径规划算法，该算法利用存储布置的特定不变量来保证完备性并防止死锁。

Result: 实验证明该方法在机器人数量方面实现了接近线性的完工时间改进，最多可达m=C（C为网格宽度）。支持鲁棒性的算法开销可忽略不计，系统通过鲁棒存储布置处理出发序列的不确定性，与非鲁棒基准相比在速度上无显著损失。

Conclusion: 该方法成功弥合了几何可行性与执行效率之间的差距，通过利用存储布置的不变量保证完备性、防止死锁并实现可扩展性，为高密度拼图式仓库系统中的多机器人协调提供了高效可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Automated warehouses face a fundamental trade-off between maximizing storage density and achieving high retrieval throughput. While puzzle-based storage (PBS) architectures increase capacity by eliminating aisles, coordinating multiple robots in these high-density spaces is computationally challenging due to the potential for deadlocks. This paper introduces a novel multi-robot formulation for the ``ordered storage and retrieval problem at maximum capacity''. The focus is on rectangular grids accessible from a single boundary, where loads need to be first stored up to full capacity and then efficiently retrieved, given a planned departure sequence. This work bridges the gap between geometric feasibility and execution efficiency by leveraging the properties of relocation-free arrangements. These properties guide an online, prioritized multi-agent path-finding algorithm, which is the main contribution of this work. Unlike general centralized planners, the approach exploits the specific invariants of the storage arrangement to guarantee completeness and prevent deadlocks, enabling scalability. Experiments demonstrate that the method achieves near-linear improvement in makespan with respect to the number of robots, up to $m = C$, where $C$ is the grid width. Crucially, the algorithmic overhead of supporting robustness is negligible; the system handles uncertainty in departure sequences using robust storage arrangements with no significant penalty in execution speed compared to the non-robust baseline.

</details>


### [127] [Enhancing Autonomous Vehicle Navigation with a Clothoid-Based Lateral Controller](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07740)
*Aashish Shaju,Steve Southward,Mehdi Ahmadian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究提出一种基于clothoid曲线和自适应lookahead机制的自动驾驶车辆横向控制策略，通过Euler螺旋实现平滑曲率过渡，提高横向稳定性和路径跟踪精度，减少乘客不适感和车辆侧翻风险。通过TruckSim和Simulink联合仿真验证，该方法在多种驾驶场景下均表现出显著的横向控制性能改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本研究旨在解决自动驾驶车辆在横向控制方面的挑战，特别是提高横向稳定性和路径跟踪精度。现有方法在变工况条件下可能产生乘客不适感和增加车辆侧翻风险，因此需要开发一种能够适应实时车辆动力学和道路几何条件的先进控制策略。

Method: 1. 采用基于clothoid曲线的方法，利用Euler螺旋实现平滑曲率过渡；2. 创新性地引入自适应lookahead距离调整机制，基于实时车辆动力学和道路几何条件进行优化；3. 开发准反馈控制算法，在每个时间步构建最优clothoid曲线并生成适当的转向输入；4. 应用前馈滤波器补偿车辆横向动力学滞后，提高控制响应性和稳定性。

Result: 通过TruckSim和Simulink的综合联合仿真验证，提出的控制器在多种驾驶场景下均实现了显著的横向控制性能改进。仿真结果表明该方法能够有效提高路径跟踪精度和横向稳定性，同时减少乘客不适感。

Conclusion: 本研究成功开发了一种先进的自动驾驶车辆横向控制策略，通过结合clothoid曲线和自适应lookahead机制，实现了优异的横向控制性能。未来工作将专注于将该控制器扩展到高速应用场景，并进一步优化以减少偏轨误差，特别是针对铰接车辆的应用。

Abstract: This study introduces an advanced lateral control strategy for autonomous vehicles using a clothoid-based approach integrated with an adaptive lookahead mechanism. The primary focus is on enhancing lateral stability and path-tracking accuracy through the application of Euler spirals for smooth curvature transitions, thereby reducing passenger discomfort and the risk of vehicle rollover. An innovative aspect of our work is the adaptive adjustment of lookahead distance based on real-time vehicle dynamics and road geometry, which ensures optimal path following under varying conditions. A quasi-feedback control algorithm constructs optimal clothoids at each time step, generating the appropriate steering input. A lead filter compensates for the vehicle's lateral dynamics lag, improving control responsiveness and stability. The effectiveness of the proposed controller is validated through a comprehensive co-simulation using TruckSim and Simulink, demonstrating significant improvements in lateral control performance across diverse driving scenarios. Future directions include scaling the controller for higher-speed applications and further optimization to minimize off-track errors, particularly for articulated vehicles.

</details>


### [128] [CoCoNav: Conformal Control for Safe Robot Navigation in Crowds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07751)
*Cheng Guo,Mingzhe Ni,Zheng Liang,Yihu Ling,Yuan Hu,Michele Caprio,Daniele Pucci,Wei Pan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CoCoNav是一个新颖的人群导航框架，通过整合在线一致性校准与运行时认证规划，实现在动态人群环境中的安全高效机器人导航，平衡了避障、任务成功率和导航效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本研究旨在解决机器人在人群中安全高效导航的挑战，现有方法存在局限性：反应式方法易产生振荡行为，预测式规划器往往将预测视为精确或依赖限制性误差模型，而引入保守不确定性集合可能导致模型预测控制不可行。

Method: 提出CoCoNav框架，结合在线一致性校准与运行时认证规划。采用水平特定的一致性比例-积分控制器自适应轨迹误差边界，调节长期经验覆盖率；规划器采用"放宽-验证"策略，先用软约束MPC生成标称轨迹，再与应急预案一起对照校准边界进行认证。

Result: 仿真和四足机器人实验表明，CoCoNav在避障、任务成功率和导航效率方面相比基线方法取得了更好的平衡。

Conclusion: CoCoNav通过在线校准和运行时认证有效应对预测不确定性，为人群中的机器人导航提供了安全高效的解决方案，性能优于现有基线方法。

Abstract: Safe and efficient robot navigation in crowds requires anticipating pedestrian motion despite uncertain and potentially shifting prediction errors. Existing reactive methods can produce oscillatory behavior, while predictive planners often treat forecasts as exact or rely on restrictive error models. Incorporating conservative uncertainty sets as hard constraints can also render model predictive control (MPC) infeasible. We propose \textit{CoCoNav}, a crowd-navigation framework that combines online conformal calibration with runtime-certified planning. A horizon-specific conformal proportional--integral controller adapts trajectory-error bounds to regulate long-run empirical coverage, enabling the framework to respond to changing prediction errors. A \textit{relax-then-verify} planner preserves solver feasibility by generating nominal trajectories with soft-constrained MPC and separately certifying them, together with contingency maneuvers, against the calibrated bounds before execution. Simulations and quadruped experiments show that CoCoNav achieves a favorable balance among collision avoidance, task success, and navigation efficiency relative to the evaluated baselines.

</details>


### [129] [Drone-Assisted UAV-UGV Collaboration for Autonomous Navigation in Snow-Covered Terrain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07797)
*Shreyam Gupta,P. Agrawal,Priyam Gupta,R. Gautam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向高海拔积雪地形的无人机-无人车协同导航框架，通过自定义U-Net架构实现96.5%的道路分割准确率，结合EKF扩展卡尔曼滤波和YOLOv5视觉跟踪，在能见度低、地面不稳定的环境下实现精准导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高海拔积雪地形存在能见度低和地面不稳定问题，传统导航方法在此类环境下失效，需要开发新型协同导航方案以应对极端气候条件下的路径规划挑战。

Method: 1. 设计轻量级U-Net架构满足实时道路分割的计算约束，采用合成积雪数据增强技术提升分割精度；2. 无人机定位采用EKF融合GPS和IMU数据；3. 无人车定位通过YOLOv5视觉跟踪和无人机RGB-D相机深度数据实现；4. 基于分割结果开发动态路径规划算法以应对积雪漂移。

Result: 道路分割准确率达到96.5%，无人机定位最大观测位置误差为±0.5米，在遮挡测试环境中实现成功导航且偏差极小。

Conclusion: 该协同导航框架有效解决了高海拔积雪地形的导航难题，通过多传感器融合和深度学习技术，在极端环境下实现了高精度定位和路径规划，为恶劣气候条件下的自动驾驶系统提供了可行解决方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a collaborative UAV-UGV navigation framework for high-altitude, snow-covered terrain, where reduced visibility and unstable ground render conventional methods ineffective. We introduce a custom efficient U-Net architecture that falls under the computational constraints for real-time road segmentation, utilizing a novel synthetic snow data augmentation technique to achieve 96.5% segmentation accuracy. For UAV localization, we implement an Extended Kalman Filter (EKF) fusing onboard GPS and IMU data, achieving a maximum observed positional error of +-0.5 meters. The UGV position is determined via a visual tracking pipeline using YOLOv5 and depth data from the UAV's RGB-D camera. A dynamic path planning algorithm utilizes this segmentation to adjust for snow drifts, enabling successful navigation in obscured test environment with minimal deviation.

</details>


### [130] [A Mixed-Stiffness Anthropomimetic Fingertip Broadens the Operating Range for Coin Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07887)
*Kaigen Go,Yinlai Jiang,Hiroshi Yokoi,Shunta Togo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 混合刚度指尖（中心软、两侧硬）相比均匀软指尖在更多条件下能成功抓取硬币，表明垫内刚度分布对抓取性能有重要影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人抓取薄平物体（如硬币）需要重新定向物体、接触底面或专门机制。先前研究表明，刚性指甲可通过几何约束提升抓取精度，本研究探索是否可通过材料选择在指垫内创建额外的约束形成边界以扩展有效约束条件。

Method: 制造类人指尖：中心使用Shore E10硅胶，两侧使用Shore A60硅胶，并与均匀E10软指尖对比。使用自动化装置执行倾斜旋转式指尖捏合，使指垫接触硬币侧面并将其从贴面状态抬起。测试了水平接近距离、垂直指端位移和食指旋转的变化条件。

Result: 混合刚度指对在几何约束形成和稳定抓取过渡阶段，在更多测试条件下保持高成功率。无指针对在所有36个条件中均失败。均匀指对在硬币沿指轴位置变化时表现更好（条件依赖性权衡）。经硬币尺寸调整后，两种指尖均能抓取六种日本硬币。

Conclusion: 薄物体抓取的有效操作范围不仅取决于指垫软硬度，还取决于指甲支撑指垫内刚度分布的位置，使边界位置成为指尖设计的重要变量。

Abstract: Robotic grasping of thin, flat objects such as coins on hard surfaces remains challenging because conventional methods require reorienting the object, accessing its underside, or adding a dedicated nail mechanism. We previously showed that a rigid nail arrests soft-pad deformation and thereby forms a geometric constraint that improves precision grasping. Here we asked whether an additional constraint-forming boundary, created within the pad by material choice rather than by anatomy, could extend the conditions under which that constraint holds. We fabricated anthropomimetic fingertips with Shore E10 silicone at the center and Shore A60 at the sides, and compared them with uniformly soft E10 fingertips. An automated apparatus performed an oblique rotational tip pinch in which the pad engaged the coin's lateral surface, lifting it from flush contact with no gap beneath it. Over variations in horizontal approach distances, vertical finger displacements, and index-finger rotation, the mixed-stiffness pair maintained high success rates across more tested settings than the uniform pair during both geometric-constraint formation and the transition to a stable grasp. The nail-free pair failed in all 36 conditions of Experiment 1-1. However, the uniform pair performed better when coin position along the finger axis was varied, a condition-dependent trade-off. After tuning for coin size, both fingertip types grasped all six Japanese denominations. These results suggest that the operating range for thin-object grasping depends not only on pad softness but also on where stiffness is placed within a nail-supported pad, making boundary placement a candidate fingertip design variable.

</details>


### [131] [Removing Infrastructure Barriers in Human-Robot Collaboration Through Wireless Reconfigurable Cells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09658)
*Emma Takács,Mátyás Hajós,Ádám Juniki,Ádám Fischer,Zoltán Komáromi,Kristóf Abai,Dániel Horváth,Sándor Máthé,Konstantinos Kousias,Bence Tipary*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于5G的无线人-机器人协作系统，配备电池供电的多传感器平台原型，集成计算机视觉模块实现物体检测和手势识别。该系统在匈牙利和挪威进行测试，实现了12ms的往返延迟，适用于安全的自适应HRC，但也揭示了当前5G部署中的互操作性限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人-机器人协作(HRC)在动态、高混合、小批量工业场景（如再制造）中至关重要。传统设置受限于电源和数据线缆，限制了模块化与重构能力；适合实时感知和安全协作的商业无线设备选择有限。

Method: 构建基于5G的高灵活性无线系统作为通用实验测试平台。集成新型电池供电多传感器平台原型消除基础设施障碍；整合计算机视觉模块用于物体检测、位姿估计和手部识别；模型使用合成和真实数据训练；通过5G将计算密集型任务卸载到边缘端。在匈牙利和挪威实施，在公共和私立的独立和非独立5G基础设施上进行评估。

Result: 模型在 varying 光照和背景条件下可靠检测定向抓握位姿和人手，mAP@50-95达到97.74±0.10%，平均推理时间12.5ms。网络实验在兼容网络设备配对下实现低至12ms的往返响应时间，适用于安全自适应HRC。测量结果也揭示了当前5G部署中互操作性的实际限制。

Conclusion: 所提架构为解决带宽-延迟权衡做出了贡献，提供了适用于再制造、操作员培训和用户研究等应用的便携式HRC测试平台。系统在兼容的网络-设备配对下达到了适合安全自适应HRC的延迟水平。未来工作需解决当前5G部署中发现的互操作性限制问题。

Abstract: Human-Robot Collaboration (HRC) plays a vital role in dynamic, high mix, low volume industrial scenarios such as remanufacturing, which frequently face workcell rearrangements. Traditional setups are constrained by power and data cabling, restricting modularity and reconfigurations, while the selection of commercial wireless devices suitable for real-time perception and safe collaboration are limited in availability. This paper presents a highly flexible, wireless, 5G-based system that serves as a versatile experimental testbed for applications including remanufacturing, operator training, and user studies. To eliminate infrastructure barriers, the workcell integrates a novel battery-powered, multi-sensor platform prototype. Additionally, to support operator safety and system adaptability across environmental shifts, the system integrates a computer vision module for object detection and pose estimation, further augmented for robust hand recognition. Trained on synthetic and real data, the model reliably detects oriented grasping poses and human hands across varying lighting and background conditions (with an mAP@50-95 of 97.74 +- 0.10% and a mean inference time of 12.5 ms). Offloading these computationally intensive tasks to the edge via 5G, the proposed architecture contributes to resolving the bandwidth-latency trade-off. To demonstrate portability, the system was implemented in both Hungary and Norway, and was evaluated across a combination of public and private, Standalone and Non-Standalone 5G infrastructures. The performed network experiments produced results in round-trip response times down to 12 ms in case of compatible network-device pairings, suitable for safe, adaptive HRC. However, these measurements also revealed practical limitations related to interoperability in current 5G deployments that should be addressed in future works.

</details>


### [132] [Auditing Instruction-Trajectory Mismatches in Multimodal Robot Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07895)
*Simon Holk,Ryosuke Takanami,Tatsuya Matsushima,Yusuke Iwasawa,Yutaka Matsuo,Yueh-Hua Wu,Kei Ota*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究针对机器人演示数据集中的指令-轨迹不匹配问题，提出一种多模态概率融合审计框架，能够在无需训练的情况下检测并纠正这类微妙的数据错误，从而提升下游策略学习的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人演示数据集用于训练视觉-语言-动作策略时，可能存在一种微妙但有害的失败模式：行为上正确但与错误语言指令配对的轨迹。这种指令-轨迹不匹配（ITMs）不同于明显的执行失败，它们看起来合理，但会污染策略学习的语言-行为映射。这种隐性问题严重影响模型对语言指令的理解和执行能力。

Method: 提出多模态概率融合（MMPF）框架，这是一种无需训练的审计方法。核心机制包括：1）将每个模态视为专家，分别评估任务标签分布；2）通过局部邻域一致性和全局原型相似度估计标签分布；3）使用预测熵加权在产品专家模型中融合多个模态信息。该方法利用多模态信息的互补性，提高指令-轨迹匹配判断的准确性。

Result: 在LIBERO基准测试中，注入指令不匹配和真实机器人噪声数据环境下，MMPF在ITM检测和标签纠正准确性方面均达到最佳整体表现。审计结果显示，在需要语言来消除任务歧义的场景中，数据清洗能显著改善大多数下游策略学习效果。

Conclusion: 研究通过真实机器人实验验证了该方法能够有效提升策略性能。同时揭示了演示数据过滤与重标注之间的权衡关系。MMPF框架为机器人演示数据集提供了有效的质量控制方案，确保语言指令与执行行为的一致性，对提升基于演示学习的机器人策略可靠性具有重要意义。

Abstract: Robot demonstration datasets used to train vision-language-action policies can contain a subtle but harmful failure mode: trajectories that are behaviorally correct but paired with the wrong language instruction. We study post-hoc auditing of these Instruction-Trajectory Mismatches (ITMs). Unlike failed rollouts, ITMs often look plausible, and can corrupt the language-behavior mapping learned by the policy. We propose Multimodal Probabilistic Fusion (MMPF), a training-free auditing framework that treats each modality as an expert, estimates a task-label distribution from local neighborhood agreement and global prototype similarity, and then fuses modalities with predictive-entropy weighting in a product of experts. Across LIBERO benchmarks with injected instruction mismatches and noisy real-robot data, MMPF achieves the strongest overall ITM detection and label correction accuracy. We also show that auditing improves most downstream policy learning in settings where language is needed to disambiguate the task. We demonstrate in real robot experiments that our method can achieve improved policy performance and show the trade-off of filtering demonstrations compared to relabeling.

</details>


### [133] [4D-WAM: Infusing Spatiotemporal Awareness into World Action Models through Trajectory Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08023)
*Lishan Yang,Wenxuan Song,Xi Wang,Pingyue Sheng,Zheng Fang,Ziyang Zhou,Junjie He,Haodong Yan,Jiayi Chen,Nan Sun,Qiao Sun,Lingqiao Liu,Yan Wang,Yuxiang Gao,Feras Dayoub,Haoang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出4D-WAM训练策略，通过将3D轨迹场的时空知识注入世界行动模型（WAMs）来解决2D像素与3D机器人操作空间间的表征差距问题，利用运动对齐和目的地对齐两个互补目标使模型学习轨迹级时空表征，实验表明该策略在多个基模型上均提升了空间理解、执行精度、鲁棒性、泛化性和通用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的世界行动模型（WAMs）通常以2D像素空间表示视频，与机器人执行动作的3D空间存在表征差距；近期3D方法虽然引入了3D信息，但未能充分利用3D结构的动态性。因此需要一种能够有效融合3D时空知识并指导WAMs学习更优表征的方法。

Method: 提出4D-WAM模型无关训练策略，通过表征对齐将3D轨迹场的时空知识注入WAMs。具体包含两个目标：1)运动对齐：对齐相邻帧之间的时间特征变化，鼓励模型在训练中建立局部4D感知；2)目的地对齐：通过最小化源帧与目标帧之间注意力相似性分布的差距，指导模型从源帧推断最终目的地。两者结合提供局部运动监督和长周期目标指导，使WAMs学习轨迹级时空表征。

Result: 在不同基模型上进行广泛分布内和分布外实验，结果表明4D-WAM在空间理解、执行精度、鲁棒性、泛化性和通用性方面均有提升。

Conclusion: 4D-WAM通过引入运动对齐和目的地对齐两个互补目标，成功将3D轨迹场的时空知识注入WAMs，解决了2D与3D表征差距问题，并显著提升了模型的多项性能，具有良好的模型无关性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Building on recent advances in world models, World Action Models (WAMs) jointly model video prediction and action generation. However, they typically represent videos in 2D pixel space, creating a representation gap with 3D space in which robotic actions are executed. Recent 3D approaches introduce 3D information, but fail to fully exploit the dynamics of 3D structures. In this work, we propose 4D-WAM, a model-agnostic training strategy that injects spatiotemporal knowledge from 3D trajectory fields into WAMs through representation alignment. To this end, we introduce two complementary objectives: 1) motion alignment, which aligns temporal feature variations across adjacent frames and encourages the model to build local 4D awareness during training, and 2) destination alignment, which guides the model to infer the final destination from the source frame by minimizing the gap between their attention-like similarity distributions. Together, these objectives provide both local motion supervision and long-horizon goal guidance, enabling WAMs to learn trajectory-level spatiotemporal representations. Extensive in-distribution and out-of-distribution experiments across different base models demonstrate the model's improvements in spatial understanding, execution precision, robustness, generalization, and versatility.

</details>


### [134] [DA-NBV: A Direction-Aware Next-Best-View Planner for Efficient 3D Reconstruction of Ships at Sea](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08025)
*Jiaming Chen,Juntao Yang,Zhentao Zou,Qi Ming,Yi Yu,Zhihang Zhong,Xue Yang,Xue Jiang,Yue Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DA-NBV，一种方向感知的NBV策略，用于海上船舶3D重建。该方法通过引入方向观测统计信息和可学习的位置优势场(PAF)，在复杂海况下显著提高重建完整性和效率，实验显示重建完整性提升约3个百分点，Chamfer距离降低43%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 船舶3D重建对海上监管、损害评估和自主海上作业至关重要。现有NBV策略主要建模空间占用状态而忽略方向观测历史，这对具有复杂上层结构和严重自遮挡的船舶尤为不利。海上环境进一步加剧了挑战：波浪引起的横摇、纵摇和垂荡不断改变船舶姿态和表面可见性，同时风力扰动和有限机载功率对扫描效率提出更严格要求。

Method: DA-NBV在传统占用状态基础上增强方向观测统计信息，引入可学习的位置优势场(PAF)利用方向信息指导视点选择。策略采用局部约束动作空间和非线性覆盖塑形奖励以提高扫描效率。此外，开发了面向船舶的SeaShip-3D数据集和可配置海况仿真环境。

Result: 在不同横摇、纵摇和垂荡条件下的实验表明，DA-NBV将重建完整性提高约3个百分点，Chamfer距离降低43%，同时实现了更高的路径效率。

Conclusion: 方向感知的NBV策略有效解决了海上3D重建中的挑战，相比现有方法提供了更完整、高效的船舶重建解决方案，为海上自主作业提供了可靠的技术支持。

Abstract: Accurate 3D reconstruction of ships at sea is important for maritime supervision, damage assessment, and autonomous maritime operations. Although 3D reconstruction has advanced considerably, high-quality data acquisition still largely relies on manually designed trajectories or skilled operators, resulting in high costs and limited scalability. Next-best-view (NBV) planning automates this process by selecting subsequent viewpoints based on the current state. However, existing NBV policies mainly model spatial occupancy while overlooking directional observation history. This limitation is particularly problematic for ships: their complex superstructures and severe self-occlusions require observations from multiple viewpoints, and insufficient directional coverage often yields incomplete reconstructions. These challenges are further amplified at sea, where wave-induced heave, roll, and pitch continuously alter the ship's pose and surface visibility. Meanwhile, wind disturbances and limited onboard power impose stricter requirements on scanning efficiency. To address these challenges, we propose DA-NBV, a direction-aware NBV policy that augments the conventional occupancy state with directional observation statistics. We introduce a learnable Position Advantage Field (PAF) that uses directional information to guide viewpoint selection. The policy further adopts a locally constrained action space and a nonlinear coverage-shaping reward to improve scanning efficiency. We also develop the ship-oriented SeaShip-3D dataset and a configurable sea-state simulation environment. Experiments under varying heave, roll, and pitch conditions show that DA-NBV improves reconstruction completeness by approximately 3 percentage points and reduces Chamfer distance by 43% while achieving higher path efficiency.

</details>


### [135] [Compiling and Benchmarking Task-State Horizons for Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08036)
*Meiqi Wang,Shichao Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出任务状态视界（TSH）概念，并开发RoboGraph机器人任务编译器，通过构建588个任务场景评估前沿智能体模型在长周期具身任务中的表现，发现大多数模型在跟踪复杂任务状态转换方面存在显著缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人基准测试主要通过动作序列长度和子任务复杂度来衡量难度，忽略了智能体需要跟踪由探索行为和环境动态引起的不断变化的任务相关世界状态这一独特挑战。研究需要定义并评估这一新维度下的模型性能。

Method: 1. 定义任务状态视界（TSH）：智能体需要跟踪的任务相关状态转换跨度
2. 开发RoboGraph：将状态转换依赖关系转化为可执行符号图的机器人任务编译器
3. RoboGraph从空间和时间因果关系构建TSH，包括执行过程中的意外失败和干预
4. 发布包含588个任务场景（84个不同场景）的基准测试，具有变化的TSH
5. 在语义和视觉闭环环境中评估15个高级智能体模型

Result: 实验表明大多数模型在应对高要求TSH时表现不佳，揭示了在长时间范围内维持、探索和更新任务相关状态方面存在显著差距。具体而言，15个测试模型中大多数无法有效处理复杂的任务状态转换。

Conclusion: 当前前沿智能体模型在处理长周期具身任务时存在根本性局限，特别是在跟踪和维护任务相关状态转换方面。RoboGraph提供了评估这一能力的新方法，为未来研究指明了改进方向，强调了需要开发更好的算法来应对任务状态视界挑战。

Abstract: Frontier agentic models are increasingly deployed as high-level planners for long-horizon embodied tasks. Existing robotic benchmarks have advanced long-horizon evaluation, but primarily characterize difficulty through action-sequence length and subtask complexity, overlooking a distinct challenge: agents must track evolving task-relevant world states induced by both their exploration and environmental dynamics. We define the span of task-relevant state transitions that an agent must track as task-state horizon (TSH). To evaluate how agent performance varies with TSH, we introduce RoboGraph, a robotic task compiler that translates state-transition dependencies into executable symbolic graphs. Specifically, RoboGraph constructs task-state horizons from spatial and temporal causal dependencies, including those induced by unexpected failures and interventions during task execution. Building on RoboGraph, we release a benchmark comprising 588 episodes across 84 scenes with varying TSHs. Experiments evaluating 15 advanced agentic models in both semantic and visual closed-loop environments show that most models struggle with demanding TSHs, revealing substantial gaps in maintaining, exploring, and updating task-relevant state over long horizon.

</details>


### [136] [PhysX-CoT: Structured Physical Reasoning from a Single Image to Simulation-Ready 3D Assets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08053)
*Jie Huang,Xiaohe Li,Jiahao Li,Fangli Mou,Chen Qian,Yuqiang Fang,Junhao Fan,Kaixin Zhang,Zide Fan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PhysX-CoT方法，将单图像3D资产生成重构为显式的结构化物理推理过程，通过分解为可监督的部分级状态轨迹（分解、2D/3D定位、关系、粗糙几何、表面线索），显著优于现有基线方法，生成的资产在虚幻引擎5中具有高有效性的碰撞和关节功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将图像到3D的生成视为输出中心的视觉语言模型，存在两个主要局限：(1)部件放置和局部形状在全局坐标令牌流中纠缠在一起；(2)中间物理状态从未暴露用于监督、条件控制或验证。这种输出中心视角限制了生成质量和物理一致性。

Method: PhysX-CoT将单图像资产生成重构为显式的结构化物理推理过程，包含有序的、机器可解析的部分级状态轨迹。具体而言：几何结构进行因式分解，3D框携带放置信息，局部代码携带形状信息；各状态分别进行监督，用于几何条件控制，并作为奖励目标；采用CoT对齐的GRPO优化解析有效性、定位、几何、放置和物理一致性。在统一协议下，所有基线在同一主干、数据和冻结解码器上重新训练进行对比。

Result: 在统一协议下，PhysX-CoT在几何、尺度和物理属性指标上全面优于最接近的完整任务基线。通过Oracle、令牌匹配和状态顺序控制实验证明，显式状态是功能性的而非装饰性的。在虚幻引擎5中验证，生成的资产具有高有效性的解析、碰撞和关节功能。

Conclusion: 通过采用显式结构化物理推理而非输出中心方法，PhysX-CoT能够有效生成仿真就绪的3D资产，实现更好的几何质量、物理一致性和可验证性，为机器人和具身AI应用提供了更可靠的3D资源生成方案。

Abstract: Simulation-ready 3D assets are central to robotics and embodied AI. Generating them from a single image is usually framed as a vision-language model that emits a serialized asset for a decoder to turn into geometry and physical fields, leaving the image-to-3D reasoning implicit. We argue the limiting factor is this output-centric view: part placement and local shape are entangled in one global-coordinate token stream, and the intermediate physical states are never exposed for supervision, conditioning, or verification. PhysX-CoT instead casts single-image asset generation as an explicit structured physical reasoning process, an ordered and machine-parseable trajectory of part-level states covering decomposition, 2D and 3D grounding, relations, coarse geometry, and surface cues that we separately supervise, use to condition geometry, and treat as reward targets. Geometry is factorized so that 3D boxes carry placement and local codes carry shape, and CoT-aligned GRPO optimizes parse validity, grounding, geometry, placement, and physical consistency.
