Conclusion: 本研究证明了基于隐式手术注意力状态建模的方法能够有效支持腹腔镜手术的自主相机控制，为复杂动态手术场景中的手术自主化提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Autonomous laparoscopic camera control requires continuous understanding of the surgeon's operative intent in dynamic surgical scenes, where the target operative region is not a stable physical object but a latent and temporally evolving attention state. In this work, we present Surgical Latent Attention Tracking (SurgLAT), a causal online framework for latent surgical attention modeling and autonomous laparoscopic view control. SurgLAT uses a frozen DINOv3 encoder and a state-conditioned spatial token mixer to extract operative evidence under a memory-guided spatial prior, while a selective causal latent memory module jointly models short-term motion continuity and long-horizon surgical intent evolution through dynamic retrieval of current, recent, and historical latent states. The learned latent surgical attention state is decoded into a probabilistic attention heatmap and operative region for downstream endoscope guidance. Beyond perception, we further introduce a robotic deployment framework with explicit laparoscopic Remote Center of Motion (RCM) constrained control based on virtual-axis formulation, together with redundancy-aware null-space initialization for stable and smooth manipulator motion. We validate the full system on real laparoscopic surgical videos and a physical robotic laparoscope platform. Experimental results demonstrate robust online operative-region tracking and stable autonomous endoscopy adjustment under occlusion, rapid motion, and target transitions, highlighting the effectiveness of latent surgical intent modeling for surgical autonomy.

</details>


### [205] [GRACE: LLM-Grounded Semantic Metric Spaces for Scalable Mixed-Data Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07881)
*Zihua Yang,Zhencheng Xie,Junyang Chen,Liang Xie,Yiqun Zhang,Mengke Li,Yang Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GRACE框架，通过一次性LLM语义增强和统计验证，解决混合表格数据聚类中的语义异质性问题，在保持可扩展性的同时提升聚类精度和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合表格数据聚类面临连续数值和离散类别的异质性挑战。传统方法仅依赖数据集内部统计信息，无法捕获概念上存在但统计上未观测到的亲和力。虽然LLM提供外部世界知识，但将其文本推理应用于高度抽象的表格概念存在模态鸿沟，嵌入LLM到迭代优化中会带来不可接受的计算开销。

Method: GRACE框架通过多视角LLM查询策略实现属性值级别的语义获取，将异构值映射到知识增强的描述中。关键创新在于一次性语义接地策略，提取通用语义表示并将异构属性嵌入统一空间，将昂贵的LLM调用与迭代优化解耦。同时通过数据集内部统计证据进行交叉验证，确保与特定数据集聚类结构对齐。

Result: GRACE在与传统统计驱动基线相同可扩展性的前提下，在11种竞争方法中实现了更优的聚类准确性和概念可解释性。

Conclusion: 该方法成功解决了语义增强与可扩展性之间的权衡问题，为混合数据聚类提供了新的解决方案，源代码已公开发布供社区验证和改进。

Abstract: Clustering mixed tabular data requires a unified metric space to bridge the inherent heterogeneity between continuous numerical measurements and discrete categorical symbols. Traditionally, algorithms rely entirely on dataset-internal statistics to estimate categorical relationships, which confines the learned metric to empirical co-occurrences and ignores conceptually obvious yet statistically unobserved affinities. Although LLMs offer external world knowledge, applying their text-centric reasoning to highly abstract tabular concepts presents significant challenges. Bridging this modality gap to construct a semantically complete metric typically requires embedding LLMs into iterative metric learning loops to dynamically optimize cross-modality representations. This incurs intractable computational overhead, forcing a compromise between semantic enrichment and scalability. Therefore, we propose GRACE, an LLM-grounded framework for scalable mixed-data clustering. GRACE shifts semantic acquisition to the attribute-value level via a multi-perspective LLM querying strategy, mapping heterogeneous values into knowledge-informed descriptions. Crucially, this one-shot grounding extracts general-purpose semantic representations that embed heterogeneous attributes into a unified space, decoupling expensive LLM invocation from iterative optimization. Furthermore, GRACE cross-validates these external semantics against dataset-internal statistical evidence to ensure alignment with the dataset-specific cluster structure. Ultimately, GRACE matches the scalability of conventional statistics-driven baselines while achieving superior clustering accuracy and conceptual interpretability over 11 competing methods. The source code is available at https://github.com/develop-yang/GRACE-GRACE-A

</details>


### [206] [Reason Wide, Not Deep: Amortizing the Reasoning Premium into Distilled Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07885)
*Agamdeep Singh,Srishti Gautam,Priyanshu Gupta,Nikita Mehrotra,Tanmay Bakshi,Sumit Gulwani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出通过从少量轨迹中蒸馏自然语言技能并注入非推理模型，可将推理模型在多步智能体任务上的优势大部分转移到非推理模型上，同时大幅降低输出令牌消耗，且无需依赖推理轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理模式在多步智能体任务上虽优于非推理模型，但每轮需多消耗3-6倍输出令牌，其中大量用于重复推导跨任务共享的程序。这种高昂的重复成本亟需优化。

Method: 研究者设计了一个编码代理，该代理分析训练分割中的一小批既有轨迹，编译出紧凑的自然语言技能，并将其注入到非推理模型的系统提示中。通过对比使用推理与非推理轨迹蒸馏出的技能，评估其在不同基准测试上的表现。

Result: 在四个智能体基准测试（ALFWorld、tau²-bench的电信与零售、SpreadsheetBench-Verified）上，注入技能后的非推理模型恢复了对GPT-5.4-mini推理差距的55%至100%以上，在四个基准中的两个上甚至超过了推理模式。同时，输出令牌减少了2.7-6倍，且无需任何推理令牌。仅基于非推理轨迹蒸馏的技能与基于配对推理/非推理语料蒸馏的技能表现相当，具体差异因领域而异。

Conclusion: 通过研究可得出，测试时推理（每次部署重新进行深度搜索）与语料蒸馏（跨任务一次性广度搜索）能够恢复重叠的程序化知识。在多数情况下，基于廉价轨迹的广度搜索是更优选择，但在电信和电子表格等特定领域，仍需要针对实例的深度搜索来弥补剩余差距。

Abstract: Reasoning modes of language models outperform their non-reasoning counterparts on multi-step agentic tasks, but pay a 3-6x premium in output tokens on every episode -- much of it spent re-deriving procedures that are shared across episodes of the same domain. We show this recurring cost can be amortized: a coding agent analyses a small corpus of existing trajectories from a training split and compiles a compact natural-language skill that is injected into the non-reasoning model's system prompt. Across four agentic benchmarks (ALFWorld, tau$^2$-bench telecom and retail, and SpreadsheetBench-Verified), skills recover 55%-100%+ of the reasoning gap for GPT-5.4-mini on held-out tasks -- exceeding the reasoning mode outright on two of four -- while emitting 2.7-6x fewer output tokens and zero reasoning tokens. Notably, reasoning traces are not a prerequisite: skills distilled from non-reasoning trajectories alone remain competitive with skills distilled from paired reasoning/non-reasoning corpora, with domain-dependent differences between the two sources. We interpret these results through a search lens: test-time reasoning is deep search inside a single episode, re-paid at every deployment, while corpus distillation is wide search across episodes, paid once. The two recover overlapping procedural knowledge, and width over cheap trajectories is often the better buy -- with the residual gap on some domains (telecom, SpreadsheetBench) delineating where genuinely per-instance deep search remains necessary.

</details>


### [207] [TelemetrySuffBench: Is Agent Telemetry Sufficient for Failure-Origin Diagnosis?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07899)
*Yuxuan Zhu,Peng Pu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TelemetrySuffBench是评估多组件代理系统遥测数据充分性的控制基准，区分故障检测、起源定位和安全弃权，揭示检测与定位之间存在显著差距，且模型在证据不足时的弃权能力差异明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理系统虽暴露执行跟踪，但揭示故障的遥测数据往往不足以精确定位故障起源，亟需区分故障检测、起源定位和证据不足时的安全弃权能力。

Method: 构建TelemetrySuffBench基准，设计多组件轨迹及延迟绑定故障，渲染为粗粒度视图、七因素遥测掩码和精确相等模糊起源对；使用统一协议、显式候选集、无效输出统计、子群分析及冻结盲测评估五个前沿语言模型。

Result: 全遥测下各模型起源步骤Top-1准确率为33.8%–97.2%；元数据、OpenTelemetry及OpenInference视图维持99.5%–100%检测F1，但起源步骤准确率≤0.5%，暴露出稳健的检测-定位差距。因子消融显示，移除决策内容使所有模型起源步骤准确率降为零，移除溯源信息则导致模型依赖性的显著损失。在模糊输入场景，证据门控使三个模型的不支持唯一起源回答减少12.5–48.6个百分点，但两个模型仍回答所有情况，显示出强模型依赖的安全弃权能力。冻结盲测复现了该模式。

Conclusion: 终端状态信息足以支持故障检测，但可靠的因果归因需要明确的决策-溯源链接以及跨模型均有效的弃权保障机制。

Abstract: Agent systems increasingly expose execution traces, yet telemetry that reveals a failure may still be inadequate for identifying where that failure originated. We introduce TelemetrySuffBench, a controlled benchmark that separates failure detection, fault-origin localization, and safe abstention under insufficient evidence. The benchmark constructs canonical multi-component traces with delayed-binding faults and renders them as paired coarse views, seven-factor telemetry masks, and exact-equal ambiguous origin pairs. We evaluate five frontier language models using unified protocols, explicit candidate sets, invalid-output accounting, subgroup analyses, and a frozen blind holdout. With full telemetry, origin-step Top-1 accuracy ranges from 33.8% to 97.2% across models. Metadata, OpenTelemetry-compatible, and OpenInference-compatible views retain 99.5% to 100% detection F1 while limiting origin-step accuracy to at most 0.5%, exposing a robust detection-localization gap. Factor ablations further show that removing decision content reduces origin-step accuracy to zero for every model, while provenance removal also causes large model-dependent losses. On rich ambiguous inputs that require abstention, evidence gating reduces unsupported unique-origin answers by 12.5 to 48.6 percentage points for three models, whereas two models still answer every case, revealing strong model dependence in safe abstention. Results on the frozen holdout reproduce the central pattern within the same generator family. These findings show that terminal status can support detection, whereas reliable causal attribution requires explicit decision-to-provenance links and abstention safeguards that remain effective across models. The dataset and benchmark implementation are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/TelemetrySuffBench-E635/README.md.

</details>


### [208] [GraphThink: Graph-Enhanced LLM Thinking for Long-Horizon Embodied Task Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07905)
*Chen Li,Sijie Cheng,Yuelin Zhang,Junxi Li,Maozhi Huang,Yang Liu,Wenbing Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GraphThink是一个新颖框架，通过整合任务图和场景图来增强基于LLM的具身智能体规划器，解决物理幻觉、长时序任务泛化性差和环境感知不足的问题，在ALFRED基准上达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM规划器的具身智能体面临三大挑战：（1）物理幻觉问题——生成不符合物理规律的计划；（2）长时序任务泛化能力差；（3）缺乏环境感知能力，无法适应动态环境变化。

Method: 提出GraphThink框架，包含三个核心组件：①任务图（Task Graph）：提供结构化知识指导LLM通过上下文提示和迭代优化进行规划，缓解规划幻觉；②基于GRPO框架的奖励设计：利用任务图为LLM规划器提供细粒度奖励信号，提升长时序规划能力；③事件驱动的重新规划模块：基于场景图维护环境记忆，实现闭环环境感知和错误纠正。

Result: 在ALFRED基准上达到最先进性能；高级规划器在验证集和长期任务上超越领先的基于API的LLM，展现出强大的零样本和少样本能力；额外评估显示对新颖任务和环境的强分布外泛化能力。

Conclusion: GraphThink通过任务图和场景图的协同整合，有效解决了LLM规划器的物理幻觉、长时序泛化不足和环境感知缺失问题，实现了鲁棒的具身智能规划能力，具有显著的学术价值和实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Embodied agents using LLM-based planners often struggle with physical hallucinations, poor generalization to long-horizon tasks, and lack of environmental awareness. We propose GraphThink, a novel framework that integrates a task graph to provide structured knowledge for robust planning and a scene graph to maintain environmental memory for event-driven replanning. Specifically, the task graph guides LLM thinking through contextual prompting and iterative refinement, effectively mitigating planning hallucinations. Furthermore, within the GRPO framework, the task graph offers delicate reward design to train the LLM planner, enhancing long-horizon planning capabilities and improving generalization. Finally, an event-driven replanning module, powered by the scene graph, enables closed-loop environment awareness and error correction. GraphThink achieves state-of-the-art performance on the ALFRED benchmark. In particular, our high-level planner surpasses leading API-based LLMs on both the validation set and held-out long-horizon tasks, underscoring its robust zero-shot and few-shot capabilities. Additional evaluations further demonstrate strong out-of-distribution generalization to novel tasks and environments.

</details>


### [209] [When Is Benchmark Contamination Detectable? Information Limits and Power-Calibrated Audits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07914)
*Ibne Farabi Shihab,Sanjeda Akter,Anuj Sharma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了行为污染检测器的区分能力，形式化了"无证据"的两种原因（基准干净vs审计能力不足）。通过二阶矩分析证明可检测性由α·ρ·√m控制，任何标量检测器可简化为其功效ef。经验上，冻结校准功效可预测留出功率曲线，但功效-高斯预算在小样本下校准失败。提出预声明的两阶段规划器修复了预算问题，并开发样本分割证书分布自由地下界α。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为污染检测器返回"无证据"可能因为基准干净，也可能因为审计能力不足。需要形式化区分这两种情况，为检测器的可靠性提供理论保障。

Method: 1. 形式化框架：假设未知比例α的条目在训练中见过，构建稀疏混合Q_α=(1-α)P_0+αP_1
2. 二阶矩论证：证明可检测性由α·ρ·√m控制，其中ρ²=χ²(P_1‖P_0)
3. 检测器简化：任何标量检测器可简化为功效ef=|E₁f-E₀f|/√(Var₀(f))≤ρ
4. 样本分割证书：开发分布自由的下界α方法，无需方向性假设
5. 两阶段规划器：预声明的模拟部署测试的规划器

Result: 经验发现呈两面性：(1)冻结校准功效可预测留出功率曲线，R²=0.83-0.98（六个精确置换通道）；(2)仅功效的高斯预算在小样本下校准失败，9/9个门通过通道失败，尽管功效本身可传输；(3)失败在于反转而非校准；(4)两阶段规划器修复了预算，统一保守，当探测不传输时弃权；(5)证书有效但在审计规模下空洞；(6)五种子配对注入研究恢复了机制排序：原文>意译>表面。

Conclusion: 非拒绝结论只有在与其产生的功效、预算和有效性门控一起考虑时才可解释。本文报告审计契约及其失败：仅功效的预算在小样本下校准失败，需要预声明的两阶段规划器来修复。检测器的可靠性不仅取决于其统计性能，还取决于对校准和预算的正确理解。

Abstract: Behavioral contamination detectors can return "no evidence" either because a benchmark is clean or because the audit has little power. We formalize this distinction for a benchmark in which an unknown fraction alpha of items was seen during training. With matched clean and seen controls, the behavioral channel is the sparse mixture Q_alpha = (1 - alpha) P_0 + alpha P_1, and an exact second-moment argument shows that detectability is governed by alpha * rho * sqrt(m), where rho^2 = chi^2(P_1 || P_0) measures behavioral separability. Any scalar detector reduces to its efficacy, ef = |E_1 f - E_0 f| / sqrt(Var_0(f)) <= rho, which can be estimated from controls before the audit is run. A separate sample-split certificate lower-bounds alpha distribution-free, without requiring an orientation assumption. Our empirical finding is two-sided. Frozen calibration efficacy predicts held-out power curves, with R^2 = 0.83-0.98 across six exact-permutation channels, but the efficacy-only Gaussian budget is miscalibrated at the small sample sizes it prescribes, failing in 9/9 gate-passing channels even though efficacy itself transports. The failure is in the inversion, not the calibration. A predeclared two-stage planner that simulates the deployed test repairs the budgets, is uniformly conservative, and abstains when its probe does not transport. The certificate is valid but vacuous at audit scale, and a five-seed paired injection study recovers the mechanism ordering verbatim > paraphrase > surface, in which the apparent answer-only signal is explained by baseline drift. We report the audit contract and its failures together: a non-rejection is interpretable only alongside the efficacy, budget, and validity gates that produced it.

</details>


### [210] [TongGuOCR: A Layout-Aware and Token-Augmented OCR Framework for Chinese Historical Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07917)
*Zhongheng Zhou,Yi Sun,Huiguo He,Yuyi Zhang,Peirong Zhang,Yulin Fang,Dezhi Peng,Minghui Liao,Lianwen Jin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TongGuOCR是一种面向中文历史文献的布局感知与标记增强OCR框架，通过构建局部连贯识别块与字符级词汇扩展、行间转移建模，有效应对复杂版面、稀有字符和复杂阅读顺序问题，在两个中文历史文献OCR基准上均超越现有传统OCR和多模态大模型，实现了更高的识别准确率与阅读顺序保持能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中文历史文献蕴含重要文化遗产，但多数仍以扫描件形式存在，缺乏全文检索、校勘与计算分析能力。传统OCR在处理复杂版面、稀有字符与非平凡阅读顺序时准确性不足，亟需一种更鲁棒的识别框架以弥合这一差距。

Method: TongGuOCR包含两个核心模块：1) 布局感知预处理模块，用于构造并细化局部连贯的识别块，在保留局部上下文的同时减少区域间干扰；2) 标记增强识别模块，从字符级和行间两个层面增强转录目标——字符级词汇扩展为每个稀有字形提供直接的一标记表示以缩短解码路径，行间转移建模注入离散空间位移标记以引导解码器沿复杂阅读路径工作，无需精确坐标。

Result: 在两个中文历史文献OCR基准测试上，TongGuOCR均优于代表性传统任务专用OCR模型、通用多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）及OCR导向MLLMs。在更具挑战性的M5HisDoc基准上，TongGuOCR取得93.76%的AR（字符识别准确率），将NED（归一化编辑距离）从10.43降至6.15，RO-ED（阅读顺序编辑距离）从7.53降至3.49，各项指标均较最佳竞争模型有显著改善。

Conclusion: 作者提出了一种布局感知与标记增强的中文历史文献OCR框架TongGuOCR，通过预处理构建局部连贯识别块、字符级词汇扩展与行间转移建模，显著提升识别准确率与阅读顺序保持能力，相比传统OCR、通用多模态大模型及OCR专用大模型均取得领先，为中文历史文献的数字化与计算分析提供了更优的解决方案。

Abstract: Chinese historical documents preserve valuable cultural heritage, but many collections remain accessible only as scanned page images, preventing full-text retrieval, collation, and computational analysis. Optical character recognition (OCR) can bridge this gap, but accurate transcription remains challenging because historical documents often contain complex layouts, rare characters, and nontrivial reading orders. We propose TongGuOCR, a layout-aware and token-augmented OCR framework for Chinese historical documents. First, a Layout-Aware Preprocessing module constructs and refines locally coherent recognition blocks to preserve local context while reducing interference across regions. Second, a Token-Augmented Recognition module augments the transcription target at two complementary levels: character-level vocabulary expansion gives each rare glyph a direct one-token representation and shortens its decoding path, while line-to-line transition modeling injects discrete spatial displacement tokens that guide the decoder along complex reading paths without requiring precise coordinates. Experiments on two Chinese historical document OCR benchmarks show that TongGuOCR outperforms representative traditional task-specific OCR models, general-purpose multimodal large language models (MLLMs), and OCR-oriented MLLMs. On the more challenging M5HisDoc benchmark, TongGuOCR achieves 93.76 AR and reduces NED from 10.43 to 6.15 and RO-ED from 7.53 to 3.49 relative to the best competing score for each metric. An online demo is available at https://jzzh2004.github.io/TongGuOCR.

</details>


### [211] [ZhuLong: Execution-Grounded LLM Agent for EDA Scripting with Offline API Self-Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07925)
*Yang Liu,Shiwei Hou,Xiyuan Chen,Yu Wang,Sen Yuan,Qirui Gan,Shao You,Feifan Chen,Wencheng Li,Shuyang Hu,Yongzhou Liu,Emma Xia,Xiaojing Lu,Hao Wang,Fan Xu,Yanfeng Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出ZhuLong，一种面向EDA脚本编写（PyAether和SKILL）的执行接地LLM编码代理。系统通过结合API检索、文档检查、沙盒执行及离线API自探索机制，解决了特定工具且无文档API的长尾瓶颈。在EDA-Eval-PyAether基准测试中达到78.5% Pass@1，远超纯LLM基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EDA脚本编写中依赖特定工具且缺乏文档的API构成了现有LLM难以处理的长尾瓶颈。

Method: 构建ZhuLong系统，利用统一MCP工具整合API检索、文档检查与沙盒执行。创新性地引入离线API自探索机制，通过反事实实验推断未记录API行为。

Result: 在158个任务的EDA-Eval-PyAether基准上，ZhuLong获得78.5% Pass@1（基线23.6%）。消融实验表明沙盒执行是关键驱动力（移除后下降41.2pp），自探索机制带来3.2pp提升及22.1%工具调用减少。在20个交互式任务中，PyAether和SKILL分别达60.0%和50.0% Pass@1。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了执行接地LLM代理结合自探索机制在处理复杂EDA工具链时的有效性，显著提升了代码生成性能并降低了工具调用成本。

Abstract: EDA scripting with tool-specific, often undocumented APIs remains a long-tail bottleneck that existing LLMs fail to address. This paper presents ZhuLong, an execution-grounded LLM coding agent for PyAether and SKILL that combines API retrieval, documentation inspection, and sandbox execution via unified MCP tools, augmented by an offline API self-exploration mechanism that infers undocumented API behaviors through counterfactual experimentation.
  We evaluate ZhuLong on EDA-Eval-PyAether, a benchmark of 158 real-world tasks with assertion-based execution, where the complete system achieves 78.5% Pass@1 in the commercial Empyrean Aether environment, substantially outperforming a pure LLM baseline (23.6%). Ablation studies identify sandbox execution as the dominant performance driver (41.2 pp drop when removed), with the self-exploration mechanism contributing an additional 3.2 pp accuracy gain and a 22.1% reduction in per-task tool calls. On 20 interactive tasks involving unsaved layouts and schematics, ZhuLong achieves 60.0% Pass@1 for PyAether and 50.0% for SKILL.

</details>


### [212] [REIN: Bridging the Gap between Reasoning and Reliability via Reflection and Abstention Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07931)
*Zhengze Huang,Luyang Yu,Di Hong,Xinzhe Huang,Wanyu Lin,Zhixuan Chu,Zhan Qin,Tianhang Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 超大推理模型(LRMs)易产生幻觉，REIN通过结构化推理序列和奖励机制，在单次前向传播中显著降低幻觉并提升选择性准确率，同时保持高覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LRMs的幻觉源于推理错误和知识缺失，影响可靠性和安全部署。

Method: REIN训练LRMs生成"思考→反思→回答"的结构化推理，引入奖励机制鼓励在无有效推理链时明确放弃，无需过程监督或多轮批评。

Result: 在数学和常识推理基准上，REIN选择性准确率提升6.6–14.2%，幻觉代理降低58–72%，覆盖率保持86–91%。

Conclusion: REIN能有效缓解LRMs的推理和知识幻觉，以低开销实现显著性能提升，为可靠部署提供可行方案。

Abstract: Large reasoning models (LRMs) are prone to hallucination, which undermines their reliability and poses challenges for safe deployment. Hallucinations in LRMs arise from two distinct failure sources: reasoning hallucination, where flawed inference steps propagate to an incorrect conclusion, and knowledge hallucination, where the model lacks the requisite factual knowledge to answer the query. To address reasoning hallucination, we propose REIN, an alignment framework that trains LRMs to produce a structured reasoning sequence, $\texttt{<think>} $$\rightarrow$ $\texttt{<reflection>} $$\rightarrow$ $\texttt{<answer>}$, enabling explicit self-reflection before committing to a final answer. To address knowledge hallucination, REIN introduces a reward mechanism that encourages explicit abstention (e.g., "I don't know") when none of the sampled reasoning chains yields a correct answer, allowing the model to refrain from unsupported predictions. Extensive evaluations on mathematical and commonsense reasoning benchmarks show that REIN consistently improves selective accuracy, reduces incorrect-but-self-endorsed responses, and maintains high coverage compared with competitive baselines. Notably, REIN achieves these gains within a single forward pass, without requiring process supervision, inference-time controllers, external search, or multi-round critiques. Experiments on multiple backbones show that REIN reduces the hallucination proxy by $58\sim72\%$ relative to the base models while maintaining $86\sim91\%$ average coverage, and improves selective accuracy on attempted questions by $6.6\sim14.2\%$.

</details>


### [213] [Locating Failure in Multi-Page Visually Rich Document Understanding: An Empirical Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07943)
*Lewei Xu,Yihao Ding,Zihan Xu,Daniel Yitian Su,Daochang Liu,Siwen Luo,Yifan Peng,Wei Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了多页视觉丰富文档理解(MP-VRDU)的核心挑战，识别出三种失败模式(表示、选择、推理)，通过受控实验发现：视觉信息必要但不能替代文本提取，缺失页面是影响准确性的主要瓶颈，推理器即使获得完整信息也难以跨页整合证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多页视觉丰富文档理解面临证据稀疏、跨页分布且常超出模型上下文窗口的挑战。先前工作提出了相互竞争但未充分验证的系统构建主张，需要系统的实证研究来澄清哪些因素真正影响系统性能。

Method: 研究团队将错误答案归因于三种失败模式(表示、选择、推理)，并在多页文档理解数据集上进行了受控实验——通过干预其中一种失败模式而保持其他两种不变，从而隔离各模式的影响。他们还测试了提示技术对推理行为的改变效果。

Result: (1) 视觉信息是必要的但不能替代文本提取；(2) 缺失页面严重制约准确性，而干扰项影响较小；(3) 推理器即使获得完整信息也难以跨页整合证据；(4) 提示能显著改变推理行为，改善某些结果的同时牺牲其他结果。

Conclusion: 基于上述发现，研究提出了在固定计算预算约束下构建多页视觉文档理解系统的实用指导原则，强调需要平衡视觉与文本处理、优先解决页面覆盖问题、并优化跨页证据整合机制。

Abstract: Multi-page visually-rich document understanding (MP-VRDU) requires managing evidence that is sparse, spread across pages, and often exceeds a model's context window. Prior work has produced competing, largely untested claims about how these systems should be built. We attribute incorrect answers to three failure modes, representation, selection, and reasoning, and isolate each over a multi-page document understanding dataset by intervening on one while holding the others fixed. We find that vision is necessary but does not replace text extraction, that missing pages bound accuracy while distractors cost little, and that reasoners fail to integrate evidence across pages even when it is fully supplied. Prompting can shift reasoning behaviour substantially, improving some outcomes at the expense of others. We translate these findings into guidance for building such systems under a fixed compute budget.

</details>


### [214] [Directed Neuro-Symbolic Stochastic Execution for Verification of Distributed Parallel AI Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07947)
*Gautham Koorma,Vikas Sharma,George Edwards,Mahdi Eslamimehr*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DNSSE混合测试框架，结合LLM引导的调度预测、符号约束求解和覆盖率引导的随机变异，解决了分布式并行AI程序因非确定性执行、高维输入和非线性操作导致的传统测试失效问题。实验显示该框架在五个分布式AI基准测试中检测到比最强基线多2.9%的并发bug，分支覆盖率从68.6%提升至91.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式并行AI程序暴露出传统测试无法弥补的可靠性缺口：并行执行具有非确定性特征，而AI工作负载带来高维输入和非线性操作，这些特性使得单独使用模糊测试和符号执行失效。

Method: 提出DNSSE（有向神经符号随机执行）混合测试框架，该框架将基于大语言模型（LLM）的调度预测与符号约束求解及覆盖率引导的随机变异相结合。将分布式AI执行建模为非确定转换系统，用线性时序逻辑规范正确性，并证明混合求解器的健全性、有界完备性和概率完备性，同时提供LLM引导调度探索的期望成本分析。

Result: 在PyTorch和Ray上的可扩展实现能够比最强基线多检测2.9%的并发bug，并在五个真实分布式AI基准测试中将平均分支覆盖率从68.6%提升至91.6%。

Conclusion: 该研究成功构建了一种创新的混合测试方法，有效解决了分布式并行AI程序的测试难题，通过理论证明和实验验证展示了其在检测并发bug和提升代码覆盖率方面的显著优势。

Abstract: Distributed parallel Artificial Intelligence (AI) programs expose reliability gaps that conventional testing cannot close: parallel executions are non-deterministic, and AI workloads bring high-dimensional inputs and non-linear operations that defeat fuzzing and symbolic execution in isolation. We present Directed Neuro-Symbolic Stochastic Execution (DNSSE), a hybrid testing framework that couples schedule prediction guided by a Large Language Model (LLM) with symbolic constraint solving and coverage-guided stochastic mutation. We model distributed AI executions as non-deterministic transition systems, specify correctness in linear temporal logic, and prove soundness, bounded completeness, and probabilistic completeness of the hybrid solver, together with an expected-cost analysis of LLM-guided schedule exploration. A scalable implementation on PyTorch and Ray detects 2.9% more concurrency bugs than the strongest baseline and raises average branch coverage from 68.6 % to 91.6 % across five realistic distributed AI benchmarks.

</details>


### [215] [Guixu: Valuation-Driven Data Discovery for Autonomous AI Agents with On-Chain Attestation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07949)
*Yifan Wu,Yuchen Peng,Jiaqi Chai,Yufei Qian,Xilin Li,Ke Chen,Lidan Shou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Guixu是一个面向自主智能体的估值驱动数据发现系统，通过三阶段估值流水线、代理标签传播和多轮背包优化实现任务感知数据估值，集成智能体支付协议支持预算约束下的数据采购，利用链上数据市场和认证信号实现可验证数据发现，使智能体能够超越关键词检索进行任务和预算感知的数据发现与采购。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据发现系统主要面向检索，仅从异构源中展示候选数据集，但缺乏对任务特定效用的估计、预算约束下成本效益数据集选择以及来自先前使用的可信反馈的支持。

Method: 提出Guixu系统，采用三阶段估值流水线：(1)代理标签传播进行任务感知数据估值；(2)多轮背包优化算法实现成本效益选择；(3)集成智能体支付协议支持预算约束下的数据采购工作流；(4)利用链上数据市场和认证信号实现可验证数据发现。

Result: 演示展示了Guixu如何使智能体超越关键词基于的数据集检索，转向任务和预算感知、可信的数据发现与采购，支持从自然语言任务规范和多源搜索到数据估值和可验证交易反馈的完整工作流。

Conclusion: Guixu系统成功实现了估值驱动的数据发现方法，解决了传统检索导向系统的局限性，为自主智能体提供了任务感知、预算优化和可验证的数据发现与采购能力，为数据发现系统的未来发展提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Autonomous agents increasingly rely on external data to complete downstream tasks such as model training and decision support. However, existing data discovery systems remain largely retrieval-oriented: they surface candidate datasets from heterogeneous sources, but provide limited support for estimating task-specific utility, selecting cost-effective datasets under budget constraints, or incorporating trustworthy feedback from prior usage. This paper presents Guixu, a valuation-driven data discovery system for autonomous agents. Guixu employs a three-phase valuation pipeline with proxy-label propagation and multi-round knapsack optimization for task-aware data valuation. Guixu integrates agentic payment protocol to enable budget-constrained data procurement workflows. Guixu leverages on-chain data market and attestation signals for verifiable data discovery. Our demonstration highlights how Guixu enables an agent to move beyond keyword-based dataset retrieval toward task- and budget-aware, trustworthy data discovery and procurement. Attendees can interactively explore the full workflow, from NL task specification and multi-source search to data valuation and verifiable transaction feedback.

</details>


### [216] [KGCache: Amortized Subgraph Retrieval for KG Reasoning with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07954)
*Uros Stanic,Changcheng Yuan,Sabuj Laskar,Ariful Azad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Large language models can answer knowledge-intensive questions more reliably when they are grounded with knowledge graphs, but systems such as Think-on-Graph and Reasoning-on-Graph repeatedly query the same graph neighborhoods across different questions. In this work, we study this repeated retrieval in Knowledge Graph Question Answering~(KGQA) workloads and propose KGCache, an in-memory cache for one-hop knowledge graph neighborhoods. KGCache is designed to be compatible with both iterative traversal (ToG) and one shot planning (RoG) KGQA paradigms. KGCache is placed between the KGQA engine and the backend serving the KG, so repeated entity requests can be served from cache instead of issuing new KG queries. We evaluate KGCache on WebQSP and CWQ using LRU, LFU, and a trace-aware Oracle policy. Our analysis shows that both datasets contain substantial entity reuse among starting entities and entities reached during traversal. We also explore semantic caching for similar queries, which shows additional hit-rate gains on WebQSP and needs further accuracy testing on CWQ. Entity caching accelerates KG retrieval by up to $1.91\times$, while semantic-context caching achieves up to $1.06\times$ full-system speedup in the evaluated WebQSP configurations, with each hit being up to $3.73\times$ faster.

</details>


### [217] [Self-Evolving Neuro-Symbolic Skills for Tool-Augmented Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07955)
*Shi-Yu Tian,Zhuo-Xia Wang,Xuan-Yi Zhu,Zhi Zhou,Xinwei Yang,Kun-Yang Yu,Ming Yang,Yang Chen,Yu-Feng Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了NeSy-Spatial，一种神经符号框架，通过将工具交互和几何操作抽象为可执行的类型化指令，并组合为工具使用技能和几何技能，通过轨迹分析实现技能的自演化，在空间推理任务中显著提升了准确性和工具利用精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉-语言模型在多模态推理中表现优异，但在需要精确空间感知和细粒度几何计算的细粒度空间任务上仍不可靠。现有的工具增强方法要么从头规划工具调用且缺乏显式依赖约束，要么依赖固定管道且冗余、泛化性差。

Method: NeSy-Spatial将工具交互和几何操作抽象为可执行的类型化原子指令，并组合为两种互补的技能类型：用于组织工具执行的工具使用技能和用于结构化几何推理的几何技能。推理时，在闭环过程中检索并执行相关技能；演化时，分析缓冲区中成功和失败的轨迹以优化技能结构并剪枝不可靠或无用的条目。

Result: 在三个空间推理基准上的实验表明，NeSy-Spatial持续提升推理准确率，并实现更精确的工具利用。

Conclusion: 该自演化神经符号框架通过可复用的技能积累和自适应组合，有效克服了现有方法在细粒度空间推理中的局限，为需要精确空间感知和几何计算的任务提供了更具泛化性和准确性的解决方案。

Abstract: Large vision-language models have achieved strong performance in multimodal reasoning, but they remain unreliable on fine-grained spatial tasks that demand both precise spatial perception and fine-grained geometric computation beyond end-to-end generation. Tool augmentation offers a natural solution, while existing methods either plan tool calls from scratch without explicit dependency constraints or rely on fixed pipelines that are redundant and generalize poorly across spatial tasks. An effective spatial reasoning agent should instead accumulate reusable experience and adaptively compose it for new problems. To this end, we propose NeSy-Spatial, a neuro-symbolic framework for self-evolving spatial skills. NeSy-Spatial abstracts tool interactions and geometric operations into typed executable atomic instructions and composes them into two complementary skill types: Tool-Use Skills for organizing tool execution and Geometry Skills for structured geometric reasoning. During inference, NeSy-Spatial retrieves and executes relevant skills in a closed-loop process. During evolution, it analyzes buffered successful and failed trajectories to refine skill structures and prune unreliable or inactive entries. Experiments on three spatial reasoning benchmarks show that NeSy-Spatial consistently improves reasoning accuracy with more precise tool utilization.

</details>


### [218] [SCOUT: Self-Checking and Recovery-Aware Tool-Thought Agents for Ultra-Long Egocentric Video Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07959)
*Keyang Zhong,Kuo Wang,Peng Liu,Quanlong Zheng,Junlin Xie,Zhijia Liang,Yanhao Zhang,Guanbin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SCOUT框架和UPS-GRPO训练方法，解决超长第一人称视频理解中时序稀疏证据推理的难题。通过自适应策略平衡探索与开发，实现鲁棒的多跳长程推理，并在超长视频基准测试中达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超长第一人称视频理解需要在数小时或数天内分布的时间稀疏证据上进行推理，这对现有具有有限上下文和关键视频段落定位能力的多模态模型构成挑战。虽然Chain-of-Tool-Thought (CoTT)代理系统实现了迭代检索和检查，但由于缺乏恢复机制的刚性zoom-in策略导致误差传播。此外，训练多轮工具使用代理仍存在挑战，现有强化学习方法依赖稀疏的结果级奖励，缺乏对扩展决策轨迹的监督，导致长程推理中的信用分配次优。

Method: 1) SCOUT框架：引入自适应策略评估中间工具观测，动态权衡开发(zoom-in)与探索(区域切换)，实现鲁棒的多跳长程推理。2) UPS-GRPO方法：优先级不确定的策略优化，将探索集中在高不确定性工具后状态，同时保持样本效率。3) 转向级优势分解：整合结果奖励与工具定位时间对齐奖励，改进信用分配机制。

Result: SCOUT在超长第一人称视频基准测试中达到最优性能，同时在较短时间跨度的长视频设置中保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 通过引入恢复感知代理框架SCOUT和创新训练方法UPS-GRPO，本研究有效解决了超长视频理解中的关键挑战，实现了在极长时间跨度上的鲁棒多跳推理能力，为第一人称视频理解领域提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Ultra-long egocentric video understanding requires reasoning over temporally sparse evidence distributed across hours or days, challenging current multimodal models with limited context and the grounding of key video segments. While Chain-of-Tool-Thought (CoTT) agent systems enable iterative retrieval and inspection, they suffer from error propagation due to rigid zoom-in strategies that lack recovery mechanisms. In this work, we address these challenges through SCOUT (Self-Checking Chain-Of-Tool-thought), a recovery-aware agentic framework introducing an adaptive policy that evaluates intermediate tool observations and dynamically trades off exploitation (zoom-in) and exploration (region switching), enabling robust multi-hop reasoning over extremely long horizons. However, training such multi-turn tool-using agents remains challenging, as existing RL methods rely on sparse outcome-level rewards and lack supervision over extended decision trajectories, resulting in suboptimal credit assignment for long-horizon reasoning. To address this, we develop UPS-GRPO, an uncertainty-prioritized policy optimization method that concentrates exploration on high-uncertainty post-tool states while preserving sample efficiency. We further introduce a turn-level advantage decomposition that integrates outcome rewards with tool-grounded temporal alignment rewards for improved credit assignment. Experiments show that SCOUT achieves state-of-the-art results on ultra-long egocentric benchmarks, while remaining competitive on shorter-horizon long-video settings.

</details>


### [219] [CyberAGENTS: Structured Autonomy for Agentic Gamified Learning in Cybersecurity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07965)
*Ivan Hornung,Deepthi Marasinghe Arachchige,Tharindu Kumarage,Garima Agrawal,Yuli Deng,Ying-Chih Chen,Huan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CyberAgents框架，通过能力本位进阶模型、行为模式约束和网络安全本体验证，解决生成式AI在教育中的风险问题，实现结构化的游戏化网络安全学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 游戏化学习在网络安全等需要主动问题解决和迭代技能建设的领域特别有效。生成式AI代理虽然能够规模化提供适应性学习体验，但在教育环境中存在已知的风险：行为不一致、推理幻觉以及与教学框架不对齐。因此，将这些系统扎根于学习科学至关重要。

Method: 研究提出了CyberAgents代理框架，该框架采用能力本位进阶模型（按难度和先决关系组织主题，反映支架式教学原则），将学习循环分解为四个专门代理（挑战、支持、评估、奖励），每个代理由行为模式治理（编码操作模式和进阶逻辑），并通过网络安全本体验证所有生成内容（强制领域一致的推理和安全约束）。评估方法包括本科生的课堂部署以及教育者和领域专家的专家评估。

Result: 结果显示，当行为模式和本体验证处于激活状态时，学习者的参与度提高，反馈解释更清晰，对AI生成响应的信任度更高。与无约束配置的初步比较进一步支持了结构化控制在稳定教学行为中的作用。

Conclusion: 该研究为设计教学扎根的代理游戏化学习系统提供了蓝图，证明了结构化控制机制在平衡生成式灵活性与教学可靠性方面的有效性。

Abstract: Gamification is especially effective in learning domains requiring active problem-solving and iterative skill-building, such as cybersecurity education. Generative AI agents offer a path to delivering such experiences adaptively at scale, but introduce well-documented risks in educational settings: inconsistent behavior, hallucinated reasoning, and misalignment with pedagogical frameworks. Grounding these systems in learning science is therefore essential. We present \model, an agentic framework for gamified cybersecurity learning that enables structured autonomy through ontology-guided validation, schema-governed behavioral control, and competency-based progression. The system is organized around a competency-based progression model that structures topics by difficulty and prerequisite relationships, reflecting evidence-based principles of scaffolded instruction. The learning loop is decomposed into four specialized agents: challenge, support, evaluation, and reward, each governed by behavioral schemas that encode operational modes and progression logic, bounding agent autonomy without eliminating generative flexibility. A cybersecurity ontology validates all generated content prior to display, enforcing domain-consistent reasoning and safety constraints. We evaluate CyberAgents through classroom deployment with undergraduate students, complemented by expert evaluations from educators and domain specialists. Results indicate improved engagement, clearer feedback interpretation, and greater learner trust in AI-generated responses when behavioral schemas and ontology validation are active. Preliminary comparisons with an unconstrained configuration further support the role of structured control in stabilizing instructional behavior. These findings offer a blueprint for designing pedagogically grounded agentic gamified learning systems.

</details>


### [220] [VDGR-RAG: Vectors, Directories, Graphs, and Reflection Are All You Need for Unified Reasoning over Hierarchical Enterprise Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07994)
*Wenqi Chen,Haofei Yang,Rui Yang,Fangming Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对企业知识问答中现有RAG方法在领域路由、文档结构利用和推理能力上的不足，本文提出VDGR-RAG框架。该框架构建层级异构知识图谱(H²KG)保留目录结构和语义关系，通过目录增强路由、多路检索、目录回溯和动态反思四个原子工具实现统一检索，在四个无线领域产品文档上的实验表明其在检索召回率和问答准确性上显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索增强生成(RAG)对企业知识问答至关重要，尤其在电信等复杂产品文档领域。然而现有RAG方法大多忽略了多种检索优势的整合作用，导致领域路由不准确、层级文档结构利用不足，进而限制了企业知识推理能力。

Method: 提出VDGR-RAG——一种智能体GraphRAG系统。首先从文档片段构建层级异构知识图谱(H²KG)，以保留目录层级结构和语义关系；随后提供可自由组合的原子检索工具：(1)目录增强路由工具——利用目录结构将用户查询路由到相应领域H²KG；(2)多路检索工具——结合向量搜索、基于目录的智能体搜索和图搜索进行综合检索；(3)目录回溯工具——纠正知识定位偏差；(4)动态反思工具——迭代规划后续检索阶段。

Result: 在四个无线领域(如节能和故障管理)的企业产品文档上进行广泛实验。结果表明，该方法在知识检索召回率和问答准确性方面显著优于多种RAG基线方法。

Conclusion: VDGR-RAG通过整合向量检索、目录驱动推理、图遍历和迭代反思，有效解决了现有RAG方法在领域路由、文档结构利用和推理能力方面的局限，为企业知识问答提供了更准确的解决方案。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) is essential for enterprise knowledge question answering (QA), particularly in domains with complex product documentation like telecommunications. However, existing RAG approaches largely overlook the holistic integration of diverse retrieval strengths, leading to inaccurate domain routing, poor utilization of hierarchical document structures, and consequently limited reasoning capabilities over enterprise knowledge. To address these limitations, we present VDGR-RAG, which integrates vector retrieval, directory-driven reasoning, graph traversal, and iterative reflection in a unified framework for accurate enterprise knowledge QA. Specifically, VDGR-RAG is an agentic GraphRAG system that first constructs a Hierarchical Heterogeneous Knowledge Graph ($\text{H}^2$KG) from document chunks to preserve both hierarchical directory structures and semantic relationships, and then employs a set of atomic tools for knowledge retrieval that can be freely composed to navigate the $\text{H}^2$KG: (1) a directory-enhanced routing tool that uses table-of-contents (TOC) structures to route user queries to appropriate domain-specific $\text{H}^2$KGs; (2) a multi-route retrieval tool that combines vector search, TOC-based agentic search, and graph search for comprehensive knowledge retrieval; (3) a directory backtracking tool that corrects knowledge localization biases; and (4) a dynamic reflection tool that iteratively plans the next retrieval phase. We conduct extensive experiments on our enterprise product documents across four wireless domains (e.g., energy saving and fault management). Experimental results demonstrate that our method significantly outperforms a variety of RAG baselines in terms of both knowledge retrieval recall and QA accuracy.

</details>


### [221] [Thought-Level Beam Search for Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08020)
*Lijie Yang,Hongyin Luo,Tri Dao,Ravi Netravali*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Gambit是一种新的推理算法，通过思想级束搜索在测试时动态分配计算资源，在相同硬件约束下相比现有方法显著提升准确率（最高+6.7%）、吞吐量（>2倍）和降低token消耗（最高68.5%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型（LRMs）的性能主要依赖测试时计算扩展，但现有方法效率极低。关键问题已从"投入多少计算"转变为"将计算分配到哪里"。传统并行采样导致内存瓶颈，而剪枝方法浪费硬件资源且无法充分调整输出分布，需要一种能主动将计算分配到最有前景部分轨迹的新范式。

Method: 提出Gambit推理算法，执行思想级束搜索：通过周期性剪除有前景的轨迹并立即从高质量前缀分支，利用轻量级评分器探测隐藏状态，动态将计算集中到最有前景的推理轨迹上，同时维持持续高硬件利用率。

Result: 在多个模型和基准上，Gambit严格优于现有基线：相同硬件约束下，在HMMT-24上准确率绝对提升最高+6.7%，在AIME-25上提升+3.3%（相比剪枝基线）；推理完成吞吐量提高>2倍；相比标准并行采样，总token消耗最多减少68.5%。

Conclusion: Gambit通过测试时推理中的计算分配问题，实现了比传统并行采样和剪枝方法显著更优的性能，为大型推理模型的高效测试时计算扩展提供了新范式。

Abstract: Test-time compute scaling is a primary driver of performance in large reasoning models (LRMs), but extreme inefficiency bounds current approaches, shifting the critical question from \emph{how much} compute to spend, to \emph{where} to allocate it. We formalize test-time reasoning as a constrained compute allocation problem over partial trajectories. Under a fixed hardware budget, existing paradigms fail to actively allocate the compute to the most promising partial progress: traditional parallel sampling treats traces independently and induces severe memory bottlenecks, while subtractive pruning starves hardware and fails to actively and sufficiently shift the output distribution. To overcome this dichotomy, we introduce Gambit, an inference algorithm that executes \emph{thought-level beam search}. By periodically pruning unpromising trajectories and immediately branching from high-quality prefixes, Gambit dynamically concentrates compute onto the most promising reasoning traces via a light-weight scorer probing hidden states while maintaining continuous high hardware utilization. Extensive evaluations across multiple models and benchmarks demonstrate that Gambit strictly dominates existing baselines. Under identical hardware constraints, our method yields up to a +6.7\% absolute accuracy gain on HMMT-24 and +3.3\% on AIME-25 over pruning baselines, delivers $>2\times$ higher throughput on trace completion, and reduces total token consumption by up to 68.5\% relative to standard parallel sampling.

</details>


### [222] [Legal Responsibilities Using Autonomous Agents For Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08022)
*Mark Burgess*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨AI代理造成损害时的法律责任问题，利用Promise理论的下游原则来分配责任并提出限制AI自由的策略建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI代理意外突破限制并获得未经授权的访问权限的事件引发了关于损害后果法律责任归属的紧迫问题。

Method: 本文采用Promise理论，特别是因果影响的下游原则，系统性地解决责任归属问题。

Result: 分析表明，当责任追溯具有影响时，责任可扩展至AI代理，且通过政策选择可限制AI代理的行动自由。

Conclusion: 法律框架可通过Promise理论将AI代理纳入责任主体，并通过政策措施约束其行动以防止未经授权的损害。

Abstract: Recent incidents involving Artificial Intelligence (AI) agents, which were reported escaping their containment `unintentionally' to gain unauthorized access, pose looming questions about who or what should be held legally responsible for resultant criminal or negligent damage. As the independent capabilities of agents expand, Promise Theory suggests a systematic method to resolve these questions, based on the Downstream Principle for causal influence. Responsibility can easily be expanded to include AI agents where tracing responsibility becomes impactical, and agents' freedoms to act can be limtied by policy choices.

</details>


### [223] [The Authority Expectancy Effect in Multi-User Conflict](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08026)
*Eunna Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究揭示社交权威信号在大语言模型中并非简单加权，而是与严重性优先级以复杂方式交互，导致模型判断发生系统性重构。研究在四种主流LLM中验证了“权威预期效应”（AEE），指出该效应依赖上下文基线、会重解释证据含义，并对权威与证据的对齐方向高度敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多将权威信号视为对模型输出的简单加权，但实际交互可能更为复杂。本研究旨在揭示社交权威信号如何与基于严重性的优先级机制相互影响，探究模型是否以及在何种条件下会因权威线索而系统性重构其判断逻辑，进而超越单纯的加性重加权解释。

Method: 研究将两个核心维度——严重性排序与社交权威信号——分别操作化为模型自发的基线（分诊层级与权威层级）。实验在四个大型语言模型（Claude、Gemini、GPT、Grok）上展开，设置三个实验阶段：资源分配、故障归因、多轮争议调解。通过对照不同权威信号（职业权威、机构文件、关系一致性）与证据线索的组合，观察模型判断的变化模式。

Result: 实验发现，职业权威、机构文件与关系一致性三类社交权威信号能够以非加性方式重塑模型判断，即同等证据因承载方是否具有权威信号而产生不同推断含义。该模式被形式化为“权威预期效应”（AEE），并呈现三个关键特性：1) 参考依赖性：效应仅相对于权威介入前的基线判断而定义；2) 证据重解释性：相同内容因权威信号的归属不同而获得不同推断意义；3) 方向敏感性：权威位置与证据线索一致时产生与不一致时相反的模型输出。

Conclusion: 社交权威信号会通过与模型既有严重性排序的复杂交互，以非加性方式重塑大语言模型的判断；该效应（AEE）具有参考依赖性、证据重解释性和方向敏感性，表明模型在整合权威线索时并非简单加权，而是依赖情境与对齐方向产生差异化推断。

Abstract: We investigate how social authority (SA) signals interact with severity-based prioritization in large language models, operationalizing each axis as a model-elicited baseline -- the triage hierarchy and the SA hierarchy. Across four LLMs (Claude, Gemini, GPT, Grok) and three experimental phases -- resource allocation, fault attribution, and multi-turn dispute mediation -- we find that occupational authority, institutional documentation, and relational congruence can restructure model judgments in ways not captured by additive reweighting of authority cues. We formalize this pattern as the Authority Expectancy Effect (AEE) and characterize it through three properties observed across our conditions: it is reference-dependent, defined only relative to a pre-authority baseline; it involves evidential reinterpretation, in which identical content acquires different inferential implications depending on which party bears the SA signal; and it exhibits direction sensitivity, producing opposite outcomes depending on whether authority position and evidentiary cues align.

</details>


### [224] [Decided Upstream, Written Late: Locating and Pricing the Cross-Lingual Refusal Circuit of a Multilingual MoE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08032)
*Ramakrishna P. Kompella,Aadit Mahajan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 多语言模型的安全对齐存在显著差异：英文中可靠拒绝有害请求的模型可能在低资源语言中顺从相同请求。研究通过机制分析发现，危害检测本身是跨语言不变的（如印地语与英语在L11层余弦相似度≈0.9），但拒绝行为的生成由特定MoE写电路完成，该电路受注意力对立面抑制且生成过程依赖时序累积。干预成本差异明显：抑制对立面高效低成本，增强写电路成本极高，而针对性修改注意力头无效。此发现为多语言安全修复提供了架构相关的成本路径图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决多语言模型中安全对齐的不均衡问题——即模型在高资源语言（如英语）中能正确拒绝有害请求，但在低资源语言中可能失效的现象。研究旨在揭示其内在机制并量化不同干预策略的成本效益。

Method: 采用机械可解释性方法分析SARVAM（印地语多语言MoE推理模型），定位危害检测方向与拒绝生成电路。通过因果干预实验验证上游方向控制拒绝行为，识别出MoE写电路及其抑制机制（注意力对立面）。使用梯度暴露法解析电路结构，并在另一独立MoE模型中验证模式重复性，同时测试多种干预方式（抑制对立面、增强写电路、修改注意力头）。

Result: 1) 危害检测具有语言不变性（跨语言余弦相似度≈0.9），但拒绝行为由生成过程中晚期组装的特定MoE写电路执行；2) 该电路受注意力对立面抑制，抑制对立面是最优干预手段（低成本高效率）；3) 增强写电路成本过高，直接修改注意力头无效；4) 电路组织形式与梯度暴露方法在另一MoE模型中重现，但干预有效性因架构而异。

Conclusion: 为多语言安全修复提供了基于成本效益的精准定位方案：应优先通过抑制注意力对立面实现高效修复，避免高成本方案。研究揭示了多语言安全对齐差异的根源在于生成机制而非检测能力，为架构特定的安全优化提供了关键指导。

Abstract: Safety alignment in multilingual models is uneven: a model that reliably refuses a harmful request in English will often comply with the same request in a lower-resource language. We trace this gap mechanistically in sarvam, an Indic-multilingual mixture-of-experts reasoning model, and find it is not a failure to detect harm. Harm is encoded as an internal direction that is nearly language-invariant in mid-network (English-vs-Indic cosine ${\approx}0.9$ at $L11$), and steering that direction upstream causally controls refusal. But the detection direction is orthogonal to the change that actually writes the refusal, which is late and assembled over the course of generation rather than read off in a single forward pass. We attribute the write to a specific, localizable circuit, a mixture-of-experts writer held in check by an attention opposer and price every way of intervening on it: damping the opposer is cheap and effective, amplifying the writer is a cost wall, and surgical edits to the responsible heads do nothing. The circuit's organization, and the gradient method that exposes it, recur in a second, unrelated MoE model, while the lever's strength is architecture-specific. The result is a cost-measured map of where a multilingual safety repair can land, and what it costs

</details>


### [225] [SkillSmith: Enhancing Locally Deployed Agents via Automatic Skill Construction and Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08037)
*Xinle Jiang,Remy Xie,Ming Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SkillSmith框架，通过云-本地智能体协作自动构建并进化"技能"知识载体，使基于开源SLM的本地智能体在任务效果上媲美前沿LLM云智能体，显著减少操作步骤并具备模型泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于云LLM的智能体框架存在隐私泄露和重复调用成本问题；本地部署开源SLM的智能体虽解决部署问题，但因模型规模有限导致环境知识缺失，任务效果远逊于云智能体。

Method: 提出SkillSmith协作框架，将"技能"作为上下文高效的知识载体，利用云智能体任务探索自动构建技能，并通过本地智能体执行反馈进化技能，以增强冻结的本地智能体。

Result: 在AppWorld和WorkBench数据集上的实验表明，自动生成的技能使Qwen3.6-27B本地智能体达到与前沿LLM云智能体相当的任务效果，超越最强非参数基线，在AppWorld-Normal上将平均任务动作从36.1次减少到9.9次，且无需重新构建即可泛化到其他SLM骨干模型。

Conclusion: SkillSmith有效弥合了云智能体与本地智能体之间的效果差距，实现了非参数化、上下文高效且无需专家标注的环境知识补充，为隐私保护和成本可控的智能体部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: LLM-based agent frameworks now act as personal assistants for multi-step tasks. Existing agent frameworks such as OpenClaw commonly follow the Cloud Agent depolyment mode using closed-source cloud LLMs as backbone model, which may expose private user information and incur repeated LLM-calling costs. Local Agents address these deployment concerns by depolying frontier open-source SLMs on user-controlled devices, but their task effectiveness still lags far behind Cloud Agents. Through diagnostic analysis, we reveal that the limited effectiveness of Local Agents with frontier SLM backbones mainly comes from missing environment knowledge caused by limited backbone model scale including environment rules and operation procedures. To supply such knowledge non-parametrically, context-efficiently, and without expert authoring, we present SkillSmith, a Cloud--Local Agent collaboration framework that uses Skill as a context-efficient knowledge carrier, automatic constructs Skill from Cloud Agent task exploration and evolves Skill using Local Agent execution feedback to enhance a frozen Local Agent. Experiments on daily agent task datasets AppWorld and WorkBench show that the automatically generated Skill enables the Local Agent with Qwen3.6-27B(SLM) to achieve task effectiveness comparable to Cloud Agents with frontier LLMs, outperform the strongest non-parametric baselines, reduce average actions per task from 36.1 to 9.9 on AppWorld-Normal, and generalize to other SLM backbone models without rerunning Skill construction.

</details>


### [226] [Lingjing: A Simulation Testbed for Multi-Agent Embodied Tasks in Open-Ended Cities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08045)
*Xiaohe Li,Yiru Wang,Junhao Fan,Mingyuan Liu,Jie Huang,Kaixin Zhang,Jiahao Li,Chen Qian,Zide Fan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Lingjing是一个面向开放式城市环境中异构多智能体具身智能的仿真平台，支持可复现的端到端评估和系统性故障诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市具身智能需要异构智能体（如无人机、地面机器人、自动驾驶车辆）在动态城市中的协调。现有仿真平台将不同载体隔离，并将任务设计与评估解耦，缺乏统一的评估框架。

Method: 提出Lingjing平台：(1)从地理数据重构并渲染动态演化城市；(2)同步多个物理引擎；(3)向智能体暴露共享物理状态和结构化城市状态；(4)提供Gym式接口支持用户自定义ReAct智能体及单/多智能体自然语言任务；(5)支持可配置星型或广播通信及资源约束；(6)每个episode生成可归因重播，关联智能体轨迹、通信与关系图变化、资源消耗及引擎评估。

Result: (1)在共享的引擎闭环协议下评估了12个视觉语言模型在9个城市任务上的表现；(2)受控实验考察了通信、可扩展性、鲁棒性和故障溯源；(3)发现持续性瓶颈在于grounding和长周期执行；(4)揭示任务相关的协调权衡，以及增加容量后的边际效益递减；(5)更重的工作负载进一步降低成功率。

Conclusion: Lingjing提供了一个统一测试平台，能够实现城市多智能体具身智能的可复现端到端评估和系统性故障诊断，为异构智能体协调研究奠定基础。

Abstract: Urban embodied intelligence requires coordination among heterogeneous agents (e.g., UAVs, ground robots, and autonomous vehicles) in dynamic cities. Simulators therefore provide a scalable foundation for developing and evaluating such coordination. Existing platforms nevertheless isolate different embodiments and decouple them from task design and evaluation. We present \textbf{Lingjing}, a simulation platform for heterogeneous multi-agent embodied intelligence in open-ended urban environments. Lingjing reconstructs and renders evolving cities from geographic data, synchronizes multiple physics engines, and exposes shared physical and structured urban state to agents. Its Gym-like interface supports user-defined ReAct agents and single- or multi-agent natural-language missions with configurable star or broadcast communication and resource constraints. Each episode becomes an attribution-ready replay that links agent trajectories and communication to relation-graph changes, resource consumption, and engine-based evaluations for systematic diagnosis. We evaluate twelve vision-language models on nine urban tasks under a shared engine-in-the-loop protocol. Controlled studies further examine communication, scalability, robustness, and failure provenance. Results expose persistent bottlenecks in grounding and long-horizon execution. They also show task-dependent coordination trade-offs and diminishing returns from added capacity, while heavier workloads further reduce success. Lingjing provides a unified testbed that enables reproducible end-to-end evaluation and systematic failure diagnosis in urban multi-agent embodied intelligence.

</details>


### [227] [JustLLMGRPO: Radiographic Control for Chest X-Ray Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08046)
*Pengxiang Cai,Xiaohan Li,Anglin Liu,Qingyuan Zeng,Zexun Li,Jintai Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出在文本条件化胸部X光图像生成中，通过优化提示词而非改进生成器来提升图像质量。研究证明，使用LLM重构提示词可显著降低RadDINO-FID值，并引入JustLLMGRPO方法在保持源提示对齐的同时实现50.6%的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本条件化胸部X光图像生成工作主要关注改进图像生成器，隐式地将提示词视为固定。这种生成器中心视角留下了提示词优化的大量未开发维度。

Method: 使用已适应CXR领域的Sana生成器（保持冻结），采用两种方法：(1)直接使用LLM进行一次性重构；(2)提出JustLLMGRPO，仅对LLM提示策略应用标准组相对策略优化(GRPO)，同时保持生成器冻结，利用组相对放射学感知图像反馈。

Result: LLM重构将RadDINO-FID从54.225降至27.572，但降低了BioViL-T对齐度(0.695→0.609)。JustLLMGRPO在CheXGenBench上将RadDINO-FID降至26.780（相比直接提示提升50.6%），同时保持对齐度(0.696 vs 0.695)，实现最先进的分布覆盖和下游分类效用。

Conclusion: 大量性能潜力存在于如何向已适应生成器表达放射学信息的方式中。通过优化提示词而非改进生成器本身，可以显著提升图像生成质量，同时保持语义对齐。

Abstract: Text-conditioned chest X-ray generation aims to synthesize realistic radiographs that faithfully depict specified findings. Existing work has primarily improved quality by updating image generators, implicitly treating prompts as fixed after CXR-domain adaptation. We show that this generator-centric view leaves a substantial optimization dimension underexplored. With a CXR-adapted Sana generator frozen, one-pass reformulation by an unmodified LLM reduces RadDINO-FID from 54.225 to 27.572. Prompt analysis shows that the LLM suppresses temporal comparisons, uncertainty, and other non-renderable report content while emphasizing visible radiographic findings. However, unconstrained reformulation reduces BioViL-T alignment with source prompts from 0.695 to 0.609. We therefore introduce JustLLMGRPO, which applies standard Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) only to the LLM prompt policy while keeping Sana frozen. Group-relative radiology-aware image feedback retains visual focus while preserving source-prompt alignment. On CheXGenBench, JustLLMGRPO reduces RadDINO-FID to 26.780, a 50.6% improvement over direct prompting, while maintaining alignment (0.696 versus 0.695). It also achieves state-of-the-art distribution coverage and downstream classification utility. These results show that substantial performance can remain latent in how radiographic information is expressed to an adapted generator. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/pxcai/JustLLMGRPO.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [228] [FedSceneX: Time-to-Target Orchestration for Same-Scene Multimodal Federated Edge Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07730)
*Dhe Yeong Tchalla,Beining Wu,Jun Huang,Shuyang Gu,Qiang Duan*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出FedSceneX，一种通过基于每小时价值的定价策略优化多模态边缘设备训练轮次组成的联邦学习编排方法，显著降低每轮活跃时间并提升资源利用效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习评估通常以通信轮次为单位，但一轮并不代表固定工作量（相同硬件下不同方法每轮耗时3.3至9.8小时），导致基于轮次的比较具有误导性。对于多模态客户端（摄像头、视频、激光雷达、雷达），训练和通信成本差异显著，而现有方法将每轮的模态组成视为固定，未能优化资源利用。

Method: 提出FedSceneX编排器，联合决定轮次组成以最大化每小时学习价值。采用每小时价值定价(VHP)优化方法：通过参数变换转换分数目标函数，对上行链路约束进行对偶化，得到闭式客户端价格（权重反映资源影子成本）。基于价格，在模态覆盖约束下选择客户端，通过逆水填充分配精度，并将更新分配给边缘服务器。

Result: 在包含15个客户端和12个基线方法的完整nuScenes基准上，FedSceneX将每轮活跃时间降低至3.31小时（基线为4.85-9.78小时）。在所有随机种子下，在20小时预算内实现最高精度，同时保留全部四种模态。其优势在10至45小时区间持续，之后传统方法超过。

Conclusion: FedSceneX通过优化轮次组成和客户端选择策略，显著提升了联邦学习的资源利用效率和训练效率，在多模态边缘场景下优于现有方法。

Abstract: Federated learning at the sensing edge is typically evaluated by communication rounds, yet a round does not represent a fixed amount of work. Even on identical hardware, the methods we compare require 3.3 to 9.8 hours per round, which makes round-based comparisons misleading. The problem is more obvious for same-scene multimodal clients, since camera, video, LiDAR, and radar workloads differ substantially in training and communication cost, while existing methods treat the modality composition of each round as fixed. To address it, we introduce FedSceneX, an orchestrator that jointly determines round composition to maximize learning value per active hour. The optimization method, Value-per-Hour Pricing (VHP), converts the fractional objective through a parametric transformation and dualizes the uplink constraint, yielding a closed-form client price whose weights capture resource shadow costs. Based on these prices, FedSceneX selects clients subject to a modality coverage constraint, allocates precision through reverse water filling, and assigns updates to edge servers. On the full nuScenes benchmark with fifteen clients and twelve baselines, FedSceneX reduces the active time per round to 3.31 hours, compared with 4.85 to 9.78 hours for the baselines. Across all random seeds, it achieves the highest accuracy within a twenty-hour budget while preserving all four modalities. Its advantage persists from ten to forty-five hours, after which conventional methods overtake it.

</details>


### [229] [Coordinated Spectrum Coexistence Across Heterogeneous Commercial and Federal Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07789)
*Minh Dat Nguyen,Paolo Testolina,Pedram Johari,Michele Polese,Tommaso Melodia*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种统一框架，使蜂窝通信、射频感知、无线电导航和定位雷达四类服务在联邦子6GHz频谱上动态共享物理资源块，在满足异构QoS约束下最大化蜂窝系统加权和速率。通过交替优化和贪婪启发式算法求解混合整数非线性规划问题，波士顿数字孪生仿真表明该方法能显著提升频谱效率和蜂窝吞吐量，同时严格满足感知和定位QoS要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来无线网络需在拥挤的联邦子6GHz频谱上支持蜂窝通信、射频感知、无线电导航和定位雷达等多服务共存，这由激增的蜂窝流量需求、6G感知与定位服务普及以及不驱离现有用户的联邦频段再利用监管压力所驱动。

Method: 提出统一框架，在集中协调下使四类服务动态共享公共PRB池。建立以加权蜂窝加权和速率最大化为目标的优化模型，约束条件包括占空比、正交性、感知信噪比和基于Cramer-Rao下界的定位精度。采用交替优化方法在PRB分配、调度和功率分配上迭代求解，配合低复杂度QoS感知贪婪启发式算法。

Result: 在波士顿Twin城市数字孪生平台上进行特定场景射线追踪仿真，结果表明该方法在严格满足感知和定位QoS的前提下，实现了频谱效率和蜂窝吞吐量的显著提升。

Conclusion: 协调多服务共享架构是实现下一代军用和民用网络中蜂窝-联邦-雷达共存的有效方案。

Abstract: Future wireless networks are expected to support the coexistence of cellular communications, radio frequency (RF) sensing, radionavigation, and radiolocation radar-among others-over congested federal sub-6 GHz spectrum under heterogeneous Quality of Service (QoS) requirements, driven by escalating cellular traffic demand, the proliferation of 6G sensing and positioning services, and regulatory pressure to repurpose federal bands without displacing incumbents. We develop a unified framework in which all four services dynamically share a common Physical Resource Block (PRB) pool under centralized coordination, formulating weighted cellular sum-rate maximization subject to duty-cycle, orthogonality, sensing signal-to-noise ratio (SNR), and Cramer-Rao-based positioning constraints. The resulting Mixed-Integer Nonlinear Program (MINLP) is solved by alternating optimization across PRB assignment, scheduling, and successive convex approximation for power allocation, complemented by a low-complexity QoS-aware greedy heuristic. Site-specific ray-tracing simulations on the BostonTwin urban digital twin show substantial gains in spectrum efficiency and cellular throughput while strictly meeting sensing and positioning QoS, establishing coordinated multi-service sharing as a viable architecture for cellular-federal-radar coexistence in next-generation military and civilian networks.

</details>


### [230] [Orchestrated Vulnerability Management for Heterogeneous Networks: Adaptive Two-Stage Vulnerability Assessment, Context-Aware Risk Prioritization, and Automated Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08162)
*Ricardo Lopes,Jose Moura,Rui Neto Marinheiro*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于SOAR编排的漏洞管理框架，通过被动资产发现、自适应两阶段漏洞评估、上下文感知风险评估及自动化SDN缓解，显著提升异构网络中的漏洞管理效率与安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构网络面临设备多样性、脆弱运行环境及固件/服务配置差异等安全挑战；传统漏洞管理依赖静态扫描和严重性优先级，忽视利用可能性和资产上下文，导致缓解延迟且运维开销大。

Method: 该框架集成被动资产发现、自适应两阶段漏洞评估（针对不同设备能力定制评估策略）、上下文感知风险评估（结合CVSS严重性、EPSS利用概率及上下文属性），并通过OpenFlow和IDS策略实现自动化SDN缓解（从监控到主机隔离）。

Result: 实验表明，自适应两阶段评估将扫描时间减少最高91%，并在初始阶段识别出71%的基准漏洞；上下文感知风险模型减少约75%需立即缓解的漏洞，且未遗漏任何已验证被利用的漏洞；相比传统评估，该框架对32台物理主机评估时间减少最高45%，缓解在毫秒级内执行。

Conclusion: 该框架通过自适应评估、上下文感知优先级和自动化缓解，实现了高效可扩展的漏洞管理，有效应对异构网络的安全挑战。

Abstract: Heterogeneous networks pose significant security challenges due to device diversity, fragile operating conditions, and heterogeneous firmware and service configurations. Traditional vulnerability management often relies on static scanning and severity-based prioritization, overlooking exploitation likelihood and asset context. This can delay mitigation and increase operational overhead. This paper proposes a SOAR-orchestrated vulnerability management framework integrating passive asset discovery, adaptive two-stage vulnerability assessment, context-aware risk assessment, and automated SDN-based mitigation. The detection engine progressively characterizes device attack surfaces using assessment strategies tailored to device capabilities, minimizing disruption to resource-constrained IoT assets. Risk assessment combines CVSS severity, EPSS exploitation probability, and contextual attributes to prioritize vulnerabilities by operational risk. Based on risk bands, mitigation is automatically enforced through coordinated OpenFlow and IDS policies, ranging from monitoring and selective service isolation to complete host quarantine. Experimental results demonstrate the framework's effectiveness. Adaptive two-stage assessment reduces scan time by up to 91% while identifying 71% of baseline vulnerabilities during the initial stage before selectively triggering further analysis. The context-aware risk model reduces vulnerabilities requiring immediate mitigation by approximately 75% without missing any vulnerability with verified exploitation. Compared with conventional assessment, the framework reduces assessment time for 32 physical hosts by up to 45% and enforces mitigation within milliseconds, enabling efficient and scalable vulnerability management through adaptive assessment, context-aware prioritization, and automated mitigation.

</details>


### [231] [WirelessOpsAgent: A Benchmark and Agent Design for Action Assurance in Wireless Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08277)
*Zijian Lu,Yiping Zuo,Hao Xu,Weicong Chen,Xin He,Jiajia Guo,Shi Jin*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出WirelessOptBench基准测试和WirelessOpsAgent代理系统，用于无线网络操作中的行动安全保障。通过注入受控遥测故障来测试执行时的支持检查，WirelessOpsAgent能在执行前修复可恢复的支持故障，显著提升安全性（不安全执行率从82.2%降至10.3%）和准确性（最高0.983）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理正成为自主无线网络操作中的规划器，但存在安全隐患：在提出时正确的任务答案可能在执行时因遥测数据过时或不一致而变得不安全。现有基准主要从固定观测评估任务解决能力，未测试执行时的支持检查。

Method: 提出WirelessOptBench基准测试，将无线任务转换为执行状态决策 episodes，包含受控遥测故障和行动约束。开发WirelessOpsAgent系统，将候选行动锚定在当前证据上，并在执行前修复可恢复的支持故障。

Result: 在三个backbone评估中各进行600个episodes测试，WirelessOpsAgent达到最高0.983的精确行动准确率。在Claude Sonnet 4.6上，相比最安全的基线，不安全执行率从82.2%大幅降低至10.3%。

Conclusion: WirelessOptBench填补了无线操作领域执行时行动保障评估的空白，WirelessOpsAgent通过证据锚定和故障修复机制显著提升了无线网络操作的安全性和可靠性。基准已公开发布供社区使用。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are emerging as planners for autonomous wireless network operations. Yet a task answer that is correct at proposal time can still be unsafe at execution time if supporting telemetry is stale or inconsistent. Existing benchmarks mainly evaluate task solving from fixed observations and leave support checking at execution time untested. We introduce WirelessOptBench, a benchmark for action assurance in wireless operations. It turns wireless tasks into execution state decision episodes with controlled telemetry faults and action constraints. We further develop WirelessOpsAgent, which grounds candidate actions in current evidence and repairs recoverable support failures before execution. Across three backbone evaluations with 600 episodes each, WirelessOpsAgent achieves up to 0.983 Exact Action Accuracy. On Claude Sonnet 4.6, the Unsafe APPLY Rate decreases from 82.2% to 10.3% relative to the safest baseline. We make WirelessOptBench available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/wirelessopsbench-artifact-D969/.

</details>


### [232] [PSP: Low-Overhead Packet-Level Load Balancing for Stale-State and Bandwidth-Asymmetric Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08425)
*Jiaqi Liu,Chunyang Zhang,Heng Pan,Yanbiao Li*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 针对AI数据中心网络瓶颈，本文提出了一种名为PSP的数据包级负载均衡算法，通过频带离散状态表示降低硬件成本并实现性能与稳定性的最优平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型和生成式人工智能服务的快速发展，数据中心网络面临严重的微突发流量和高并发问题。传统的基于哈希的流级负载均衡无法感知链路状态，导致多路径Clos网络中出现哈希冲突、热点拥塞和尾部延迟。现有的基于数据包的方案受到过时状态信息、高硬件复杂度和对异构链路适应性差的限制。

Method: 提出了概率状态比例（PSP）调度，这是一种基于数据包的负载均衡算法。PSP采用基于频带（Band-based）的离散状态表示，用局部概率映射替代全局排序，在降低硬件复杂度的同时，抑制了由过时状态引起的羊群效应和振荡。

Result: 在循环精确模拟器上的实验表明，PSP在端口规模、带宽受限路径和固定流干扰方面具有鲁棒性。在丢包率、第99百分位缓冲区占用率和可扩展性方面，PSP优于最短队列加入（JSQ）调度和随机调度，并且在硬件成本更低的情况下与Top-k保持竞争力。

Conclusion: PSP为人工智能数据中心在性能、稳定性和开销之间提供了有效的平衡。

Abstract: With the rapid growth of large language model training and generative artificial intelligence services, data center networks face severe micro-burst traffic and high concurrency. Traditional hash-based flow-level load balancing cannot sense link states, leading to hash collisions, hotspot congestion, and tail latency in multipath Clos networks. Existing packet-level schemes are constrained by stale state information, high hardware complexity, and poor adaptation to heterogeneous links.
  To address these issues, this paper proposes probabilistic state-proportional (PSP) dispatching, a packet-level load balancing algorithm. Using a Band-based discrete state representation, PSP replaces global sorting with local probability mapping, reducing hardware complexity while suppressing herding and oscillations caused by stale states.
  Experiments on a cycle-accurate simulator show that PSP is robust across port scales, bandwidth-limited paths, and fixed-flow interference. It outperforms join-the-shortest-queue (JSQ) scheduling and Random in loss rate, 99th-percentile buffer occupancy, and scalability, while remaining competitive with Top-k at lower hardware cost. PSP provides an effective balance among performance, stability, and overhead for artificial intelligence data centers.

</details>


### [233] [Carrier Leakage Suppression and Power Difference Equalization for TFDMA Coherent PON](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08444)
*Ji Zhou,Haide Wang,Liangchuan Li,Changyuan Yu,Xiangjun Xin*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本研究基于半导体光放大器(SOA)实现了载流子泄漏抑制和功率差异均衡技术，并提出了非线性补偿算法来解决SOA引起的非线性失真问题，用于TFDMA架构的相干无源光网络(PON)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在TFDMA(时分频分多址)架构的相干PON系统中，SOA作为关键器件存在载流子泄漏和非线性失真问题，这些问题会严重影响系统性能和信号质量，需要进行有效的抑制和补偿。

Method: 采用半导体光放大器(SOA)作为核心技术，实现载流子泄漏抑制和功率差异均衡功能。同时设计并提出了一种专门针对SOA非线性失真的非线性补偿算法。

Result: 成功演示了基于SOA的载流子泄漏抑制和功率差异均衡技术的有效性，验证了所提出的非线性补偿算法能够处理SOA引入的非线性失真问题。

Conclusion: 本研究为TFDMA相干PON系统提供了有效的SOA性能优化方案，通过载流子泄漏抑制、功率差异均衡和非线性补偿算法的综合应用，显著改善了SOA在高速光通信系统中的性能表现。

Abstract: In this invited talk, we demonstrate the carrier leakage suppression and power difference equalization based on semiconductor optical amplifier (SOA) for TFDMA-based coherent PON, and propose a nonlinearity compensation algorithm to deal with SOA-induced nonlinear distortion.

</details>


### [234] [Active Hemispherical Metasurface Transmitarray Antenna for Wide-Angle 3D Beam Steering and Target Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08991)
*Somayeh Komeylian,Christopher Paolini*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 开发了一种工作在250 GHz的堆叠多层半球形石墨烯基透射阵列天线，用于实现宽角度3D波束扫描。该工作提出了首个完全自动化的CST Studio Suite宏来构建多层半球形几何结构并配置121个可独立调谐的石墨烯扇区。数值结果确认，通过整合电压控制的石墨烯扇区与FCC金贴片，实现了宽角度波束扫描，在θ方向上实现了-78°至78°的俯仰扫描范围和全360°方位覆盖，同时保持稳定的辐射特性和宽带阻抗匹配（|S11| < -10 dB），相对带宽为40%。天线在所有扫描方向上实现11.38-14.72 dBi的定向性和-6.8至-9.8 dB的旁瓣电平，并保持71%至80%的高效率，因缺陷导致5%的下降，使其成为移动目标跟踪应用的理想候选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了实现宽角度3D波束扫描，满足实际应用如移动目标跟踪的需求。

Method: 采用堆叠多层半球形石墨烯基透射阵列天线设计，工作频率为250 GHz。开发了首个完全自动化的CST Studio Suite宏来构建多层半球形几何结构并配置121个可独立调谐的石墨烯扇区。通过将电压控制的石墨烯扇区与面心立方（FCC）金贴片集成来实现波束扫描控制。

Result: 数值仿真结果验证了该天线在θ方向实现-78°至78°的俯仰扫描范围和全360°方位覆盖，同时保持稳定的辐射特性和宽带阻抗匹配（|S11| < -10 dB），相对带宽达40%。定向性范围为11.38-14.72 dBi，旁瓣电平范围为-6.8至-9.8 dB。天线效率保持在71%至80%，因缺陷导致5%的下降，但在全扫描范围内均能维持。

Conclusion: 该天线由于其宽扫描范围、稳定的辐射性能和较高的效率，成为移动目标跟踪等实际应用的理想候选方案。

Abstract: A stacked multilayer hemispherical graphene-based transmitarray antenna operating at 250 GHz is developed to achieve wide-angle 3D beam steering. This work presents the first fully automated CST Studio Suite macro for constructing the multilayer hemispherical geometry and configuring 121 independently tunable graphene sectors. Numerical results confirm that the integration of voltage-controlled graphene sectors with FCC gold patches enables wide-angle beam steering, achieving an elevation scanning range of -78° to 78° in θ and full 360° azimuthal coverage while maintaining stable radiation characteristics and broadband impedance matching (|S11| < -10 dB) with a relative bandwidth of 40%. The antenna achieves a directivity range of 11.38 - 14.72 dBi and an SLL range of -6.8 to -9.8 dB across all investigated steering directions. It also maintains high antenna efficiencies from 71% to 80% with a 5% degradation due to imperfections across the entire scanning range, making it a promising candidate in practical moving-target-tracking applications.

</details>


### [235] [SparsePilot: Belief-Guided Network Planning under Sparse Wireless Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09199)
*Xuanhao Luo,Jiayuan Huang,Longyu Zhou,Mingzhe Chen,Yuchen Liu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) have emerged as a promising solution for on-demand wireless coverage planning in urban environments. Existing learning-based UAV control methods, however, typically rely on continuous access to dense user-level received signal strength (RSS) measurements. Such full-observation assumptions are difficult to satisfy in real-world deployments due to the high cost and limited availability of dense wireless feedback. Sparse-feedback decision making under severe observation constraints therefore represents a fundamental challenge. To fill this gap, we propose SparsePilot, a measurement-efficient sensing-control framework that couples active wireless probing with belief-guided network control. SparsePilot formulates spatial probing as a multi-armed bandit problem over grid cells, uses upper confidence bound probing to select informative regions, and aggregates sparse RSS measurements into a coverage belief map. A deep reinforcement learning controller then uses this belief state to generate continuous UAV mobility actions, while the full wireless state remains hidden from the policy. We further provide a theoretical analysis connecting sparse probing, belief estimation error, and the sparse-feedback performance gap. Experiments across seven urban digital twins show that SparsePilot achieves superior coverage restoration performance while using only about 3.1% of the full-observation measurement budget and demonstrates strong cross-scene generalization to unseen urban-scale wireless environments.

</details>


### [236] [Automated Synthesis of Deterministic Cross-Domain Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09312)
*Konstantinos Christodoulopoulos,Antonis Selentis-Boulntadakis*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本研究提出一个基于LLM的自动化框架，用于合成确定性网络中异构领域间的动态和静态合同。框架通过两个LLM模块协同工作：代理搜索配置机制，处理器构建网络模型并调用形式化工具验证。在5G前传和5G-TSN桥接场景的验证表明，该框架能生成精度达~1.2μs的动态合同，满足100μs截止时间，效率比静态方案高3.5倍。关键发现是LLM擅长模型构建但数值计算不可靠，因此采用LLM与形式化工具的分工模式，既保证灵活性又确保可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定性网络跨越异构领域，在每个边界上两个领域必须就假设-保证合同达成一致，规定客户端可注入的流量和承运商将保证的QoS。虽然将此类合同组合为端到端保证已标准化，但推导每个领域的合同尚未标准化。目前这些合同是手工制作的、静态的且过度配置的，当流量变化需要动态合同时难度进一步上升。亟需一种自动化方法来生成精确且可靠的领域合同。

Method: 研究提出一个自动化合同综合框架，包含两个由大语言模型驱动的推理模块：(1)代理模块接收客户端流量声明并搜索承运商配置机制；(2)处理器模块根据子网的类型化披露构建网络模型。处理器调用网络微积分内核处理模型的硬性条款，并借助独立的预言机（高保真模拟器、测试床或实时网络）验证每个候选合同，同时发现缺乏先验代数形式的要素（如重新配置的安全性和应用时机）。研究在两个不同子网技术上验证了框架：5G前传上行链路（动态合同）和5G-TSN桥接（静态合同）。

Result: 研究在6G前传上行链路TDM-PON上综合了动态合同，基于包级模拟器进行了接地验证。来自两个LLM家族的六次综合均产生了可行、已验证的合同，精度达到~1.2μs，满足反应式调度无法达到的100μs截止时间，带宽效率比静态过度配置高3.5倍。当直接要求LLM计算最坏情况延迟时，六次尝试中有五次结果不健全，这证明了分工的必要性：LLM构建模型，形式化工具负责数值权威性。同一框架未作修改即在第二子网上综合了5G-TSN桥接的静态合同，验证了框架的可复用性。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了在确定性网络领域，LLM与形式化工具的协同分工是实现自动合同综合的最佳路径。LLM负责构建模型和搜索配置机制，形式化工具负责数值权威性和验证。这种分工既发挥了LLM的灵活性和泛化能力，又保证了结果的可靠性和精度。研究框架具有良好的可复用性，能在不同子网技术上应用，为未来异构网络间的动态QoS保障提供了自动化解决方案。

Abstract: Deterministic networking spans heterogeneous domains. At each boundary, two domains must agree on an assume--guarantee contract: what traffic the client may inject, and the QoS the carrier will hold for it. Composing such contracts into an end-to-end guarantee is standardized, but deriving each domain's contract is not. Today they are hand-crafted, static, and over-provisioned. The difficulty rises when traffic changes and the contract must become dynamic. We present a framework that automatically synthesizes the per-domain contract for both static and dynamic classes, along with the registration the dynamic one rests on. Two reasoning modules implemented with large language models (LLMs) drive it: an agent takes the client's traffic declaration and searches the carrier's configuration mechanisms, and a handler builds the network model from a typed disclosure of the substrate. The handler calls a network-calculus kernel for the model's hard terms, and an independent oracle---a faithful simulator, testbed, or live network---which verifies each candidate and discovers what lacks an a-priori algebraic form: when a reconfiguration is safe, and the instant to apply it. We synthesized a dynamic contract for uplink 5G fronthaul over a TDM-PON, grounded against a packet-level simulator. Across six draws from two LLM families, every synthesis produced a feasible, verified contract tight to ${\sim}1.2\,μ$s, holding a $100$-$μ$s deadline that reactive scheduling cannot meet, at up to $3.5$ times the bandwidth efficiency of static over-provisioning. Tasked instead with computing the worst-case delay directly, the LLMs were unsound in five of six attempts---evidence for the division of labor: LLMs construct the model, formal tools hold numeric authority. The same framework, unchanged, synthesized a static 5G--TSN bridge contract on a second substrate.

</details>


### [237] [Enabling Beyond-Visual-Line-of-Sight Drones Operation over Open RAN 5G Networks with Slicing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09481)
*Pau Baguer,Esteban Municio,Gines Garcia-Aviles,Xavier Costa-Pérez*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 针对5G向6G演进中BVLoS无人机操作可靠性不足的问题，本文部署5G O-RAN开源系统并利用切片技术进行优化，成功降低了轨迹误差和延迟，并探讨了该技术的前景与挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在5G向6G演进的过程中，超视距（BVLoS）无人机操作被视为物联网机器人的关键使能技术，但对其性能、可靠性和监管框架提出了严格要求。尽管现有5G技术在吞吐量和延迟方面能够满足需求，但在实践中如何实现完全可靠性仍缺乏研究。

Method: 本研究部署了一个先进的5G O-RAN开源BVLoS操作系统，报告了当前的局限性，并利用O-RAN原生的高级能力——网络切片来解决这些问题。

Result: 提出的部署方案有效减少了因5G链路拥塞引起的轨迹误差，并将延迟控制在3GPP为BVLoS操作设定的限制范围之内。

Conclusion: 最后，讨论了未来的挑战以及5G O-RAN赋能的网络为BVLoS无人机操作带来的机遇。

Abstract: Among the foretold claims of the transition from 5G to 6G, Beyond-Visual-Line-of-Sight (BVLoS) drone operation has emerged as a prominent Internet-of-Robots enabler. However, safety concerns have been raised since BVLoS imposes strict requirements on performance and dependability on the technology, and requires robust regulatory frameworks. While current 5G technologies promise to meet the performance requirements in terms of throughput and latency, there is a lack of studies regarding how to achieve full reliability in practice. To address this challenge, the research community is actively working on open-source projects that allow for experimental validation in the field. Fortunately, new Open RAN (O-RAN) standards are paving the way for such approaches in an integrated, native manner. In this work, we deploy a state-of-the-art 5G O-RAN open-source BVLoS operational system, report current limitations, and address them via advanced capabilities natively available in O-RAN: Slicing. Our proposed deployment minimizes trajectory errors due to 5G link congestion and keeps latency well below the 3GPP limits defined for BVLoS operation. Finally, we discuss on the challenges ahead and the opportunities that 5G O-RAN-enabled networks may bring to BVLoS drone operation.

</details>


### [238] [A Semantic Communication Approach to Fiducial Marker Processing in 5G-Enabled Edge SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09620)
*Boris Radovanovic,Vukan Ninkovic,Katarina Vidojevic,Buda Bajic Papuga,Dejan Vukobratovic*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种语义分割推理框架，将DeepTag启发的CNN在机器人和边缘服务器之间进行分割，用于5G边缘SLAM中的基准标记检测，实现了准确的 keypoints 估计并量化了不同分割点的通信-计算权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主机器人越来越多地依赖边缘计算来卸载计算密集型感知任务，同时通过5G网络保持实时运行。然而，传统的基准标记检测流水线在高效任务分割方面机会有限，使其不适用于通信感知的边缘部署。

Method: 提出了一种用于5G边缘SLAM中基准标记处理的语义分割推理框架。采用DeepTag启发的卷积神经网络在机器人和边缘服务器之间进行分割，中间特征表示作为面向任务的语义信息通过无线链路传输。该框架集成到基于ROS2的机器人架构中，并在真实的5G通信测试平台上进行了表征。

Result: 实验结果表明：实现了准确的keypoints估计，展示了以下游姿态估计的影响，并量化了不同分割点相关的通信-计算权衡，为连接机器人系统中深度视觉感知的通信感知部署提供了实际洞察。

Conclusion: 该研究为连接机器人系统中深度视觉感知的通信感知部署提供了实用的指导见解，展示了语义分割推理框架在5G边缘SLAM应用中的可行性和优势。

Abstract: Autonomous robots increasingly rely on edge computing to offload computationally intensive perception tasks while maintaining real-time operation over 5G networks. However, conventional fiducial marker detection pipelines provide limited opportunities for efficient task partitioning, making them poorly suited for communication-aware edge deployment. This paper proposes a semantic split inference framework for fiducial marker processing in 5G-enabled Edge SLAM. A DeepTag-inspired convolutional neural network is partitioned between the robot and the edge server, where intermediate feature representations serve as task-oriented semantic information transmitted over the wireless link. The framework is integrated into a ROS2-based robotic architecture and characterized over a real 5G communication testbed. Experimental results demonstrate accurate keypoint estimation, illustrate the impact on downstream pose estimation, and quantify the communication--computation trade-offs associated with different split points, providing practical insights for communication-aware deployment of deep visual perception in connected robotic systems.

</details>


### [239] [Abstractions for Network Intelligence: A Reference Architecture for AI at the Wireless Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09640)
*Salil Reddy,Haohuang Wen,Ness Shroff,Venki Ramaswamy,Zhiqiang Lin,Elisa Bertino,Jim Kurose,Anish Arora*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为AI-EDGE的参考架构，旨在通过信息腰（information waist）促进智能网络与智能AI应用之间的协同交互，解决现有互联网架构在意识共享方面的不足，并通过基于O-RAN和MEC的组件级设计及用例展示了其在提升网络利用率和应用性能方面的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着网络日益采用AI技术，同时AI应用也愈发依赖网络，两者之间的意识共享（awareness sharing）对于解锁更高的网络利用率和应用性能至关重要。然而，当前的互联网架构对此支持不足，限制了这种协同效应的发挥。

Method: 本文描述了一个抽象的参考架构，即AI-EDGE架构，该架构通过一个“信息腰”实现智能应用与智能网络之间的协同交互，并支持新兴智能平面中的网络智能服务。作者讨论了该架构的设计原理、核心功能需求和核心抽象概念，并基于O-RAN蜂窝网络（用于无线网络）和3GPP Edge App及ETSI MEC（用于边缘计算）提出了组件级参考设计，以支持现有平台上的实验和开发。

Result: 通过提供来自不同类型用户视角的代表性用例，展示了该架构在实际应用中的优势。具体结果包括：促进了网络与AI应用间的意识共享，增强了系统的可移植性，简化了原型设计过程，并为架构的有效性提供了验证。

Conclusion: AI-EDGE架构为智能应用与智能网络之间的深度协同提供了一个可行的参考框架。通过在现有的无线网络（O-RAN）和边缘计算（MEC）平台上的组件级设计，该架构不仅填补了现有互联网架构在意识共享方面的空白，还通过具体的用例证明了其在提高资源利用效率、增强系统灵活性以及加速新技术原型开发和验证方面的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Networks are increasingly adopting AI as are AI applications leveraging networks. Awareness sharing between networks and AI applications promises to unlock higher levels of network utilization and application performance, but is inadequately supported in the current architecture of the Internet. In this paper, we describe a reference architecture that abstractly enables the synergistic interaction of intelligent applications and the intelligent network, via an information waist, and also supports the network intelligence services in the emerging intelligence plane in networks. We discuss the rationale for our AI-EDGE architecture, its functional requirements, and the core abstractions. We present a reference component-level design of the core abstractions to support experimentation and development on existing platforms for wireless networking (i.e., based on O-RAN cellular networks) and edge computing (i.e., based on 3GPP Edge App and ETSI MEC). Moreover, we provide representative use cases from the perspective of different types of users that demonstrate the benefits of the architecture in contexts of awareness sharing, portability, prototyping, and validation.

</details>
